# Enconvo 2.5.16 Changelog (2026-11-15)

Thanks for all the feedback during the beta. This release brings your agent into your calls, in Enconvo Conference and in Google Meet and Zoom, gives every call a meeting page in Recordings, lets Live Photo characters go live on a stream with a voice of their own, keeps a reply going on a backup model when its model can't be used, picks up work after Enconvo restarts, shows the agent's browser and app work in a Picture-in-Picture window, and puts the calls your agent answers in their own Calls section. Update the iPhone app to 1.2.13 and the Android app to 1.4.19 to try the phone changes.

## Highlights

- **Agents in your calls**: bring an agent into an Enconvo Conference, or send it into a Google Meet or Zoom meeting, where it answers in the chat or out loud and asks you before doing any work.
- **A meeting page for every call**: Recordings shows each call's summary, action items with owners, what agents made and the transcript, and you can ask questions about any meeting.
- **Live Photo characters go live**: put her on an OBS stream in one click, let her answer your Twitch, Kick or YouTube live chat, give her a cloned or ElevenLabs voice, and pick her background.
- **Model fallback**: pick up to three backup models in Settings › AI Model, and a reply carries on with the next one when its model hits a limit, loses its key or can't be reached.
- **Work picks up after a restart**: a chat stopped by an update, a quit or a crash goes on by itself once Enconvo is back.
- **Picture in Picture for Browser Use and Computer Use**: watch the agent work in a tab or window you can't see, and click to take over.
- **Calls the agent answers**: a phone notification for each one, a Calls section on Mac, iPhone and Android, and a caller can never make your agent act.
- **One-click website publishing**: put a page online at your own name.site.enconvo.com link.
- **Tools & Skills for each Agent**: let an agent find what it needs, or give it a fixed set.
- **DeepSeek, Parallel and Ask AI**: DeepSeek for speech, transcription and OCR, Parallel for web search, and Ask AI in the Settings search.
- **Remote Screen in Picture in Picture**: keep your computer's screen floating over your chats on iPhone and Android.

## Agents in Calls

- Added agents to Enconvo Conference: bring one in from People and it joins as a visible participant, switched off until someone turns it on or @-mentions it, then answers and does work with your tools.
- Added a choice of how a conference agent answers, written in the chat by default or out loud, and of what it does when a guest joins: leave, stay quiet until they leave, or carry on.
- Added Always allow on a conference agent's approval card, so someone you trust can ask it for work for the rest of the call without asking you each time.
- Added conference agents to the iPhone app: bring one in from People with the same choices as on the web, and answer its approval cards with Always allow, Just this once or Not now.
- Added the Stage to Enconvo Conference: a doc or whiteboard a conference agent makes during the call is shared with everyone signed in on it and opens beside the call, so the whole call edits it together while talking.
- Added Bring my agent to my conferences in Settings › Video Conference, and a Bring switch on each conference under Coming up, so your agent joins the conferences you choose, including Enconvo conference links in your Mac's Calendar events except ones you declined.
- Added sending your agent into a Google Meet or Zoom meeting, from a chat or the Bring switch on a calendar event, where it joins as `<agent> (Enconvo)` with camera and microphone off, reads the captions and chat, answers your requests in the meeting's chat, asks you on your Mac or iPhone before doing any work, and files the meeting's notes in Recordings.

## Meetings in Recordings

- Added a meeting page to each Enconvo Conference call in Recordings, with the summary, action items with who took them, what agents made on the call, what was on screen, and the transcript.
- Added Add to Reminders to a call's action items, which puts the item in Apple Reminders with its owner and due date.
- Added Ask this meeting to a call's page, and let your agent answer questions about your earlier calls, saying which meeting each answer came from.
- Changed a call's notes to list each action item's owner and due date, the docs and whiteboards agents made on the call, and what agents saw on a shared screen.
- Fixed calls in the same conference room being merged into one Recordings entry, which could lose the first call's recording or mix in the last time's notes, voices or summary; each call now keeps its own entry, notes and Ask AI answers, and a repeating conference keeps every time's entry.
- Fixed call notes sometimes ending their summary with a sentence saying the transcript was not cut.

## Live Photo Characters

- Added Go live to Live Photo characters in Settings › Avatar, with a stream panel that shows her stage, captions, orientation and OBS's stream state, and a transparent stage page and captions page for an OBS Browser source.
- Added one-click Add to OBS, which connects to OBS's WebSocket server, keeps its password with your other credentials, and creates an `Enconvo · <name>` scene with her stage and captions sized for the stream.
- Added live stream chat for Live Photo characters: she reads a Twitch, Kick or YouTube live chat and answers aloud, through her Agent, the viewers who mention her, ask with !ask, or pay, with per-viewer and overall cooldowns, blocked words and an optional approve-first queue.
- Added live mode to the stream panel: start her live chat there and she says each answer on her OBS stage with her lips moving, out of a call.
- Added a Voice step to Live Photo characters: clone a voice by recording in place or uploading the person's audio or video, with ElevenLabs on Enconvo Cloud or your own API key, pick an ElevenLabs voice, or follow the call voice; Change voice on her card changes it later.
- Added a Background setting to the Live page: choose Transparent, a color (green screen included), your own image, a blurred copy of her photo, or her original photo, and OBS gets exactly what the stage preview shows.
- Added using your own Atmee account for Live Photo characters: with your API key, new characters are made in your account without the daily minutes or the three-character limit, and the avatars already there can be taken in.
- Changed Live Photo calls so her voice plays only from OBS while a stage shows her, unless Also play on this Mac is on.
- Fixed a Live Photo character's face freezing for a moment in the middle of a long answer while her voice went on.
- Fixed recording a voice sample always ending with "The recording was not saved.", and the Live page showing no stage preview in Portrait 9:16.

## Chats and Agents

- Added Fallback in Settings › AI Model: pick up to three backup models, and when the model a chat uses reaches its usage limit, loses its sign-in or key, or can't be reached, the reply continues on the next backup, the chat says which model took over and why, and the original model is used again once it works.
- Changed the Fallback panel to show the chain as it works: The model in use, then your numbered backups with Ready or Paused, and a click on a backup's model name switches it to another model of that provider.
- Added picking up work after Enconvo restarts: when an update, a quit or a crash stops a chat in the middle of a task, the chat goes on by itself once Enconvo is back, checks what it already did before doing the rest, and brings back the subagents it had running.
- Added a Tools & Skills section to each Agent's settings: keep Auto-discover, or switch to Only what I choose to give the agent a fixed set; you can also turn off individual built-in tools, pin skills so they are always listed, and stop suggestions from the Enconvo Store. Create New Agent gets Everyday, Coding, Research and Writing presets.
- Added one-click website publishing: a page artifact's Publish button puts it online at your own name.site.enconvo.com link on Enconvo Cloud, with an optional password, and agents can publish a page or a site folder for you when you ask them to share it.
- Added subagent deliverables to the main chat: what a subagent makes, such as a design or a doc, shows under its row as it is made, opens beside the chat and is handed over as a card in the answer.
- Added a Wait For tool for agents: an agent can wait for a file, a line in a log, a process, or a port or address in one step, with no shell running, and is told when the wait is met, fails or times out.
- Changed sending a message while an agent works: the reply it is writing and the tools it is running keep going while Enconvo checks whether your message should stop them, so a follow-up that can wait no longer throws away the step.
- Changed Projects so Claude Code, Codex, Grok Build or Antigravity can lead a Project with members, plan their work in a Work plan and read their results, and work like any other Project.
- Added picking fast mode and ultracode in a new Claude Code chat before its first message.
- Fixed a sign-in, install or question card blocking the chat when you sent a message instead of answering it.
- Fixed agents getting stuck resending a broken tool call: the agent now gets the tool's parameters after the second failure, a note to change approach after the third, and the tool is paused after the fifth.
- Fixed an agent waiting for long work leaving a sleeping shell behind for every check, which filled the chat's background tasks with "running" entries.
- Fixed a new Work plan starting on its own while an earlier plan from the same chat was still running.
- Fixed Claude Code models, effort levels and fast mode taking around ten seconds to appear in a new chat, and Claude chats retrying at every step after an earlier reply's reasoning was refused.
- Fixed agents failing to start Higgsfield video runs in a mode that needs reference media, seeing a connected app's tool settings as an unknown type, and an empty card showing under a step when such a tool turned down the request.
- Fixed code and monospace blocks in an agent's cards showing dark text on a dark panel in the dark theme, and light text on a white block.
- Fixed agent shell commands having every "pip" changed to "uv pip" in a folder whose Python environment has no pip of its own, and Full access asking before deleting a folder whose name is built from a command's output or a command's own scratch folder.

## Picture in Picture

- Added a Picture-in-Picture window for Browser Use and Computer Use on Mac: while the agent works in a tab or app window you can't see, a live view shows in the corner of the screen, turns amber with what the agent needs when it asks for you, and takes you there when you click it so you can take over, then Return to agent hands it back; choose Always, Only When I Am Needed or Off in their settings.
- Added stacked cards when several tasks wait, with Show all saying how many there are, and live views that refresh about three times a second for tabs and stream smoothly for app windows.
- Fixed dragging the Picture-in-Picture window making it flicker and jump, and pages with another extension's widget staying black instead of saying they have no live preview.

## Calls

- Added a phone notification for every call the agent answers for you with Answer my calls: who called, what they want in one sentence and whether it's urgent, breaking through Focus when it is, with Call back and Open.
- Added a Calls section on Mac, iPhone and Android that keeps the calls the agent answered apart from your other chats, marks the ones that are urgent, want a call back or are still unread, shows when each call rang and the caller's number or contact name, and lets you call back or mark a call done.
- Changed Answer my calls so a caller can't make your agent act: the summary is written without tools, a call's chat asks before anything that sends, pays, deletes or runs commands even with Full access, and Settings › Agent Phone shows what a caller can reach.
- Added Ask for a passphrase for Numbers that can give tasks, and a Likely scam badge and warning on calls that look like scams.

## Chat Window and Dynamic Island

- Changed the Chat Window's Active chats list to show only chats that still need you, are running or have an unopened result, with an Archive button on hover.
- Changed New chat in the Chat Window to start in the project you last picked under the message box; pick No project to go back to plain chats.
- Added folding to the Chat Window sidebar's Pinned group, and Worktree rows now fold and unfold like the main branch row.
- Fixed Subagent and team member chats showing as rows of their own in the sidebar while they run, and a parallel chat or an archived chat that runs again only showing once it finished.
- Fixed Esc in the Chat Window so it stops a reply only when pressed in that chat.
- Fixed picking another computer in the Chat Window looking frozen while its page loads.
- Fixed the row of Agent cards on the new chat start page being out of reach with a mouse that has only a vertical wheel.
- Fixed the Dynamic Island's Home showing one agent's icon on the Default Agent button and another agent's model beside it; the empty Home now always runs the Default Agent the button shows.
- Fixed the Dynamic Island sometimes staying hidden after you left a full screen app until Enconvo was relaunched.
- Fixed Enconvo sometimes quitting unexpectedly when a window that shows its Dock icon opened, such as a video conference.
- Fixed screenshots dragged from their floating thumbnail not being added in Friend chats.

## Remote Screen and Pairing

- Changed pairing with another computer so the computer being paired opens its Dynamic Island on the request, with the number to compare and Allow.
- Fixed Return on another computer's screen pasting this Mac's clipboard, dragging letting go of the mouse button at once, and the cursor lagging behind your pointer while you are in control.
- Fixed the top edge of another computer's screen being out of reach in a small viewer window, and being unable to leave full screen while controlling it: ⌃⌘F now goes in and out.

## iPhone and Android

- Added Picture in Picture to Remote Screen on iPhone and Android: tap the button at the top left, or pinch in, to keep your computer's screen floating over your chats on the same session, and over other apps when you leave Enconvo.
- Changed the iPhone and Android apps to call the paired computer "computer" instead of "Mac", and Mac Screen "Remote Screen".
- Changed the Android composer to a single row with a camera button, quick chips for the computer, project, Compare answers and Skills, a plus panel where the keyboard was with recent photos, and Send standing alone once you type.
- Added a choice in Settings › Avatar to show the composer's call button as the avatar or the voice bars icon.
- Changed the Active chats card on Home to have a grabber that opens the full list when you swipe it up, and added folding to the Recent group in the sidebar.
- Fixed the phone chat so a message you send into an existing chat shows above its reply, never shows another chat's messages after reloading, and doesn't lose the previous reply.
- Fixed Wrap Code on a phone chat's code block springing back off right after you tapped it, and Home on Android shaking when the Active chats panel opens.
- Fixed typing on a computer's screen from Android with keyboards that compose a whole word, such as Samsung Keyboard and SwiftKey.

## Models and Providers

- Added DeepSeek as a Text to Speech provider, a speech-to-text provider for Transcribe and Dictation, and an OCR provider, with your own DeepSeek API key or through Enconvo Cloud.
- Added Parallel as a Web Search provider: add your Parallel API key in Settings › Credentials › Parallel and pick Turbo, Fast, Basic or Advanced as its Search Mode.
- Added Cloud prices next to models and providers in Settings, and plan benefit notes such as your free minutes left this month.
- Changed Enconvo Cloud Fast speech recognition to be free for everyone.

## Settings and Account

- Added Ask AI to the Settings search: ask a question in the search field, press Return, and the answer opens beside the sidebar with cards that take you to each setting and turn a switch on or off in place.
- Added an Email and Password section to Settings › Account on Mac, iPhone and Android, so an account made with Apple's Hide My Email can add its own email and set or reset a password, and Continue with Apple on Enconvo Feedback.
- Added a list of your free limit resets to a Claude or ChatGPT subscription card when you have more than one, to use the one you pick.
- Changed Mail Alerts to be off until you turn it on in Automations › Important mail; Mail Alerts you turned on or set up keep working.
- Changed Invites so invite codes can no longer be used from temporary or agent email addresses.
- Fixed the Authentication cards on a provider's settings page overlapping their text when the Settings window is narrow.
- Added Telegram to the calls Dictation mutes you in: in a call in the Telegram app for Mac, you're muted while you dictate and unmuted when you finish or pause.
- Fixed Bluetooth headphones such as AirPods blasting louder than your volume for a moment after dictation or Voice Command ends.

# Enconvo 2.5.15 Changelog (2026-11-13)

Thanks for all the feedback during the beta. This release adds Ukrainian and Russian to the interface and sixty languages to meeting captions, puts a calculator in the Dynamic Island and weather, flight and place lookups in the Smart Bar, lets agents open parallel chats and move a chat into a worktree, makes Full access really mean full access for Claude Code and Codex, adds Gemini 4 Hydrogen, and lets you pair a phone with its own camera. Update the iPhone app to 1.2.13 and the Android app to 1.4.13 to try the phone changes.

## Highlights

- **Ukrainian and Russian**: two new interface languages on the Mac, iPhone and Android, and every language the speech engine supports in meeting captions and translation.
- **Calculator in the Dynamic Island**: type a sum, a conversion or a time on Home and the answer appears under the island.
- **Weather, flights and places in the Smart Bar**: ask "weather in London", a flight number or "time in Chernivtsi".
- **Parallel chats**: an agent can open a new chat for a separate task that shows in your chat list like one you started.
- **Chats move into worktrees**: move a chat into a new or existing worktree with its files, and merging brings it back.
- **Full access means full access**: new Claude Code and Codex chats start without approval prompts when Enconvo is at Full access.
- **Gemini 4 Hydrogen**: Google's fast new model for everyday coding and agent work.
- **Pair with your phone's camera**: point the iPhone Camera, an Android camera or Google Lens at the pairing code and Enconvo opens to confirm.
- **Send to on your phone**: something shared into Enconvo from another app can go to a new chat or any recent chat.

## Languages

- Added Ukrainian and Russian as interface languages on the Mac, iPhone and Android apps, in Settings › General › Language. Requested by Vit_MK.
- Added every language the speech engine supports to meeting captions and translation, Ukrainian included, sixty in all and listed by name.

## Smart Bar and Dynamic Island

- Added the calculator to the Dynamic Island: type a sum, a conversion or a time on Home and the answer appears in a card under the island; press Return to copy it, or Command-Return to ask your agent instead. Turn it off, or turn on Weather and Flights, in Settings › Dynamic Island › Home.
- Added weather, flight and place lookups to the Smart Bar calculator, off by default: turn on Weather and Flights in Settings › Smart Bar, then type "weather in London", "Tokyo feels like", a flight number like "BA008" or "time in Chernivtsi".
- Added splitting a total to the Smart Bar calculator, as in "25% off $60 split by 3".
- Added Calculator switches to Settings › Smart Bar, to turn the calculator off or its online lookups on.
- Fixed the Smart Bar calculator not knowing the time in Kyiv, Lviv, Odesa, Kolkata, Kathmandu, Doha and other cities.
- Fixed the Chat Window becoming unclickable and your typing landing in the Dynamic Island while the island stayed open and an agent was working in the background.
- Fixed the Dynamic Island cutting off a message at an `@` when you press Return, so an email address and the text after it are sent whole.

## Chats and Agents

- Added parallel chats: when you ask an agent to open a new chat for a separate task, it opens one that shows in your chat list on the Mac, iPhone and Android like a chat you started, with a note at the top saying which chat started it, while reviews and tests for that task still run as its subagents; a chat an agent started can also be moved to your chat list from its card.
- Added moving a chat between the main checkout and a worktree on the Mac, iPhone and Android: from the chip after its title or the chat menu on the phone, or by asking its agent, it can move into a new or existing worktree with the files it changed, and merging the worktree brings the chat back to the main checkout to go on, with a line in the conversation at each move.
- Added a violet spinner to chats in the sidebar on the Mac, iPhone and Android whose reply has finished while subagents or background tasks they started are still running, with a blue dot inside it when there is a new reply to read.
- Added the model picker to chats on a paired computer: pick the model, reasoning effort and speed for an agent there, and see that provider's subscription limits, the same as in your own Chat Window.
- Fixed the background tray over the message box showing new subagents only after the agent finished writing their tasks, which could take a minute; they now show as soon as the chat's subagent card does, on the Mac and the phone.
- Fixed an Agent set to Auto answering a question about a problem in your own app, project or setup without its tools (and saying it had none): such questions, and every question in a Project chat about its work, now run as Agent, so it looks into your code, logs and data.

## Permissions

- Fixed Claude Code and Codex chats still asking for approval at Full access: with Enconvo's Permissions at Full access, new Claude Code and Codex chats started on this Mac begin in Bypass permissions and Full access (where Claude Code's settings allow Bypass), a Codex chat at Full access approves its subagents' commands, edits and permission requests and trusts new hooks without asking, and Claude Code at Bypass permissions no longer asks about protected folders or its subagents' calls.
- Fixed a Project's lead chat keeping the Permission mode its turn started in: switching the Project to Full access now takes effect on the next command of a run already going.
- Changed Approve for me to stop asking before commands that only set PATH for themselves, or that delete what they made under the temporary folder (such as a temporary git worktree), while deleting anything else still asks.
- Changed approval cards to say which setting made them ask, such as "Asked because this Project is on Approve for me."
- Fixed agents and subagents in Full access being asked to approve a sync or delete into a folder the same command had just set to a fixed path, as if it could delete the whole disk.
- Fixed Full access letting a few deletes that empty the working folder or the home folder run without asking, such as a ?* or ** glob, a folder reached through a link, or rsync --delete into a folder named by a variable that may be empty.

## Models

- Added Gemini 4 Hydrogen, Google's fast successor to Gemini 3.8 Flash for everyday coding and agent work: choose it in Enconvo Cloud, with your own Gemini API key (the free tier works too), or in Google Antigravity when your plan offers it. Its thinking levels are Low, Medium, High (default) and X-High, which thinks longest on hard multi-step tasks, also on iPhone and Android.

## Recordings and Dictation

- Added an adjustable width for the Summary panel in Recordings: drag its left edge to widen it for long summaries, double-click the edge to go back to the default, and the width is kept for every recording. Requested by plkv_andrii.
- Fixed text in the Summary, Action items and Notes tabs of Recordings not being selectable: drag over any part of it to copy it with Command-C, the right-click menu or the selection toolbar.
- Fixed Dictation's meeting mute for Google Meet in other languages: the microphone is now muted in Meet in any of its languages, Russian and Ukrainian included, and when Meet's button can't be read you're told once to mute yourself there. Reported by plkv_andrii.
- Fixed the app freezing, with every Settings page stuck, while a long audio file dragged into Transcription is being converted for transcription.
- Fixed confirm dialogs, such as deleting a recording with a long file name, spilling the title and buttons out of the dialog; long names now wrap inside it.

## iPhone and Android

- Added pairing with your phone's own camera: point the iPhone Camera, an Android camera or Google Lens at the pairing code in Settings › Connections and Enconvo opens and asks you to confirm the Mac; without the app, the link helps you install it and then pair, and scanning from the Enconvo app works as before.
- Added a Send to sheet on iPhone and Android: content shared into Enconvo from another app can go to a new chat or to any recent chat, with an Always share to new chat switch in the sheet and in Settings.
- Added your subscription limits to the model sheet on iPhone and Android when you chat with your Mac: the plan, the 5-hour and weekly limits with what is left and when they reset, and a model's own limit on its row.
- Fixed messages sent from the iPhone and Android apps into a Mac chat, with photos or files too, showing only after a delay; they now show the moment you send and steer a reply that is still running, as Return does on the Mac.
- Fixed the first message of a new Mac chat started on iPhone or Android sometimes getting lost, leaving the chat with its title and nothing in it: the phone now checks the Mac received it and sends it again if it didn't.
- Changed the phone apps' Home on a Mac: your active chats show right above the composer, All opens a panel of every active chat grouped by what needs you, what is running and what has results, a Screen button in the header opens Mac Screen, and the project name no longer gets cut off.
- Changed tapping a subagent in a Mac chat on the iPhone and Android apps to open its steps in a sheet over the chat that a swipe down closes, instead of leaving the chat for the subagent's own thread.
- Added a fold to the Pinned group in the iPhone and Android apps' sidebars, and tapping anywhere left of the Friends and Spaces buttons now folds or opens those groups.
- Changed the phone apps' Speed and reasoning sheet to list the reasoning efforts above the speeds.
- Changed answering an Agent's call on Android, from the ring notification or the lock screen, to connect the call in the background without unlocking or opening the app, like a phone call, with an ongoing call notification that ends the call or opens its chat.
- Fixed charts in chat flashing and redrawing while a reply is still being written, most visibly on iPhone and Android.
- Fixed stat tiles cutting off or spilling a long value: the value now shrinks to fit its tile and wraps when it still doesn't, and a row of stats wraps to fewer columns on a narrow screen.
- Fixed the suggested next message over the message box in a project's lead chat staying on screen for a moment after sending.

# Enconvo 2.5.14 Changelog (2026-11-12)

Thanks for all the feedback during the beta. This release brings recording summaries with templates in the language you choose, recommends a model for each kind of work, adds Temporary chat for one-off questions and an Active group that gathers the chats that need you, lets you add to a task while it runs, and makes Dictation and Live Captioning far more flexible: press Return to dictate and send, pause, mute your meeting, and put the captions where you want them. Update the iPhone app to 1.2.13 and the Android app to 1.4.10 to try the phone changes.

## Highlights

- **Recording summaries your way**: pick the language summaries are written in, and choose Meeting summary, Lecture notes, Interview highlights or a template you write yourself, with timelines, tables and checklists.
- **Model recommendations**: model pickers and New Agent suggest the best fit, the lowest-cost and the best-quality model for the job, with prices.
- **Temporary chat**: a chat for one-off questions that stays out of your history and is deleted when you leave it.
- **Active chats on top**: the Chat Window, iPhone and Android sidebars gather the chats that are running, waiting for you, failed or have a new result.
- **Add to a running task**: type while the agent works and Send adds your message to the task; an unrelated request goes to a subagent in the background.
- **Dictate and send**: press Return to stop, insert and send in one go, or turn on Send after inserting; pause and resume with a key, and Dictation mutes you in Zoom, Teams and Google Meet while you talk.
- **Live Captioning where you want it**: left, right, top or bottom of any display, a hotkey to start and stop, and a caption area you can resize.
- **Generated agent avatars**: draw four avatars from an agent's name and description in the style you pick.
- **Tutorials in Settings**: update videos and walkthroughs play inside Enconvo, with the new ones marked.
- **Wrap Code**: long lines in code blocks can wrap instead of scrolling sideways.
- **Phones for everyone**: a free account can pair the iPhone and Android apps with its Mac, and reading a chat on one device clears its notification on the others.

## Recordings

- Added an output language to recordings: pick the language a recording's summary, action items and template results are written in from the top of its side panel, or set one for all new recordings; the transcript and your own notes stay as they are.
- Added summary templates to recordings: besides the Meeting summary there are Lecture notes and Interview highlights, and you can write your own in plain words, try it on a recording before saving, and switch a recording between templates while each result is kept.
- Changed recording summaries to show more than text: the Meeting summary has figures and a timeline whose times jump to that moment, and a template's result can use cards, tables, checklists, flashcards, quizzes and charts.
- Added Show translation to a recording's Notes tab: it adds a translation in the recording's output language under each note you typed, and your notes stay as you wrote them.
- Added Send tasks to Action items to summary templates, so the tasks a template finds also show in the recording's Action items tab, where you can tick them off.
- Added Talk time to the Meeting summary, showing each speaker's share of the conversation when a recording has two or more speakers.
- Added Summary settings to the Recordings page, next to New recording, for the output language and template new recordings take and for managing your templates.
- Recordings and Live Captioning: a long recording transcribes with Enconvo Cloud Fast again. A file over 95 MB (about an hour as the WAV a Recording saves, or a long video) was refused with HTTP 413 before the upload began; it is now re-encoded as compact speech audio first, so recordings up to the 300-minute limit go through. Reported by plkv_andrii.

## Chats and Agents

- Added Temporary chat for one-off questions: turn on the dashed-bubble switch on a new chat in the Chat Window, the App Sidebar or the Dynamic Island, and that chat stays out of your chat history, saves no Memories, and is deleted when you leave it. Keep chat on its banner saves it as a normal chat instead. Requested by Vit_MK.
- Fixed Temporary chat in the Dynamic Island and the App Sidebar: turning it on now lights the switch blue and gives the composer a dashed edge with a Temporary chat tag you can close, and a chat you start there really stays out of your history and is deleted when you leave it.
- Added an Active group at the top of the Chat Window sidebar that gathers every chat that is running, waiting for you, failed or has a result you haven't opened, with its Project and state, so none has to be looked for down the lists.
- Changed the composer button while a task runs: once you type or attach something it turns from Stop back into Send, and a click sends the message into the running task as Return does (hold ⌘, or Ctrl on Windows and Linux, to queue it instead).
- Changed a message you send while the agent is working: an unrelated request now goes to a subagent that works in the background while the agent carries on with its task, instead of waiting for the task to finish.
- Changed a Project's main chat so its agent can start subagents for its own work, alongside the tasks it gives to the Project's members.
- Design work in a Project now starts from the project's own DESIGN.md: when the agent loads its design skill, the whole file comes with it, so its colours, type and component rules shape the design from the first artboard.
- Fixed Project chats loading only the first 32,000 characters of the project's AGENTS.md or CLAUDE.md: the agent now gets every instruction file from the repository root down to the working folder in full, so rules near the end of a long file are no longer missed.
- Changed how an answered question shows in a chat: each answer is now a quiet capsule beside the question, several chosen options sit one per line under it with what you typed, a call that asked several questions names each, and a skipped question shows a dashed Skipped capsule, instead of everything joined on one cut-off line.
- Added a Wrap Code setting (Settings › General › Appearance) and a wrap button beside Copy on code blocks in a reply, so long lines of code can wrap instead of scrolling sideways; it is off by default. Requested by liduchuan.
- Changed the buttons on a code block in a reply to plain icon buttons on the block itself, without the dark box that showed behind them in the Chat Window.
- Chat composer: an attached video shows its first frame with a play mark instead of a plain video icon, and falls back to the icon when the frame can't load.
- Chat history is the same everywhere: the Dynamic Island's History and the Smart Bar's chat list no longer show AI Command chats (Translate, Fix Spelling and Grammar, your own AI Commands), which the Chat Window never listed, so a chat deleted there no longer lingers in the other lists. The App Sidebar's history now lists only the chats started in that app's sidebar, and the Dock badge counts the same chats as the lists. Reported by Vitaliy Malanchuk.
- Fixed a message sent from the Smart Bar sometimes going nowhere, leaving the Smart Bar on Responding… and a chat with only a title and no messages in the main window. When a chat on the same page closed, it took the Smart Bar's send event with it; now a chat gives up only the events it still holds, and the chat still on screen takes them back. If a send still isn't picked up, the Smart Bar sends it once more, and if that fails it shows an error and puts your message back in the input.
- Fixed some cases where a message sent from the Smart Bar, for example to Claude Code, failed with "Couldn't send the message": the Smart Bar now hands it to its own chat page more reliably, and when it still can't, your text, attachments and the message you were editing all come back to the input.
- Added a voice button to the PopBar Ask panel: click it or press the Voice Command hotkey to speak your ask, and it is sent when you stop.
- Changed Browser Use so the tabs an Agent opens close when its turn ends, as Computer Use puts apps back: a page the Agent hands you as the result, a tab handed to you, a tab you pinned and the tab you are looking at stay open, and your own tabs are never closed. Before, every task left its tabs open in a tab group of their own.

## Claude Code

- Claude Code chats: an Ultracode switch in the effort picker, shown while Claude Code says the session can use it (CLI 2.1.322 or later, with workflows on and a model that supports it). It turns on workflow orchestration for the rest of the session at whatever effort is picked, keeps it across an effort change, and follows a typed `/effort ultracode`.
- Fixed Claude Code's Ultracode switch showing an old state after effort or model changes, a session reopening, or a failed change, and translated it in all ten interface languages.

## Models

- Added model recommendations to model pickers in Settings: the list now starts with the best fit, the lowest-cost and the best-quality model for that kind of work, each with a short reason and its price, and a line under the setting tells you when the chosen model costs many times more than the recommended one.
- Added a recommended model to New Agent: it reads what the agent will do from its description and suggests a model, with a cheaper and a stronger one beside it, that you can pick in one click.
- Changed the Enconvo Cloud Plan model list to group models by vendor in every model picker, the Smart Bar and other native windows included: Enconvo's own models come first, then Anthropic, OpenAI, Google and the rest, each under its name with a count. A vendor shows its newest four models and "Show N earlier models" opens the others; the chat's model always shows, and a search still lists every match.
- Added a Generate tab to an agent's avatar, in Create New Agent and in the agent's settings: it draws four avatars from the agent's name and description in the style you pick (Flat, 3D, Anime, Photo, Line or Watercolor) with your image model, only when you press Generate, and you can add details or draw again before choosing one. Requested by Vit_MK.

## Dictation and Live Captioning

- Dictation: press Return while you dictate, holding the hotkey or after tapping it, and the dictation stops, its text goes in and is sent, in one press. Stopping with the hotkey or ✓ only inserts, as before. The setting, now Settings › Dictation › Insert › Return inserts and sends, is on by default; an app rule's Return key column still turns it on or off for one app. Fixed the Return never sending in Discord, Edge, Chrome, Slack and other Chromium or Electron apps, which ignored the key the way it was sent. Requested by plkv_andrii.
- Fixed Return inserts and sends leaving the dictated text unsent in web apps and other slow editors: Enconvo now waits until the pasted text shows in the field before it presses Return.
- Fixed Return inserts and sends in the Chat Window: dictating into the composer with the Dictation key and pressing Return now sends the message instead of leaving the text in the box.
- Added a Return inserts and sends switch to the Dictation HUD card, under Draft, so you can turn it on or off while you dictate.
- Added a Send after inserting switch in Settings › Dictation › Insert: when a dictation ends, its text is inserted and sent without pressing Return. It is off by default and works beside Return inserts and sends.
- Dictation can pause: point at the level in the Dictation HUD and click Pause, or press ⌥P (change it in Settings › Dictation › Pause / Resume). The microphone goes off and your words stay; Resume, or the same key, goes on with the same dictation, and the Dictation hotkey or ✓ finishes it. The key works only while you dictate, and a dictation you hold a key for has no pause.
- Changed the Dictation Pause / Resume hotkey to take more than a key combination: a single key such as Space, or one modifier key on its own that you tap, double-tap or hold (holding pauses until you let go). Set it in Settings › Dictation › Pause / Resume.
- Dictation mutes you in your meeting while you dictate: in a Zoom, Microsoft Teams or Google Meet call, starting a dictation mutes your microphone there, and finishing or pausing it unmutes you. A call you had muted yourself stays muted. Turn it off in Settings › Dictation › Mute my meeting microphone while dictating.
- Dictation also mutes you in an Enconvo Conference and in Zoom and Microsoft Teams calls that run in a browser tab while you dictate, and unmutes you when you finish or pause, as it does for the Zoom and Teams apps and Google Meet.
- Fixed Dictation not muting you in a Google Meet, Zoom or Teams call that runs in Safari or in a website added to the Dock from Safari.
- Dictation: a Live transcript switch hides the words while you dictate, so the bar keeps just its buttons and waveform and covers less of the screen. Turn it on or off in the card that opens when you hover over the bar, without stopping the dictation, or in Settings › Dictation › Listen. Notes, the wait for a microphone, and Clean up's Insert as Spoken still show. Requested by plkv_andrii.
- Changed the Dictation window to leave out the LISTENING label while you simply dictate, so it takes less room; Paused, Clean up's progress and a failure still show their label.
- Changed the Dictation Draft panel to say less: its header shows only Draft and, at its end, the app the draft works in; esc and the Dictation key stand alone without a word beside them; and the Dictation HUD below no longer repeats Draft. ↵ now always pastes the draft into the app it started in, bringing that app back first when another one is in front, instead of copying it.
- Added Send selected text to Settings > Dictation > Draft, on by default: when you start a Draft with text selected that cannot be edited, such as a message or part of a web page, the model gets it as reference and the draft is new text that Return pastes into the field you go back to, instead of a rewrite that could only be copied. Selections on Safari and other web pages are now read as well.
- Live Captioning: choose where the captions bar opens: Left Side (as before), Right Side, Top Center or Bottom Center, where captions line up at the bottom like subtitles. With more than one display, also choose its screen, or keep the one with the pointer. Pick both in the Start Live Captioning window, or right-click the captions bar to move it during a session. Requested by plkv_andrii.
- Fixed Live Captioning not moving to another display when one was picked in the captions bar's right-click menu: the bar was pulled back to the edge of the display it was on. Reported by plkv_andrii.
- Live Captioning: start it from the menu bar menu's Start Live Captioning, or with a hotkey you set in Settings › Shortcuts › Voice; the same hotkey stops captions that are running. Requested by plkv_andrii.
- Live Captioning at the top or bottom of the screen now lays its controls out in a row with a wide caption area beside them, and you can resize the caption area by dragging its edges, at any position. Requested by plkv_andrii.

## iPhone and Android

- Changed the iPhone and Android apps so a free account can pair with its Mac, use the Mac's chats and Mac Screen from the phone, and use the Avatar, which needed an Enconvo Cloud plan or lifetime license before.
- Added an Active group at the top of the iPhone and Android sidebars that gathers the chats waiting on you, failed, still running or holding an unread result, each with where it is and its state, and folds to one row.
- Changed the top bar of a Mac chat in the iPhone and Android apps: the sidebar and New chat buttons now sit together on the left, Side Panel and the chat menu on the right, the title stays centered between them, and messages fade out under the bar instead of ending at a line.
- Added read sync for notifications: once you read a finished chat on your Mac, iPhone, or Android phone, its notification disappears from your other devices, while a question that still waits for an answer stays.
- Changed Android notifications for Mac activity, chat replies and messages so sound, vibration, the notification light and the app badge are all on by default, also on phones that already had Enconvo installed.
- iPhone: an x.com link opened in a chat's side panel shows the post instead of a blank page. X sent the app's web view to Safari with an `x-safari-https:` redirect that the page tab quietly dropped; the tab now reports itself as Safari, as the Mac's side panel does, and opens any such redirect in place.
- Fixed videos and files from a chat failing to play or download on the phone when the Mac has a large chat history.
- Fixed the Android app so a message sent while a reply is running shows in the chat at once instead of after the Mac answers.
- Fixed the first message of a new chat sent from the phone with a photo or file sometimes not showing in the chat while the reply did.
- Fixed pictures in a Friend Chat on Android opening the system's "Open with" list of other apps: a tap now shows the picture full screen inside Enconvo, where you can pinch or double-tap to zoom, swipe down to close, save it, or share it. Open With… stays in the picture's long-press menu.

## Also in this release

- Added Tutorials to Settings, where update videos and step-by-step walkthroughs play inside Enconvo and the ones you haven't seen are marked New.
- Added a card in the Chat Window sidebar that offers a version's update video the first time you use Enconvo after updating.
- Changed What's new in the Chat Window's settings menu to open Tutorials in Settings.
- A computer's Screen can now go real full screen: the new full screen button (or ⌃⌥F) puts the picture over the whole display, with the controls sliding in when the pointer reaches the top edge; Esc also leaves it while watching. A Fill switch next to it scales the picture to fill the view with no black bars, cropping the edges that do not fit.
- Changed calls with a live video character so a silent call no longer uses up your daily video time: after a minute without anyone speaking her live picture rests until you talk again, after a minute and a half she asks "Still there?", and a minute later the call hangs up with a Continue button to pick it up again.
- The Out of Cloud points card (and its Waiting and Points added states) now shows in your interface language. Before, every one of its words stayed English in all 10 languages.
- Changed Share anonymous product analytics on the Mac to be on unless you turn it off, in the first-run guide or in Settings › General › Privacy; a Mac where you already set the switch keeps your choice.
- Fixed the Settings sidebar sometimes opening blank, with its menu items still clickable but not drawn.

## Local API

- Added `agent/session/list`, a route that lists the chats running now across every agent and Project (Claude Code and Codex chats included), with each one's title, agent and Project; it checks each run, so a chat whose status still says running after a crash is left out, and `filter: "waiting"` lists the chats waiting for your answer. `agent/session/status` now checks one chat only and asks for its `sessionId`.
- Added `agent/session/steer`, a route that adds a message to the turn another chat is running now without stopping it, as `agent/session/list` finds them; a chat that isn't running answers `not_running` and points to `agent/call`, and a run below Full access can't steer an Enconvo turn.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.13 Changelog (2026-11-11)

Thanks for all the feedback during the beta. This release adds Spaces, pages of your own that the agent builds and keeps in your sidebar and on your phones, Workspaces for each client or job, redesigned Projects with one Board for every task, and Team Knowledge Bases. Agents can call your friends and people from your own iPhone number, answer the calls you miss, work on SSH hosts, steer their Subagents and keep going until a goal is met. Saved API keys and sign-ins are now encrypted on your Mac and stay out of the conversation, App Sidebar reads the page or app beside it, and Friend Chat gets groups and reactions. Update the iPhone app to 1.2.13 and the Android app to 1.4.7 to try the phone changes.

[Watch the 2.5.13 update tour](https://youtu.be/viSojOLdGBU) to see Spaces, Workspaces, Projects and the Board, App Sidebar, SSH hosts and Dictation drafts in action.

<YouTubePlayer videoId="viSojOLdGBU" title="What's New in Enconvo 2.5.13" />

## Highlights

- **Spaces**: ask the agent for a page of your own, such as a live board of deals or a tracker, and it builds it as a plugin that stays in the Chat Window sidebar, on the Plugins page, in the Dock or menu bar, and full screen in your iPhone and Android apps; share one with a link anyone can install.
- **Workspaces**: keep each client or job's chats, Memory, knowledge and Connector accounts apart, put each teammate in the Workspaces they work in, and switch between them from the top of the Chat Window sidebar with ⌃⌘1 to ⌃⌘9.
- **Projects and the Board, redesigned**: Chat, Board and Team & settings tabs, a Needs you / Working on / New results panel, Smart start for ready plans, archiving without deleting, and one Board under Memory that shows the tasks of every chat, Project and team.
- **Team Knowledge Bases**: share a knowledge base with people or your whole Team as Can view, Can add or Can manage, with additions reaching every Mac within seconds.
- **Agents call people**: an agent can call an Enconvo friend or teammate, or anyone from your own iPhone number through Your iPhone, Twilio or Telnyx, and bring back their answer, a recording and the transcript; Answer my calls takes the calls you miss and sends you a message.
- **Keys and sign-ins stay out of the chat**: saved API keys and sign-ins are encrypted with a key that stays in this Mac's Keychain; when a task needs a key, a Connect card asks for it and the agent only ever names it, and website and Mac app logins are filled in without the agent seeing the password.
- **Goals**: type `/goal` and what should be true when the work is done, and the agent keeps working turn after turn until a check finds it met.
- **Steer a running task**: Return sends your words to the running task at its next step, ⌘Return queues them for when it finishes, and the iPhone and Android apps steer the same way.
- **Subagents do more**: agents can message, interrupt and steer running Subagents, choose their model and effort, nest them, run Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, Grok Build or Muse Code as Subagents, each in its own git worktree, and run them on another paired computer.
- **Muse Code and Grok Bot**: Meta's coding agent joins the Coding agents, and your own xAI Grok Bot routine chats in Enconvo and can ask an agent on your Mac for help.
- **SSH hosts and clearer pairing**: Settings › Connections lists Phones, Computers and SSH hosts, agents run commands on your SSH hosts, and a device switcher at the foot of the Chat Window sidebar shows one computer at a time.
- **App Sidebar beside every app**: it reads the page or app beside it, such as a video's captions and chapters, with built-in profiles for YouTube, GitHub, Gmail, Finder, code editors and more, and Finder file jobs on the selected files.
- **Friend Chat groups, replies and reactions**: start a group and @mention people, see who has seen your message, react with any emoji, reply with a quote, and select several messages to forward or copy, on the Mac, iPhone and Android.
- **Live Photo characters**: turn a portrait into an Avatar character that stands and breathes on your desktop and appears as live video in calls.
- **Quick tools in Live Talk**: during a call the assistant can search your Memory and past chats, read your Calendar and Reminders, search your mail, and stay quiet until you call her by name.
- **GitHub triggers for Automations**: run an automation when an issue or pull request opens, a comment mentions you, CI fails or a release is published, with a time limit for each run and every run in Chats.
- **Several accounts per Connector**: connect more than one Notion, Linear or other account, pick a default, and set one for each Workspace.
- **Image and Video artifacts**: keep editing an image or video an agent made beside the chat, with each model's settings and a version for every edit.
- **Quick access in the sidebar**: pin pages, Projects and links to the Chat Window sidebar, and see chats that need you on the Dock icon's badge.
- **New models**: Claude Fable 5.2 with up to 1M tokens of context, Gemini 4 Carbon, and ElevenLabs Scribe v3 and Scribe v3 Live for Dictation and Live Talk.
- **Dictation to Draft and Voice Clone**: tap ⇧ while dictating to turn what you said into a Draft's instruction, and clone a voice for ElevenLabs, Fish Audio, Gemini and Qwen3.
- **Faster, sturdier phone apps**: the iPhone and Android apps connect to your Mac directly on the same network, send while your Mac is offline, and keep Mac chats in view through dropped connections.

## Spaces

- Added Spaces: ask the Agent for a page of your own, such as a live board of deals gathered from several forums or a tracker, and it builds it as a plugin that stays in the Chat Window's sidebar under Spaces, on the Plugins page's new Spaces tab and in the Enconvo apps on your phones; press New space in either place to start one.
- Added Spaces to the iPhone app: they sit in the sidebar above Friends, open full screen with a box to ask about the one you're looking at, and can be held and dragged into a new order that your Mac keeps too.
- Added sharing for plugins and Spaces: choose Share… to send a link that anyone you give it to can install, or list it in the Plugin Store; Enconvo reviews each version before the link installs it, the link opens its page on enconvo.com, and you can update it, change who can install it or stop sharing at any time.
- Added Share to a Space's menu in the iPhone and Android apps, which sends its link through the system share sheet, or opens Share on your Mac for a Space you haven't shared yet.
- Changed how the Agent builds Spaces that show live values such as stock quotes: they show when the source set each value and whether the market is closed, color rises and falls the way your region does, and keep a list you edit on the page showing after you add or remove an item.
- Changed how the Agent lays out Spaces with lists of numbers on a phone: each item takes two short lines instead of a squeezed table, so names no longer wrap one character at a time.
- Changed how the Agent builds Spaces you fill in yourself, such as a log, a tracker or a daily score: a request like that becomes a Space without asking where to import the data from, every change saves at once, an empty day says nothing is logged instead of showing a score, the score shows how it was worked out, the page no longer repeats the Space's title, and it gets an icon of its own.
- Changed how the Agent builds Spaces that track dates and bills, such as subscriptions: it can add a reminder to Apple Reminders before a renewal and read receipts from mailboxes you let agents read, and a form field it hides on the page stays hidden.
- Changed opening a Space you opened before in the iPhone app: it shows its page as you last saw it as soon as the page loads, then brings in the newest data from your Mac, so it opens in a few seconds instead of about ten.
- Changed how the Agent builds Spaces that gather from several sources: a source it can't reach is named at the top of the page while its earlier items stay, and Ask about can answer from what the Space shows.
- Changed Ask about on a Space in the Chat Window to tell the Agent which Space you mean and where its files are, so it can change it without looking for it first.
- Changed Ask about on a Space in the iPhone and Android apps to tell the Agent which Space you mean and where its files are, as the Chat Window does, so your question goes in on its own.
- Changed Refresh in a Space's menu in the iPhone and Android apps to gather the Space's newest items on your Mac before showing it again.
- Changed the iPhone and Android apps to keep the Spaces you opened last ready, so going back to one shows it at once and brings in what changed.
- Changed new Spaces to keep separate data in each Workspace, so a Space used in a client Workspace doesn't show or overwrite your own records.
- Fixed app and Space icons drawn as SVG showing a generic icon in the iPhone and Android apps.
- Fixed a Space made in Create plugin being labeled an Enconvo plugin on its card; it now says Space.
- Fixed new Spaces opening in the Chat Window without the Ask about box, which changes the Space by talking to it.
- Fixed New space in the iPhone app starting in the Project you last chose, which put the new Space's files in that project; it now starts without one and leaves your choice for new chats as it was.
- Fixed a Space you just made landing at the end of the Spaces list, behind See all in the phone apps; new Spaces now show at the top until you drag them somewhere else.
- Fixed a Space in the Chat Window sidebar showing old entries after you went to another page and came back, when they had changed from a chat or a phone meanwhile.
- Fixed Share saying a plugin's link was up to date after the review declined its newer version; it now shows why and offers Fix in chat.
- Fixed a Space you took out of the sidebar no longer opening from a chat's view of it in the iPhone and Android apps.
- Fixed the iPhone app's sidebar losing your Spaces when it opens while your Mac is away; it shows the ones it last saw, and a Space's own close button now takes you back Home.
- Fixed a Space's file upload buttons doing nothing in the Android app, and a Space staying open after you removed its Mac.
- Fixed an open Space not showing a change you asked for in its chat until you opened it again: the Space beside the chat and its own page now update within a few seconds of the agent's change.

## Projects, Board and Workspaces

- Added Archive project to a Project's ⋯ menu in the sidebar: the Project and its chats leave the sidebar and its scheduled tasks pause, with nothing deleted. Restore it in Settings › Archived › Projects, where its tasks turn on again; a chat of an archived Project can't take messages until you restore it.
- Added a to-do's own board: opening a to-do that has tasks shows just its tasks, with a switch to its chat with the lead.
- Added Team & settings to each Project, where you see the team, add members, and choose when the team checks with you.
- Added "Start planning as soon as a to-do is added", on by default, so the lead plans each new to-do right away.
- Added Smart, now the default for when a plan is ready: the plan starts at once when it does what you already asked, and waits for your Start when it goes further; Always ask me and Always start are still there.
- Added Project members on your other paired computers: add an Agent from another computer to a Project, and the Lead hands it tasks that need that computer, with the answer coming back as the task's result.
- Added a Board under Memory in the sidebar that shows the tasks of every chat, Project and team in one place, with a filter at the top left to pick the Projects you want and a count of the tasks that need you.
- Added Workspaces: create one in Settings › Workspaces for a client or a job, and it keeps its own chats, Memory and knowledge apart from yours, with a switcher at the top of the Chat Window sidebar (⌃⌘1 to ⌃⌘9) to move between them.
- Added Team members by Workspace: once you have a Client Workspace, the Teams page lets you put each member in one or more of your Workspaces, and members then see, chat with and call only the teammates who share a Workspace with them; on your Mac each teammate shows in the Friends list of the Workspaces they are in.
- Changed a Project's page to three tabs, Chat, Board and Team & settings, with the team's faces (the lead first, up to five) at the top left.
- Changed the panel above a Project chat's composer to three columns, Needs you, Working on and New results, each row opening its to-do.
- Changed the team menu at the top left of a Project to show the whole team at once: the Lead on its own card with Change, each member with a role you can edit in place, and Add member with search on its own page.
- Fixed the Chinese name of a Project's members, which read as "会员" (membership) instead of "成员".
- Fixed agents asking for approval at Full access when they handed work to another agent or a Coding agent: the agent they call now works in the same Permission mode as the one you chat with.
- Fixed a Project member on another computer whose task couldn't start because that computer has no device access on your account: the task now says so and points to Manage devices, instead of blaming the pairing.
- Fixed webhook automations no longer receiving requests after an automation was saved or deleted in a Workspace.
- Fixed background mail sync, Board sweeps and Memory review running for a Workspace instead of your own data after the first request after an update came from that Workspace.
- Added a Workspace menu at the top of the Chat Window sidebar even before you have a second Workspace, and creating your first Workspace now starts with a short guide to what a Workspace is and how to use it.
- Added Artifacts to Desktop Sync: your artifacts, with every version, now reach your other Macs signed in to the same account, including documents, designs, slides, dashboards, pictures and videos, and conflicting edits on two Macs keep both versions.
- Added Quick access to the Chat Window sidebar: pin pages, Projects and links from the Settings menu's Sidebar Shortcuts, then drag to reorder, rename or remove them.
- Added the Make something row (Slides, Doc, Design, Film, Dashboard, Web page, Image, Video) under a new chat's composer inside a Project.
- Fixed a Board task failing when the model's reply broke off partway on a closed connection; it now runs again by itself after a pause, as it does after other passing model errors.

## Agents and Subagents

- Added an Open button on a plugin's page in Settings › Plugins when the plugin has a view in the Chat Window, such as Email: it opens that view there (a plugin with several views gets an Open menu), so what lives inside the Mini App, such as adding a mailbox, is one click from its page.
- Added a Retry with button to the Out of Cloud points card in chat: once you switch the chat to a model outside Enconvo Cloud, one click runs the refused message again on that model.
- Changed how agents find skills, plugins, Connectors and MCP servers for a task: they now find ones that fit a request worded differently from their descriptions, or written in another language such as Chinese, not only ones that share its keywords.
- Fixed an Agent set to Auto saying it had no skills when you asked which skills or tools it has; that question now runs as Agent, which lists them.
- Fixed an Agent set to Auto sometimes answering that it couldn't open a web page when you asked it to summarize one by its link; it now reads the page.
- Fixed the agent sometimes saving a file you asked for in your home folder (a path starting with ~) inside its own workspace instead.
- Fixed the agent sometimes not seeing a file it downloaded in the browser, such as a PDF opened in a new tab, and retrying the download.
- Fixed the agent sometimes answering an English request in Chinese, along with its step labels, when the computer's time zone or the account name pointed to China.
- Fixed the agent sometimes saying a reminder or scheduled task would report back in the current chat; it now points to Automations, where the result shows.
- Fixed the agent sometimes giving the wrong time for another city around a daylight saving change; it now works it out from the time zone database.
- Fixed the agent being unable to check a skill it had just created when its Python environment lacked the YAML package.
- Fixed the close button of an agent's settings panel in the Chat Window sitting under its menu button, which opened the menu instead; the panel's back arrow closes it.
- Fixed agents guessing the timing when making subtitles from audio; a transcription that asks for timestamps or speakers now gives each line its times and speaker.
- Fixed agents failing to use an MCP server's tools on the first try when the server's name has spaces.
- Fixed an agent call to a Coding agent that isn't signed in reporting success with an empty answer; the calling agent or app now gets the sign-in error.
- Fixed a question from an agent sometimes showing no options to choose from, along with other tool calls that lost a list or setting the agent passed in.
- Fixed the Agents list in Settings saying an agent was Not used yet after you chatted with it in the Chat Window, and agent lists now put the agents you chatted with most recently first.
- Fixed the Chat Window opening with no agent, model or permissions after you deleted the agent it was set to start with; it now goes back to Mavis.
- Fixed an agent set to Auto answering in plain chat instead of running the command, plugin, workflow or app you @-mentioned in your message.
- Fixed an agent that ran an AI Command for you stopping as if you had pressed Stop, and the command's own reply landing in your chat.
- Fixed an agent answering in a messaging app or another chat with no window promising to report a background command's result later, though nothing would tell it the command had finished; it now asks you to check back, or waits for the result.
- Fixed a chat open in two windows not showing a reply started from the other window until it was opened again; the reply now streams in both.
- Fixed some new chats being titled "summary", or getting a title that starts with "User message:".
- Fixed an agent's command or file write sent together with opening a web page being skipped, so the agent had to send it again.
- Fixed an agent that called a feature such as YouTube transcripts by a wrong name taking several tries to find the right call.
- Fixed agent progress messages in Telegram and Discord, and some step tooltips in chat, saying only "Enconvo API" instead of naming what the step called.
- Fixed an automation's result, its notification and the chat message it sends starting with the agent's opening line instead of its answer.
- Fixed the assistant, asked to have Claude Code or Codex do something, running their command line itself instead of starting a chat with them.
- Fixed a skill rewritten after it was created keeping its old description, so the assistant didn't recognize it.
- Fixed Auto answering a request to use one of your skills by name as a plain reply, without the skill.
- Fixed an agent that handed work to Codex taking Codex's warning for its answer when Codex couldn't sign in, instead of saying to sign in again.
- Fixed Paste and Insert after a Coding agent's reply in the Smart Bar adding its warnings and notices to the text.
- Fixed agents failing to install Python packages with `python -m pip` or to run `pip --version`, and the word pip being changed inside other text in the commands they run.
- Fixed a Claude Code or Codex chat that could fail to start on a new computer when it opened while Enconvo was still starting up.
- Fixed agents asked for an automation that reacts to new files in a folder or another event leaving a background script running, which stopped when the computer restarted and could not be managed: they now set it up as an Automation.
- Fixed agents asked to check a web page every hour and report changes setting up an hourly agent run that used points even when nothing changed: they now watch the page and run only when it changes.
- Fixed shell automations that check for a problem, such as a nearly full disk, finishing without telling you: agents now write them to fail when they find it, so you get a notification.
- Fixed agents asked about your notes in an app such as Obsidian searching the knowledge base and the web instead of using that app's installed plugin when it hadn't been used before.
- Fixed agents not finding built-in features that have no SmartBar command, such as Usage Statistics and Mail Alerts, and saying Enconvo had no such feature.
- Fixed agents not knowing about Usage Statistics, Updates, Mail Alerts, Snippets and Quicklinks: asked about your AI usage, what Updates handled, whether Mail Alerts watches your mail, or to save a snippet or quicklink, they now use those features directly.
- Fixed agents saying a new automation would run for feed items, files or messages that were already there when you asked them to try a trigger first; those already happened, and an automation runs only for what comes after it is turned on.
- Fixed agents offering to install a Plugin Store plugin that doesn't run on your computer, which the install then refused.
- Fixed agents failing to open an installed store plugin, such as the Plugin Development Guide, whose guide has a different name than the store lists.
- Fixed agents not finding Clipboard History, Bookmark Search and File Search when looking for a tool that does a task.
- Fixed the chat model picker showing a newly picked model when saving it failed; it now says the change wasn't saved and shows the model the chat still uses.
- Fixed the model picker on its first open after launch selecting a different provider than the one you clicked, because the provider list regrouped under the pointer.
- Added agent-to-agent steering: an agent can now add a message to a running Subagent's task, interrupt it, or reach another agent's chat in the same Project, and stopping a chat now stops every Subagent below it.
- Added model, reasoning effort, instructions, name, and fork options when an agent starts a Subagent, with Subagents nesting up to three levels and Claude Code or Codex available as Subagents.
- Added more to Subagents in the chat: starting one shows the model and effort it runs on, a message to one says whether it steered the running task, waited, interrupted it or started a new turn, a Subagent's chat marks each message with the agent that sent it, and the chat's Lineage shows the whole tree, three levels deep.
- Added Workspace management to agents: when you ask, an agent can list, create, rename, recolor, reorder, delete and restore your Workspaces, and it confirms before deleting one.
- Changed Lineage in a Coding agent's chat Details to show the Enconvo chat that started it.
- Fixed the mark that says which agent sent a message: it now always names the agent that really sent it, also on queued /loop commands and on messages steered into a Grok Build or Muse Code turn, and an open Lineage shows a new Coding agent Subagent without reopening it.
- Fixed agents, Coding agents, Workflow steps and Automations being able to answer a question meant for you, write or edit a chat's messages, change a Coding agent's Enconvo Capabilities, or start another agent's run by sending the call through the Local API's request route, another command, several wrapped commands or a context read; all of them now refuse those calls the same way.
- Fixed agents, Workflow steps and Automations being able to start a workflow's repair agent as another agent, such as a Coding agent, outside your chat's Permission mode; the repair agent now always works as the chat's own agent, within its mode, and its task shows who sent it.
- Added Antigravity, Grok Build and Muse Code as Subagents beside Claude Code and Codex, and a Subagent's card now shows its Coding agent's icon, where it works, and the tool calls, time and tokens it used.
- Added a git worktree of its own for each Subagent that changes files while another one does, so two Subagents never overwrite each other's work; a worktree with no changes is removed when its Subagent finishes.
- Added running a Subagent on another of your paired computers, so an agent can hand a task to that computer's agent or a Coding agent there.
- Added your own Subagent helpers from `~/.enconvo/agents`, `~/.claude/agents` and `~/.codex/agents` to every chat, and the skills, MCP servers, turn limit, background and worktree settings in Claude Code agent files now take effect.
- Added Subagent pipelines, so an agent can run a planned set of Subagents in one step, such as the same check over a list of files, and resume a failed run without running the finished Subagents again.
- Added Chat tasks to Project Boards: when a chat in a Project changes files or runs a command, its work shows as a card that follows the chat to Done with a short summary and the files it changed, opens the chat when clicked, and lets the Project lead steer clear of the same files.
- Added handing work to a model by name in a Project's Lead chat, also when the Project has no members, and in a chat where you @mention other Agents: ask to give part of a request to a model such as Opus 5.5 and Mavis does that task on it, or name an Agent and a model together, such as Claude Code on Opus 5.5, or Codex, Grok Build, Antigravity or Muse Code on one of their own models, to run that Agent on it for that task only, even when that Agent isn't one of the Project's members; the plan's card and the Board show the model beside who does it, the lead asks when the name could mean several models or none, and the plan waits for Start while you are signed out.
- Fixed Codex, Claude Code and Antigravity members of a Project asking you to approve commands the Project's Permissions setting allows: they now work in the lead's mode on every task, and Approve for me runs Codex in its own Approve for me.
- Added worktree cleanup to Project settings: a worktree is now archived, and its folder removed to free disk space, once none of its chats is left in the chat list or after its chats sit idle for 7 days (3, 14 or 30 to choose), while a worktree with uncommitted changes stays and Restore brings an archived one back.
- Changed sending a message while a task runs: Return now steers the task at its next step, and ⌘Return queues the message for when it finishes.
- Changed Esc to stop a running task on its second press, so one stray press no longer ends a long task.
- Added goals to agent chats: type `/goal` and what should be true when the work is done, or choose Set a goal from the composer's + menu, and the agent keeps working turn after turn until a check finds the goal met; the goal shows above the composer with its running time, where you can edit, clear, or resume it.
- Changed the Chat Window sidebar to leave out the chats your agent starts when it asks Codex, Claude Code or another agent for help: open one from its line in the chat that asked, and it still gets a row while it is open, pinned, or waiting for your answer.
- Changed the summary an agent gives when a task ends: it now opens with the outcome, then lists what changed, what was checked and what needs your attention, without the steps that led there, and draws the checks and changes as views as far as the chat's Intelligent UI level says.
- Added your friends and teammates to the composer's @ menu: name someone in a chat and the agent knows whom you mean, so it can message them privately, read their replies, or call them when you ask.

## Coding Agents

- Added Muse Code, Meta's coding agent, as a Coding agent: install it and sign in with Meta from its settings, then chat with it in a project folder, where it edits code with diffs, runs commands, asks before acting, offers its skills and /compact in the / menu, and its own sessions show up to continue under the names you gave them.
- Added Continue in Terminal and Copy command to Muse Code chats: the chat opens with `muse resume` in its folder, and the turns you run there come back into the chat when you quit Muse Code and send from Enconvo again.
- Added Enconvo Capabilities to each Coding agent's settings: turn off Enconvo's plugins for Claude Code, Codex, Grok Build, Muse Code or Antigravity altogether, or one plugin at a time, such as messaging Enconvo agents, image creation, memory or Computer Use, so that agent can't see or use it.
- Changed Muse Code to keep working after it updates itself, and to say when Enconvo needs an update to work with a newer Muse Code.
- Changed Muse Code chats to work without a Meta sign-in when Muse Code runs on its local model, and to take their first message as their title when they start from the Smart Bar or the iPhone and Muse Code doesn't name them.
- Changed Muse Code's approval questions to show the change an edit would make, and to pass what you type with Deny to Muse Code in the same turn, so it can act on it right away.
- Fixed a Muse Code chat continued with `muse resume` typed in a terminal by hand not showing as running in that terminal, and long Muse Code chats taking much longer to reopen than short ones.
- Fixed a Coding agent chat whose first message was refused, such as when the agent isn't signed in, taking that message as its title; it stays New Chat.
- Fixed links to an agent's settings, such as Muse Code's, opening "This plugin isn't installed" instead of the agent's page.
- Fixed a step you denied still reading as done, such as "Edited notes.txt", and a Coding agent reply stopped from the iPhone or Android app before it showed anything leaving no line in the chat.
- Fixed coding agents' steps and "Thought for" lines staying in English in chats shown in another language.
- Fixed Coding agent chats in a client's Workspace saving their replies, asking their questions, calling other agents and loading skills in Personal; they now stay entirely inside that Workspace.
- Fixed a Coding agent's Enconvo Capabilities sometimes not applying to its open chats when they were changed in Personal and in a client's Workspace at the same time.
- Added Grok Bot, xAI's agent that runs in the cloud, as a Coding agent: connect your own Grok Bot routine in its settings, then chat with it in Enconvo, where each message goes to the routine and its reply comes back to the chat, even when it arrives late.
- Added Enconvo's MCP server for Grok Bot: connect it in Grok Bot's settings, add its address in Grok Bot's Plugins, and Grok Bot signs in with your Enconvo account, so it can reply to your chats, notify your Mac and your paired phones, and ask an agent on your Mac, which you approve each time unless you always allow it; each request stays listed in the Grok Bot on this Mac chat, and disconnecting Grok Bot, or your Mac going offline, withdraws a request still waiting.
- Added a length limit to Grok Bot messages: a message over 32,000 characters is refused with a note to shorten it, and attached files and images stay on your Mac while only the text is sent.
- Fixed Grok Bot losing the answer to a request it sent your Mac when you approved it after about a minute; Grok Bot now gets the answer once it comes, and the request still runs once.
- Fixed tapping a Grok Bot notification on iPhone and Android opening nothing: it opens the Grok Bot chat the notification is about, or your Mac's chats when it names none, and clicking it on the Mac opens that chat too.
- Fixed the agent picker on the Mac, iPhone, and Android to show Grok Bot as Not set up, instead of Not installed, until its routine is connected.
- Fixed Grok Bot staying connected for replies after its webhook was cleared in Credential Management, and its settings showing or putting back an old webhook after it was changed.
- Fixed Grok Bot's Test saying it couldn't reach Enconvo when the routine or Enconvo was only turning away too many messages; it now says so.
