- Fixed the Files tab of a Grok Bot chat on iPhone and Android offering files from your Mac; it now says the files Grok Bot makes stay in xAI's cloud, without Sort or search.
- Fixed Context & Usage in a Grok Bot chat on iPhone and Android asking you to send a message to fill it; it now says xAI's cloud doesn't report the tokens Grok Bot's replies use.
- Fixed the note box in Grok Bot's request to your Mac saying the note goes to Enconvo; it now says it goes to Grok Bot, which gets your note as the answer.
- Fixed Grok Bot's notes, requests, records and delivery errors showing in English on the Mac, iPhone, and Android when the app uses another language, its note button saying it sends to an agent, the Grok Bot on this Mac chat keeping the name it was first given in another language, and a stray space before an agent's name in Chinese.
- Fixed Grok Bot replies that arrived while Enconvo on the Mac restarted or was busy with another turn: they now wait at Enconvo Cloud until the chat has them, and show once.
- Fixed Grok Bot asks to an agent on your Mac that still ran after you disconnected Grok Bot, and an ask too long to show now shows the whole request before you approve it.
- Fixed Muse Code questions that accepted more or fewer choices than Muse Code allows, and Muse Code's effort setting not following a model you switch to mid-chat.

## Credentials and Logins

- Changed how saved API keys and sign-ins are kept on the Mac: each is now encrypted with a key that stays in this Mac's Keychain, so a copy of Enconvo's settings folder can no longer be read on another computer, and keys are entered again after going back to an older version.
- Changed who can read saved API keys and sign-ins on the Mac: another program that asks Enconvo's local API for settings now gets `[hidden]` in place of each key, and a plugin from the store can use only its own keys and the credential picked in its settings, while Settings shows and checks keys as before.
- Changed when saved API keys and sign-ins are opened on the Mac: only code that Enconvo itself installed and that is still unchanged gets them, so a program that rewrites one of Enconvo's built-in extensions or its runtime in Enconvo's settings folder gets no key that way, while the app and everything that needs no saved key keep working.
- Changed who can change where a saved API key is sent on the Mac: only Enconvo's own windows can change a provider's or credential's address, or make an MCP server send a saved key or connect somewhere else with one, while another program using Enconvo's local API, the phone and the agent are refused; the phone's Connect card still connects a Connector with an API key, to the server the Connector declares.
- Fixed a setting or a refreshed sign-in sometimes being lost when two tasks saved the same provider's settings at the same moment.
- Added a way for the agent to ask for an API key in the chat: when a task needs a service's key, a Connect card asks for it, the key goes to Credentials instead of the conversation, and later chats use it without asking again.
- Added support for services Enconvo has no provider for: the Connect card shows the host a key will be sent to, saves it under Other services on the Credentials page, and the agent can use it only in requests to that host.
- Changed how the agent uses saved keys: it names a credential instead of holding the key, and the key is replaced with [redacted] wherever a request or command returns it.
- Added website logins: when a browser task reaches a sign-in page, a card asks for the user name and password once, Enconvo fills them in on that site only, and the agent never sees the password; saved logins appear under Logins on the Credentials page, and unticking Remember this login uses one for that sign-in only.
- Added typing a saved login into a Mac app: when an app's sign-in needs a password, a card that names the app asks for the user name and password, and Enconvo types them into that app only, the password only into a field that hides what is typed, without the agent ever seeing it.
- Added taking a key out of the chat: when you paste an API key into a message anyway, Enconvo saves it in Credentials and replaces it with [redacted] in the saved chat, so later requests and your other devices no longer carry it; a new key you paste for a service that already has one replaces the saved key.

## Agent Phone and Calls

- Added calls to friends by your agent: ask an agent to call an Enconvo friend or teammate, and it rings their Enconvo apps, talks with them for you from what you tell it, and brings their answer and the call's transcript back to your chat; a scheduled task can message and call the friends you let it reach, so it can, for example, send someone a report at midnight and call them in the morning if they haven't replied.
- Changed Record calls in Settings › Agent Phone to also record agent calls you take in the iPhone and Android apps, saving them to Recordings on your Mac with their transcript.
- Fixed answering an agent's call in the Android app on some phones ending the call at once with "The microphone isn't available right now.": the call now connects and your agent hears you.
- Fixed Record calls in Settings › Agent Phone so calls agents make to other people for you are recorded to Recordings too, with a recording button in Recent calls.
- Fixed agents ringing the Enconvo app on your phone when you asked them to call you on a phone number: a number you give is now dialed through your phone provider, and agents tell apart calls to a number, calls to you in the app, and calls to Enconvo friends.
- Added Your iPhone to Settings › Agent Phone: agents can call other people from your own iPhone number through your Mac, so the people they call see your number.
- Added a picture-led setup guide for Your iPhone that shows how your Mac and iPhone connect, walks through one step at a time, and turns on Calls from iPhone in FaceTime for you in one click.
- Added Calls from to Settings › Agent Phone › Tasks awaiting your input: when an agent calls your number, it can ring from Your iPhone, Twilio or Telnyx, and you can ask an agent in a chat to call you through any of them or the EnConvo app.
- Added Answer my calls: when you don't pick up a call to your iPhone, an agent can answer it on your Mac, take a message, and send the call to Updates.
- Added recordings to an agent's calls to your friends: the friend hears and sees that the call is recorded, and when it ends the recording is in Recordings and opens from the call card in the chat.
- Changed agents to call your Enconvo friends and teammates on Enconvo first, and to call their phone number only when you ask for a phone call or that call doesn't reach them.
- Changed an agent's calls to your friends so the agent chats freely when asked, such as telling a story or explaining something, instead of refusing anything outside its brief.
- Fixed the person an agent calls through Your iPhone not hearing the agent: Enconvo Audio now plays the agent's voice through a device of its own, so the call's echo cancelling no longer removes it, and each call picks Enconvo Audio as the Phone app's microphone and puts your choice back afterwards; Settings asks you to add Enconvo Audio again once.
- Fixed agent calls through Your iPhone not going out on macOS 27, where the call prompt shows as a notification.
- Fixed agent calls through Your iPhone staying on after the other person hung up; the call now ends as soon as they hang up.
- Fixed Add again on Your iPhone in Settings › Agent Phone giving no word on macOS's password window and staying on Enconvo Audio is missing after you typed the password.
- Fixed an agent's call to a friend hanging up before the friend could answer; the agent now waits for their reply before it says goodbye.
- Fixed an agent's call to a friend showing as a conference with "You're the only one here" after the agent hung up; the friend's screen now shows it as a call with the agent, and the call ends for them when the agent hangs up.
- Added a phone line setup to call cards when no line is set up, so you can set up Your iPhone or Telnyx right in the chat and the agent's call goes through once it's ready.
- Added Numbers that can give tasks to Answer my calls, so calls from your other numbers talk to your agent and can give it tasks while other callers only leave a message.
- Added Answers first to Answer my calls, so with several Macs you pick the one that answers and the others answer only when it's offline.
- Fixed Contacts access on the Mac, so macOS now asks for it and Answer my calls can recognize callers saved in Contacts.
- Changed Answer my calls to ask for Contacts when you set it up or add a number, and to hand a call shown by a contact's name to a Mac that can read Contacts.
- Fixed Answer my calls on macOS 27.2 and 27.3, which sometimes left a ringing call unanswered, so an agent now answers the calls you don't pick up.
- Changed Answer my calls to wait 5 seconds by default before an agent answers, with 5 seconds added to the choices.
- Changed Agent call settings into separate sections for Your iPhone, Phone providers, and the EnConvo mobile app, so each way of calling is set up on its own.
- Fixed agents being unable to press keys on calls through Your iPhone, so they can now get through automated phone menus such as a carrier's service line.
- Fixed calls through Your iPhone to a contact saved under a short name, which failed saying macOS never showed the call prompt.
- Fixed calls through Your iPhone on macOS 27.3, which shows the call prompt and the call under the menu bar, so agents can place calls and press keys there too.
- Fixed Your iPhone's one-click setup leaving notifications off for Phone and FaceTime, which kept macOS from ever showing the call prompt; it now turns them on, or shows where to do it.
- Changed Your iPhone's Check the audio to check this Mac's setup again first, and to reopen the setup guide when Calls from iPhone or notifications were turned off.
- Fixed Your iPhone's audio check reporting that Enconvo can't hear calls on Macs whose sound plays through multi-channel speakers such as Studio Display, and its setup now points to the System Audio Recording Only list.
- Changed Full access so it no longer asks before an agent calls or messages your friends and teammates: it now asks only before changes that could harm your computer.
- Changed Your iPhone's setup to guide you through each setting on this Mac step by step, showing where to click and opening the right place, instead of clicking through System Settings for you.
- Changed an agent's call to a friend so the agent greets them within a few seconds of their answer, instead of after about ten.
- Fixed the person an agent called seeing an empty conference for a moment when the agent hung up; the call now just ends.
- Fixed an agent calling a friend losing its greeting when the friend spoke over it, and letting talk near the friend keep a silent call going.
- Fixed the iPhone's incoming call screen staying up for good when Answer couldn't connect after the caller had already hung up; it now shows the call as missed.
- Fixed an agent's call to a friend going quiet for the rest of the call when its voice provider stopped responding.
- Fixed an agent's call to a friend losing its report in the chat, or leaving the friend's last words unanswered, when Enconvo restarted during the call.
- Fixed an agent's call report showing a friend's declined call as unanswered, and a card number the friend said now never reaches the chat.
- Changed an agent's calls so they reach only your friends and teammates, never people you only met on a call.
- Fixed a friend call's steps in the chat: a call that couldn't be placed no longer looks done, and the transcript now shows what people near your friend said, marked as not said to the agent.
- Fixed an agent's calls to friends recording the call while Settings › Agent Phone › Record calls was off; with it off, the agent no longer says the call is recorded.
- Fixed scheduled tasks below Full access messaging or calling friends and teammates they weren't set up to reach.
- Fixed a friend still being rung after the chat that placed the call was stopped, and a declined friend call showing as missed when the connection dropped while it rang.
- Changed an agent's calls to friends to keep no recording when the person called says they don't want the call recorded.
- Fixed calls from your iPhone being tried while notifications for Phone or FaceTime are off, which kept macOS from showing the call: Agent Phone now says so and offers the setup guide, and calls go through your phone provider meanwhile.
- Fixed an agent messaging or calling someone whose address only looked like the one it was given (eve@example.com.au for eve@example.com): a full address now reaches only that exact person.
- Removed the limits on how many calls your agent places and how often: calls to other people no longer wait 2 minutes apart or stop at 10 a day, calls to you no longer turn into a notification after 6 an hour or 20 a day, and calls to friends are no longer held to 10 a day on your Mac.
- Removed the remaining limits on calls and voice: calls your agent places no longer end after 10 minutes or hang up after 2 quiet minutes, a friend can be rung again right after declining, and Live Talk no longer caps a sentence at 2,000 characters or 60 seconds of audio; Enconvo Cloud's realtime voices no longer close a connection at 30 minutes or 20,000 characters.
- Fixed the note on a call that came as a notification to say why: your other call ran long.

## Friend Chat

- Added group chats to Friend Chat on the Mac: start a group from the + in the Friends sidebar, the Friends page or a chat's details, @mention people, see who has seen your message, and rename a group, change its picture, set its notifications, add or remove people, hand it on or leave it from its details, with group messages counted on the Dock icon.
- Added replies and emoji reactions to Friend Chat on the Mac: hover or right-click a message to react with a quick emoji or any emoji, or to reply with a quote, click a reaction to add or remove yours, and click a quote to jump to the original message; the Chat tab of a friend call shows the same quotes and reactions and lets you react and reply there too.
- Added group chats to Friend Chat in the iPhone app: start a group from the + in the Friends list, @mention people, see who has seen your message, and rename a group, change its picture, set its notifications, add or remove people, hand it on or leave it from its details.
- Added group chats to Friend Chat in the Android app: start a group from the + in the Friends list, @mention people, see who has seen your message, and rename a group, change its picture, set its notifications, add or remove people, hand it on or leave it from its details.
- Added replies and emoji reactions to Friend Chat in the iPhone app: hold a message to react with a quick emoji or any emoji, or to reply with a quote, tap a reaction to add or remove yours, hold it to see who reacted, and tap a quote to jump to the original message.
- Added replies and emoji reactions to Friend Chat in the Android app: hold a message to react or reply, tap a reaction to add or remove yours, and tap a quote to jump to the original message.
- Added an unread count on the Enconvo Dock icon for friend messages, which follows chats read on any device and leaves out muted chats.
- Changed the iPhone app's Friend Chat message bar to a lighter messenger look, with a flat text field on its own band and thin, evenly sized voice, emoji and + buttons.
- Fixed conference links tapped in a chat on iPhone opening the web join page, so they now join the call in the app.
- Fixed an agent calling a friend for you taking someone talking near them as their answer and moving on; it now keeps waiting, or asks again, until they answer it.
- Changed conference Notes so each person turns their own notes on and off and gets their own Doc, instead of the organizer's Notes turning them on for everyone and leaving nobody else in control.
- Fixed sharing a Doc for the first time, which failed and kept the Doc on this Mac, so call notes shared after a conference now open for everyone they were shared with.
- Fixed conference notes appearing under Earlier for people they couldn't be shared with.
- Added selecting several messages in a Friend Chat: choose Select from a message's menu or Select messages from the chat's menu, click or Shift-click to pick, then copy, forward, delete them together, or start a new agent chat with them in the Project you pick, with a message telling the agent what to do.

## Live Talk, Dictation and Voice

- Added a Translation Style to Dictation's Translate option, like the Translator's: Sound Native rewords what you said the way a native speaker would put it, and Custom follows a style you describe. Pick it in Settings › Dictation › Clean up › Translate.
- Added a Voice Clone tab to the ElevenLabs, Fish Audio, Gemini and Qwen3 voice providers in Settings › Text-to-Speech, where you can record or upload a voice to clone, play, use, rename or delete your cloned voices, and see how many Cloud voices you have left; providers that can clone voices show a badge in the list.
- Changed the Clean up chip on the Dictation card to show the chosen option's own icon, with its name on hover.
- Changed voice clone recording to stay on the clone page: the floating recording bar no longer appears, the page's level meter now moves with your voice, and the reading instruction is shown apart from the text to read.
- Fixed the hard edge that sometimes showed around the Dictation HUD: its outline now sits inside the pill and card, its glow fades out before the edge, and its shadow is no longer cut off.
- Fixed a voice clone recording staying on after the Settings window was closed: the take now stops and keeps what was recorded, a recording no longer starts if the window closes during its countdown, and a single take ends by itself after 5 minutes.
- Fixed Translate and Dictation's Translate sometimes leaving text in its own language instead of translating it, most often with Sound Native or a Custom style: text already in your target language now goes into your other language under every style.
- Fixed the agent failing to make speech with ElevenLabs when you have no ElevenLabs API key of your own; it now speaks through your Enconvo Cloud Plan, as Read Aloud does.
- Fixed replies read aloud keeping every spoken sentence on disk, which could add up to hundreds of megabytes; sentences are now removed a day after they play.
- Fixed Microsoft Speech credentials offering no region to choose, and Microsoft text-to-speech with your own key ignoring the region you picked.
- Added quick tools to Live Talk calls: the assistant can now search your Memory and past chats, save something you ask it to remember, read your Calendar and Reminders, search your mail, and use read-only tools of your MCP servers right in the call, without starting a task.
- Changed Live Talk's updates during a long task: they now tell what the task has found so far, not only what it is doing.
- Fixed slower replies in the first turns of a Live Talk call.
- Fixed Live Talk remembering the whole sentence it was cut off in when you talk over it with a Mac or Gemini voice; it now remembers only what you heard.
- Added a quick way to turn a dictation into a Draft: while dictating, tap ⇧ (your Draft modifier) once, even after letting go of the Dictation key, and what you said so far becomes the Draft's instruction.
- Added Send app context and Include a screenshot to Settings › Dictation › Draft: a Draft now reads the app you're in, its window title, web address and visible text, and optionally a screenshot of its window, so the draft fits what you're working on.
- Added Remove Filler Words to Translator and Fix Spelling & Grammar, on by default: dictated text loses its ums, ahs, stutters and false starts, so translations and corrections read like written text.
- Changed Dictation to paste sooner after you stop: a short dictation no longer waits on the snippet check unless its words are the name of one of your snippets, and the text field is read while the last words are finished.
- Fixed Voice Clone recording so pressing Record goes straight to the reading screen and stays there through the countdown, the microphone starting and saving, instead of flashing back to the upload screen; the countdown can now be canceled, and the same holds for Gemini's consent recording and the Qwen3 clone dialog.
- Fixed the Live Talk voice menu listing an old or empty set of xAI voices when Speak uses xAI on Enconvo Cloud: it now lists the voices that provider offers, as its Settings page does.
- Fixed Dictation saying "No text field found" after the text was pasted into a web page or a remote screen: Dictation now pastes wherever the focus is, without the notice.
- Fixed Live Talk calls taking about ten seconds longer to connect when Fish Audio can't be reached from your network.
- Added a way to quiet Live Talk and Voice Command: tell her to be quiet or to stop talking for a while and she answers nothing and holds her news until you call her by name, say she can talk again, type to her, or click the QUIET status.
- Fixed Read Aloud waiting several seconds per sentence before reporting that the voice's provider has no API key: it now stops at the first sentence and says so.
- Added ElevenLabs Scribe v3 and Scribe v3 Live: choose them for Dictation, Voice Input and file transcription with your ElevenLabs API key or Enconvo Cloud. Scribe v3 Live shows the first words in under a second, about twice as fast as v2 Realtime, with fewer errors in Chinese and noisy audio; Chinese and Japanese come out without spaces between words.
- Live Talk on the Mac, iPhone and Android now hears you with ElevenLabs Scribe v3 Live when it uses ElevenLabs: your words show about a second sooner, Chinese in a noisy room comes through more accurately, and Korean keeps the spaces between its words.

## Avatar and Live Photo Characters

- Added Live Photo characters to Settings › Avatar: open the Live Photo tab and choose Create to turn a portrait, or one made from a description you type, into an Avatar character that stands and breathes on your desktop in a rounded frame with the photo's own background, or cut out if you choose Remove under Backdrop, and appears as live video in calls.

## Paired Computers and SSH Hosts

- Added Agent control for paired computers: a new switch under Settings › Connections lets another computer's agents use this Mac's screen and browser directly, such as to retest a change right away, while an overlay here names who is in control and lets you take over or stop it.
- Added a strip at the top of the chat and a pill above the composer while an agent uses another computer, showing the latest screenshot from there and a Stop button, and the finished run shows as one card with the steps and screenshots.
- Added your paired computers and SSH hosts to the composer's @ menu, each machine listed once under Computers, so you can @ one and the agent does the work there, running commands over SSH or handing the task to that computer's own agent.
- Added SSH hosts in Settings › Connections: add a Host from your SSH config or type user@host, test it, and agents run commands there over your own ssh with nothing installed, asking before each command as the chat's Permission mode says or every time, with the host shown on each command step and approval in chat.
- Added letting agents run commands on another of your paired computers when that computer allows Terminal for this Mac, asking first as the chat's Permission mode says, with Agents and Commands switches on each computer's page in Settings › Connections.
- Changed Settings › Connections into three lists, Phones, Computers and SSH hosts, where each phone and computer opens its own page with what it can do, and a Who can reach this Mac row with Stop all access to cut every phone and computer off at once.
- Added an app of its own for each paired computer on the Mac: Open in its own window shows that computer's chats in a window with its own Dock icon in the computer's color, and Keep in Dock in its ⋯ menu keeps the icon there to open it later; the Settings window also gets its own Dock icon while it is open.
- Added a choice of where each paired computer opens, in the Chat Window or in its own window, and a shortcut that brings it up from any app on the Mac, both under How it opens here in Settings › Connections and in the computer's ⋯ menu.
- Added a short guide after pairing a computer that sets where it opens (in the Chat Window or its own window), gives it a shortcut that brings it up from any app, and shows what you can do on it; open it again any time from the computer's ⋯ menu › How to use.
- Changed Pair a computer so it always shows which computer controls and which is controlled, can be started from the computer you're at by asking the other one to allow it, lets you choose what the other computer can reach while allowing it, and on the Mac opens the newly paired computer in the Chat Window instead of a separate window.
- Changed Settings › Connections to list each paired computer once with which way it controls, and Open on a computer there now opens it in the Chat Window on the Mac.
- Changed a request from another computer to pair with your Mac to arrive as a notification with Show and Not now, instead of opening the Chat Window on its own; Not now tells the other computer right away.
- Added a note on your Mac after another computer pairs with it, saying what that computer can use here, with Change to adjust it in Settings › Connections.
- Fixed a request to pair from another computer staying on your Mac after that computer quit or lost its connection.
- Fixed Remote on a Mac using another Mac failing some requests during a burst of activity, which now wait their turn instead.
- Fixed handing a task to another Mac waiting silently while that Mac asked for approval; the chat now says the other computer waits for approval on its own screen, and what for.
- Fixed Ask for approval asking before an agent reads how to use your other computers, and the approval for handing a task to another computer showing only a route; it now names the computer and the task.
- Fixed approvals for work on another paired computer showing the computer's id instead of its name, in the card and in what Allow for this chat covers.
- Fixed testing an SSH host on your local network telling you to check its address when macOS was holding the connection for its Local Network permission; it now says to allow Local Network for Enconvo.
- Fixed the composer's first @ menu after Enconvo starts showing no Computers.
- Fixed a second SSH host for a computer you already reach over SSH taking over that computer's entry and listing it twice; the first host stays linked, and removing it links the other one.
- Fixed turning off Commands on a computer's page leaving its SSH host free to run commands.
- Fixed a wrong SSH password or passphrase reopening its box with no word that it didn't work.
- Fixed the Computers list in Settings › Connections showing what a computer allows from when the page opened, until you opened it again.
- Fixed a computer with two saved SSH hosts showing the wrong one on its page, so Before each command and Remove SSH access changed a host other than the one in use.
- Changed the Allow Local Network card of an SSH host test to open System Settings at Privacy & Security, where Local Network is.
- Changed Pair a computer's Chats and tools choice to say that the other computer's agents can then run commands on this one.
- Fixed an SSH host you just added missing from the composer's @ menu for a few seconds: the @ menu now lists it right away.
- Fixed a chat started with an @ SSH host or computer showing "$name" in its title: the title now reads the machine by its plain name, as its chip does.
- Fixed a command an agent ran on an SSH host or another computer being summed up as "Used a tool" in the chat: the line now says "Ran a command", and each step still names the machine.
- Changed how paired computers show in the Chat Window: the sidebar now lists one computer at a time, and a device switcher at its foot switches between this computer and the paired ones, shows what is waiting on the others, and opens any of them in its own window.
- Added Add a device to the sidebar's device switcher: pair a computer, add an SSH host or pair a phone from there, also before anything is paired.

## Knowledge, Plugins and Connectors

- Added Team Knowledge Bases: share a knowledge base with people by email or with your whole Team as Can view, Can add or Can manage; what anyone adds reaches every Mac within seconds, shows who added it, and is searched on each Mac from its own copy.
- Changed adding a website to a knowledge base to keep to the section of the site its address points into, such as one part of a documentation site, and to index a page found under two addresses once.
- Fixed a web page the agent added to a knowledge base sometimes staying in Processing forever; it now loads as a web page, and anything that can't be read shows as failed.
- Fixed the agent sometimes reporting knowledge base items as deleted when they weren't; it now hears which ones were not found.
- Fixed an agent asked to add a file, web page or text to a knowledge base not finding how to, so it gave up instead of saving it.
- Fixed adding a website to a knowledge base from an address that redirects, such as one without its trailing slash, missing the site's pages.
- Added several accounts to Connectors that sign in on your Mac, such as Notion, Webflow and Linear: connect another account from the Connector's page, pick the default, disconnect one, and name an account in a request so the agent uses that one.
- Added a default account per Workspace for every Connector, so in a client's Workspace the agent uses that client's account unless you name another.
- Changed the Shopify plugin to work across several stores: the agent asks which store when you don't name one, names the store in every change it shows, and runs a change meant for several stores once per store.
- Changed Expo into a Plugin Store plugin with the Expo team's official skills and the Expo MCP Server, replacing the read-only Expo Connector, so the agent builds, upgrades and ships Expo and React Native apps from Expo's own guidance, reads your EAS builds, logs, crashes and reviews, and starts builds and submissions only after you approve each one; an Expo sign-in you already made carries over.
- Fixed an updated Connector that runs on your Mac keeping its old version and tools until Enconvo restarted.
- Fixed a plugin you uninstalled coming back when an automatic update was checking for new versions at the same time.
- Fixed MCP servers imported from a Codex config losing the environment variables they pass through and their working folder.
- Fixed an auto-syncing Knowledge Base folder on a drive that wasn't connected when Enconvo started never syncing again once the drive was back.
- Fixed agents loading a folder of documents getting an error instead of the files in it.
- Fixed adding a document by a file://localhost link failing to read it.
- Fixed adding a document whose file name contains "#" sometimes reading a different file.
- Changed loading a folder with more than 5,000 documents into one request to say so instead of failing.
- Fixed loading a folder into a knowledge base skipping subfolders it couldn't read without saying so.
- Fixed Create Knowledge Base in the menu bar menu opening the Knowledge Base page without its New Knowledge Base dialog: the dialog now opens over the knowledge base you last had open.
- Fixed reconnecting a Connector whose sign-in had expired: the Connect card in a chat and the Plugins page now show it connected as soon as you allow access, instead of waiting until the wait runs out.

## Automations

- Added GitHub triggers to Automations: an automation can run when an issue or pull request is opened, an issue is labeled, a comment mentions you, CI fails, or a release is published in a repository you connect, only for the people you choose, with events kept sealed for your Mac until you allow outside triggers there.
- Added Reply on GitHub to an automation's Then options: after a run, Enconvo asks you before posting its reply as a comment on the issue or pull request that started it.
- Added Add to Ideas to Automations: each GitHub issue, Discord or Telegram message, email or webhook an Automation hears becomes an idea on a Project's Ideas board, where its lead can reproduce a bug and plan the fix, or research a request and close it with a reason or plan it with designs, then asks you before anything changes.
- Added a Time limit to each Automation's Advanced Settings, from 15 minutes to 8 hours, so long agent runs are no longer stopped at 30 minutes, and a run that hits its limit can be given more time and run again in one click.
- Fixed Automation chats missing from chat history: each Automation's latest run now shows in Chats with a clock mark as soon as it starts, and its earlier runs are one click away in the run's chat.
- Fixed the iPhone getting only one of two Automation results when Automations in different Workspaces finished runs with the same number.

## Image and Video Artifacts

- Added Image and Video artifacts: click an image or video an agent made in chat to keep editing it beside the chat, with each model's own settings (resolution, aspect ratio, variations, GIF, masks, video length and frames), a version for every edit, and every generated picture and clip collected in the Artifacts library and in ~/Enconvo/Media.
- Fixed OpenAI image generation with a ChatGPT sign-in offering GPT Image 2.5 and 2.6, which OpenAI doesn't serve to ChatGPT plans; it now shows your plan's image model and says to use an API key or Enconvo Cloud for GPT Image 2.6.

## Browser Use and Computer Use

- Added full-page screenshots to Browser Use: ask the agent to capture a whole web page and it saves it as a PNG file where you want it, at the page's own resolution and without the agent's cursor.
- Fixed subagents that use Computer Use in different apps waiting for each other: each now acts in its own app at the same time, and only agents working in the same app take turns.
- Fixed the browser agent failing to type into or press keys in a field that the page swaps for a new one once used, such as Wikipedia's search box; it now finds the same field again and acts on it.
- Fixed the browser agent seeing nothing on pages built from frames (a frameset), so it can now read and use what each frame shows.
- Fixed the browser agent sometimes pressing two buttons at once when another button covered the one it meant; it now stops and says what is in the way.
- Fixed Browser Use missing a file that a newly opened tab downloaded when it was the chat's only browser tab, which made the agent think the page had failed to load.
- Fixed Browser Use telling the agent that a click on a download link did nothing, so the agent searched for the file or said the download failed.
- Fixed Browser Use failing to press keys on, read the value of, or focus an element it had found with Find, such as pressing Enter in a search box.
- Fixed Browser Use not finding a checkbox or field by a label that wraps it, or the page's buttons, links and checkboxes by their role.
- Fixed Browser Use waiting far longer than asked when a wait was given in milliseconds; a fixed wait is now at most a minute.
- Fixed agents reporting no results when a website command landed on a sign-in or a human check; they now see the page and can hand the tab to you.
- Fixed Browser Use hiding ordinary file names and links from the agent, such as a name with a date like 20261026 or a date and a time, so the agent couldn't tell you which file it picked.
- Fixed agents reading a Wikipedia article's long list of other languages before the article itself, which made them fetch the page several times to reach a table near its start.
- Fixed Browser Use telling the agent that a click had not worked when it brought up a JavaScript alert or confirm box, so the agent could report a working step as failed.
- Fixed Browser Use closing a website's dropdown menu or picker right after opening it, so agents couldn't open menus built with common web toolkits.
- Fixed agents reading a spreadsheet with several sheets as a single sheet, so a table or note on a later sheet looked like the last rows of the first.
- Fixed Browser Use reporting a correct choice in a pop-up dropdown, such as a Material UI or shadcn select, as a click on the neighbouring option, and not telling the agent which option such a dropdown shows.
- Fixed Browser Use sometimes clicking the option next to the one asked for while a dropdown menu was still sliding open.
- Fixed Computer Use telling the agent a key press did nothing in apps that draw their own content, such as games and drawing canvases, so it stopped or repeated keys that had worked.
- Fixed Browser Use and Computer Use workflows rejecting a typing step named type_text, which made the agent try again.
- Fixed Browser Use and Computer Use agents unchecking a checkbox that was already checked when asked to check it: they now set checkboxes, radio buttons and switches to the asked state instead of clicking them.
- Fixed Browser Use agents failing to fill a dropdown on a web page and having to try again: filling a dropdown now picks the option.
- Fixed Browser Use agents sending a web form without its file when they clicked the file field during a series of steps: they now attach the file first.
- Fixed Browser Use agents waiting on a page that asks for a password as if it were still loading when its address held a username and password: they now look for the browser's sign-in prompt.
- Fixed Computer Use clicks on buttons that a page shows only on hover, which often did nothing the first time.
- Fixed Computer Use sometimes refusing a click in Enconvo's own windows with a note that the window had changed.
- Fixed Computer Use reporting a click in Enconvo's own windows as done when nothing was clicked.

## App Sidebar

- Added a page card to App Sidebar beside a browser: it shows the page you're on with what can be read, such as a video's length, captions and chapters, an article's length even on older page layouts or a PDF's pages, and reads the page ahead so the first answer starts sooner.
- Added built-in profiles for YouTube, Bilibili, GitHub, Reddit, Hacker News, X, Amazon, Gmail, Outlook and Stack Overflow in App Sidebar, each with starters and answers that respect the available page content, including video chapters you can click to jump to.
- Added built-in App Sidebar profiles for Finder, chat apps including WeCom and Teams, git clients including SourceTree, code editors including Windsurf, terminals, notes apps, Pages and Word, with starters for each and an Insert button that puts a reply, commit message or command into an identified text field without sending or running it, with clear guidance when insertion is unavailable.
- Added file jobs for Finder in App Sidebar: compress, convert and resize images, compress PDFs, convert video and audio, zip and unzip, and batch rename the selected files, with smaller PNG copies, clear unsupported-format messages and starters that follow your selection, each shown in a card with before and after sizes and Reveal in Finder; your originals stay untouched.
- Added per-site settings to App Sidebar's site pill: turn page reading on or off, choose the answer language and summary style, add your own instructions, and edit the starters.
- Added Transcribe the audio for YouTube videos without captions in App Sidebar: when you pick it, Enconvo transcribes the video's audio and summarizes it with timestamps.
- Added a Now on row to App Sidebar when you move to another page during a chat, so you can ask about the new page in one click.
- Added a card in App Sidebar beside Chrome, Edge, Brave, Arc and other Chromium browsers without the Enconvo Companion extension, with an Install button; once the extension connects, the card turns into the page card and clears its setup reminder.
- Changed the App Sidebar handle to stay hidden until you move the pointer to the right edge of an app window, with a short guide the first time; turn off "Show handle only at window edge" in Settings or the handle's menu to keep it always visible.
- Changed the App Sidebar handle's hover card to show the page you're on, such as a video's length and captions.
- Changed App Sidebar to read pages without the browser's debugger, so the "started debugging this browser" bar no longer appears while it reads your tab.
- Fixed App Sidebar opening a second tab with the same page when you asked about the page you had open, and the original YouTube tab sometimes going dark after a summary; your tab is now read as it is and never reloaded or changed.
- Fixed App Sidebar showing an update prompt when an older Enconvo Companion cannot read the page.
- Changed the App Sidebar handle to show and hide the moment the pointer reaches or leaves a window's right edge, without a delay or a fade.
- Fixed choosing an item in the App Sidebar handle's right-click menu taking focus away from the app you were working in.
- Fixed the App Sidebar handle on the Finder desktop: it now appears there with or without Finder windows open, follows switches between Finder windows, and stays on screen after Show Desktop.
- Fixed the App Sidebar handle and its preview staying on screen over Quick Look, Finder dialogs, or a minimized, hidden, or closed window, and kept the first-use tip from covering the handle on narrow windows.
- Fixed the App Sidebar showing an empty page beside an app installed while Enconvo was running, such as a browser copied in from a download: it now shows the app, its Conversation starters and, beside a browser, the page card.
- Fixed App Sidebar's key moments for a YouTube video whose caption track runs past the video's end: the transcript now stops at the video's length, so the timestamps stay within the video.
- Fixed Finder file jobs in App Sidebar answering without file cards when the files tool wasn't available: the answer now shows each new copy as a file card.
- Fixed times in an App Sidebar timeline about a video so each one jumps the video to that moment, as a cited time in the answer does.

## Models and Providers

- Changed Claude models that take up to 1M tokens, including on Enconvo Cloud, to start at a 500K context, so long chats compact sooner and cost less; you can still raise it per model.
- Added Claude Fable 5.2, Anthropic's most capable model, on Enconvo Cloud and for your own Anthropic API key and Claude subscription, with a context of up to 1M tokens (500K by default), reasoning effort from Low to Max, and Fast mode on Anthropic's API.
- Added Gemini 4 Carbon: choose it in Enconvo Cloud (at its launch price, half the standard rate through November 30), with your own Gemini API key on a paid tier, or in Google Antigravity on a Google AI Ultra plan. Its thinking levels are Low, Medium (default) and High.
- AI Commands now run on Enconvo Fast by default (Settings › AI Commands › All Commands): writing tools, the PopBar, and Dictation's Polish and Translate use it unless you picked another model, and Use Recommended switches back to it.
- Improved the native model picker on macOS: a click on a row lands on the first press even when the picker's panel isn't the key window, it opens with the providers, sign-ins and usage it last showed instead of loading them again, the account card keeps its height while usage is read so the models under it don't move, and an arrow key or a pick redraws only the rows it changes.
- Added free limit resets to Claude and ChatGPT subscription usage in Settings: the card shows how many resets Claude or Codex granted you and until when, and Use reset refills your limits after you confirm.
- Added the time so far beside “Thinking…” while a model thinks, and fixed OpenAI models showing “Thought for 1s” while they were still thinking, with the parts of their reasoning run together on one line: the parts now make one thinking step, each in its own paragraph, timed from start to finish.
- Added each model's own limit to the model picker's account card: a Claude subscription now shows what is left of the weekly Fable limit beside the 5-hour and weekly ones.
- Added the weekly Fable limit to the native model picker's account card on macOS: a Claude subscription shows it beside the 5-hour and weekly limits, as any model's own limit does.
- Added tags to the models in the Chat Window's model picker: a Cloud model can show New (for its first 14 days), a discount such as 50% OFF, Free, Preview or the date it leaves, with the price behind a discount when you point at the row or reach it with the arrow keys. Enconvo Cloud decides the tags, so they change without an app update.

## Chat Window, Dynamic Island and Settings

- Changed the iPhone switch in Mail Alerts settings to Phone, since alerts also reach a paired Android phone.
- Changed the Dynamic Island to hide its resting pill over full-screen apps again by default, so a pill such as "Result ready" no longer covers and blocks the top of a full-screen app; only a window the app itself reports as full screen counts, so a maximized window leaves it alone, and opening the island or talking to it there still brings it up.
- Changed lifetime licenses to show as Standard and Premium everywhere in the app, matching the website and your purchase, so they no longer share the Plus and Pro names of the Enconvo Cloud plans; the free trial is now the Standard trial.
- Fixed Enconvo Cloud web search failing around the moment the monthly free search quota runs out; those searches now switch to the backup search provider.
- Fixed a plugin skill you deleted still showing up in search after you rebuilt the plugin once.
- Fixed What matters to you in Automations' Important mail opening an empty Settings page; it now opens the Mail Alerts settings.
- Fixed the message for a plugin that can't be installed on this computer naming it by its internal name instead of its title.
- Fixed the voice play button in Settings having no name, so it now shows a Preview voice tooltip and screen readers can find it.
- Fixed a plugin you uninstalled showing as installed on the Plugins page after its folder came back, which also made Settings search say some settings could not be loaded.
- Fixed the Credential Management list in Settings still showing a credential as Not configured after you entered its key, until the page was reopened.
- Fixed the Chat Window's sidebar in a narrow window staying open over the chat after you picked a chat or a page from it, and gave it its own button to close it.
- Fixed choosing a folder that already has a project for a new chat doing nothing, or saying the project already exists; it now opens that project.
- Fixed Compress Audio making M4A and OGG files larger, turning every MP3 into the lowest quality, and failing on M4A files with cover art; files now shrink by the quality you pick and keep their cover.
- Fixed spreadsheets written by scripts losing their formula cells when read into a chat or a knowledge base; a formula that was never calculated now reads as written.
- Fixed Image Compress refusing a folder; the images in a folder and its subfolders are now compressed, in the same layout.
- Fixed Dynamic Island settings showing Normal for "Fold when you move away" before you picked one, though the island folds after half a second; Quickly now means that half second.
- Fixed the Chat Window's message box in a narrow window cutting off the chosen model's name: the points, then the model and then the mic now step aside one at a time, and model names in the model picker stay on one line.
- Fixed the Chat Window downloading an unused audio file every time it opened.
- Fixed Bookmark Search missing Firefox and Zen profiles that were created in a folder of your choosing.
- Fixed a Settings link to a plugin that isn't installed showing an empty page: it now says the plugin isn't installed on this computer.
- Fixed the Dynamic Island suggesting "Type ?" and math expressions in its input, which only work in the Smart Bar.
- Fixed typing in the Clipboard History, Bookmark Search and File Search lists so the results keep up with fast typing, and made Chinese searches in Clipboard History faster.
- Fixed Connectors that are switched on sometimes showing as off when many Connectors are installed.
- Fixed tasks asked for in a call about a chat with no window, such as a plugin development chat, finishing without a notification.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing for a moment when reading selected text from an app that answers slowly, which now copies through that app's Copy menu without holding Enconvo up.
- Fixed clicking Paste under a Smart Bar answer again while the first click was still being handled pasting the answer twice.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing for a moment after a copy while Clipboard History is on and the system clipboard answers slowly.
- Fixed Enconvo quitting at launch when it couldn't create its extension folder; it now says so once and keeps running.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing for a moment when reading selected text while another app's copy is still on the clipboard and that app answers slowly.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing for a moment at launch while the Dynamic Island checked whether it may use your location for weather.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing for a moment while the permission window checked Automation and Location access.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing now and then while the disk was slow, from Quick Capture rereading its settings every second.
- Fixed the Smart Bar showing the Automations section title and the Automation tag in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed Connector errors on the Plugins page that could show an empty message instead of explaining what went wrong.
- Added a Pull request tab to the chat side panel (⌃⇧P): see the branch's pull request on GitHub with its checks, review and comments, or create one from a Worktree chat.
- Added a Device tab to the chat side panel (⌃⇧M): watch and control an iOS Simulator or Android Emulator next to the chat, after a one-time setup.
- Added Keep in Dock and Show in Menu Bar to a Space's menu in the Chat Window sidebar: the Space gets its own icon in the Dock that opens it in a window of its own, or its own menu bar icon that drops it down, both with Ask about.
- Added Contrast and Chat Width to Settings › General: make borders, dividers and secondary text stronger or softer across Enconvo, and let messages and the composer grow wider in a large window.
- Changed the Agent overlay for Computer Use and Browser Use: a light wave now ripples out from each click and gently bends the window beneath it, a soft light runs around the window edge while the agent works, and the dot grid lights up only around the agent cursor.
- Fixed the Chat Window's chat list sometimes showing a finished chat as still running when the Dynamic Island or Smart Bar was open too.
- Fixed the send button showing an X after a message couldn't be sent, which looked like a stop button that never went away.
- Fixed the first message of a new chat disappearing when it couldn't be sent, such as when the chat's folder no longer exists; it now stays, with the reason under it.
- Changed the Dynamic Island's task list so chats that need your approval, failed, or have a result ready show there too; it opens in place of the input, lets you allow or decline an approval right in its row, and opens other chats in the window they started in.
- Added Open in Chat Window to the Dynamic Island's top bar before a chat starts too, so it opens a new chat in the Chat Window and takes along what you've typed.
- Fixed the Dynamic Island showing Result ready when a task had failed or was waiting for your approval.
- Fixed the Dynamic Island's Sessions list showing a chat that finished while you were away as read until the list loaded again.
- Changed the App Sidebar handle to appear as soon as the pointer comes near a window's right edge, across a wider area down the whole window, instead of only at the very edge.
- Fixed the App Sidebar handle sometimes appearing behind its window or past the top of the screen, and its right-click menu taking focus or staying open after Escape or an app switch.
- Fixed the Chat Window's title showing Agent for an AI Command's chat, which now shows the command's name and icon.
- Fixed an agent's commands running without its Python environment after the environment had to be rebuilt, and two first commands at once making the environment twice.
- Fixed commands waiting on a stalled uv download even when their Python environment was already there, and Python environments in folders with a space in their name not activating.
- Fixed the first command an agent ran on a new install running without its Python environment, so pip and python went to the system Python until the next command.
- Fixed clicks and keys in the live view of a browser tab an agent handed to you being refused, so you can sign in there and press Return to agent again.
- Fixed Show in Finder and Open doing nothing for files whose names contain quotes, dollar signs, backticks, or parentheses, and image compression failing on a file whose name starts with a dash.
- Fixed Enconvo Cloud requests from a long-running command still using your previous sign-in after you signed out.
- Fixed a step waiting for a background tool reading "Waiting for Wait for …".
- Fixed a message sent while a task runs appearing several times when the app was slow to respond.
- Changed a message typed in the Smart Bar or Dynamic Island while a task runs to show right away.
- Changed the Dynamic Island so two or more tasks fold into one Tasks chip, which no longer shows a spinner of its own while its list is open.
- Changed Account and Settings in the Chat Window sidebar to open the Settings window again, instead of covering the Chat Window.
- Changed the composer's response style to be picked only from the + menu's Use style, which now also names the Intelligent UI level; the chip beside the other controls is gone.
- Changed the Chat Window side panel to be open or closed per chat: opening it in one chat no longer opens it in the others, and each chat comes back the way you left it.
- Changed Settings and the help menu at the foot of the Chat Window sidebar into one menu.
- Fixed Enconvo keeping a copy of every AI request, with your messages and the system prompt, in its log folder on your Mac: these request logs are no longer written, and the copies earlier versions left are deleted.
- Fixed the Chat Window writing the start of what you typed into a diagnostics log each time you pressed Return.
- Fixed the Create Agent dialog showing its field names, hints and heading in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the new chat heading ("What can I help with?") showing in English when the app language is set to another language.
- Fixed the "Thinking…" and "Running" status shown while an answer starts showing in English when the app language is set to another language.
- Added chats that need you to the Dock icon's badge: a chat with a result or a failure you haven't opened yet, or one waiting for your approval or answer, now counts there, alongside unread messages from friends.
- Changed the side panel of a chat that never opened it to start on a New tab instead of Files; the first thing opened there takes its place.
- Changed Video Conference in the Chat Window sidebar to scroll with Plugins and the other pages; only New chat and Search stay fixed above the list.
- Fixed the chat box keeping only the first character of text another app typed into it all at once, such as an automation or a text expander, when the box was empty or all its text was selected.
- Removed the "Running outside the sandbox" and "Ran outside the sandbox" lines under a command in a chat: a command you allowed to run outside the Sandbox now shows like any other.
- Improved editing an earlier message in a chat: its images and files come back into the message box with its text, so you can remove one or add another before sending, and Cancel takes them out again.
- Fixed pasting from Clipboard History when it was opened over one of Enconvo's own windows: Return now pastes into the Chat Window's message box instead of sometimes closing the Chat Window and pasting nothing.
- Changed Clipboard History's Copy shortcut from Command-Return to Command-C. Command-C still copies the text you selected in the search field or in an item's preview.
- Removed the Tasks chip (such as "Tasks 6 · Failed") from above the message box while a chat is open in the Dynamic Island. Home without a chat open and the notch still show your tasks.
- Fixed settings such as Model Name, Reasoning Effort, the provider fields, and the Smart Bar, PopBar, Dynamic Island, Pet, Dictation and Voice Command options showing in English when the app language is set to another language.
- Fixed the Chat Window sidebar still offering Log in after signing in on the website: a card above the device now follows the sign-in from the browser until your account loads, says when the network is slow, and offers Retry if the account doesn't load, while the Settings menu shows the sign-in finishing instead of Log in.
- Fixed Enconvo ignoring the system proxy with Use System Network Proxy Preferences on: model requests, web fetches, Connectors and the other requests its engine makes now go through the proxy, so Claude models no longer answer "not available in your country or region" when your proxy is in a supported country. A proxy you turn on, change or turn off while Enconvo runs now takes effect without restarting it. Servers on this Mac and your local network, such as LM Studio and Ollama, are still reached directly.

## Links, Command Line and Local API

- Added `files=` to Enconvo links, which attaches the files at those paths to the Agent or AI Command, so images can be sent from another app or script.
- Added `enconvo ai-command run` and the local API route `ai-command/run`, which run an AI Command in the background with text and files and return its answer.
- Added a Link, CLI and API row to each AI Command's and Agent's settings, which shows the deeplink, `enconvo` command and local API call that run it with your text and files, ready to copy.
- Fixed the enconvo command line failing to call an agent with `enconvo agent call` or `enconvo agent/call` unless the route was typed as `enconvo agent call index`.
- Fixed writing commands such as Translate, Summarize and Convert to List failing from the enconvo command line and the local API with "load command failed."; they now take the text as an argument (`enconvo writing_package convert_to_list "…"`) and answer without adding a chat to your history.
- Fixed `enconvo agent main` on the command line printing no answer while the agent was busy in its latest chat: it now answers in a new chat.
- Fixed `enconvo agent call "…"` on the command line failing as an unknown command: a message after `call` now goes to the agent, as it already did with `enconvo agent/call "…"`.
- Fixed Enconvo links opening the PopBar's small Smart Bar and doing nothing when that was the last panel used; a link now runs its Agent or AI Command in a new chat in the Smart Bar, or in the Chat Window or Dynamic Island when it names one with `window=`.
- Fixed opening an Enconvo link bringing the Settings window up over other apps.
- Fixed the Chat Window's title showing Agent for an AI Command's chat, which now shows the command's name and icon.
- Added `files=` to Enconvo links, which attaches the files at those paths to the Agent or AI Command, so images can be sent from another app or script.
- Added `enconvo ai-command run` and the local API route `ai-command/run`, which run an AI Command in the background with text and files and return its answer.
- Added a Link, CLI and API row to each AI Command's and Agent's settings, which shows the deeplink, `enconvo` command and local API call that run it with your text and files, ready to copy.
- Fixed Enconvo links opening the PopBar's small Smart Bar and doing nothing when that was the last panel used; a link now runs its Agent or AI Command in a new chat in the Smart Bar, or in the Chat Window or Dynamic Island when it names one with `window=`.
- Fixed opening an Enconvo link bringing the Settings window up over other apps.
- Fixed a hotkey saved with short modifier names, such as "ctrl+alt+cmd+t" from the Local API or an Agent, registering its bare key: every T typed in any app opened the command. Short names (cmd, ctrl, alt, opt, fn) and the modifier symbols are now read as modifiers, a hotkey with a word Enconvo does not know is not registered, and a key that types is never registered as a hotkey without Command, Control, Option or Function.

## Discord Bots

- Fixed a Discord bot that requires approval letting a member who wasn't approved slip a message in alongside an approved member's, and fixed /stop, /new and the other bot commands typed in Discord going to the agent instead of being run.
- Fixed a message sent to a Discord bot just before /stop starting after the stop, a bot command sent with an attachment going to the agent, and a Discord bot you turned off still answering a message whose attachments were downloading.
- Fixed a Discord bot turned off and on again quickly connecting twice, and a message with attachments sent just before /stop or /new still starting a reply.
- Fixed a Discord bot staying offline after its connection went silent, instead of reconnecting.

## iPhone and Android

- Added Reply to the phone notification for a finished reply, so you can answer the chat from the notification without opening the app.
- Added a direct connection between the iPhone or Android app and your Mac when they're on the same network, so the app connects and answers faster at home or at the office; elsewhere it still connects through the internet.
- Added sending in Mac chats on the iPhone and in the Android app while your Mac is offline or still reconnecting: your phone answers what it can, such as questions and web lookups, and messages that need your Mac are sent to it by themselves once it connects.
- Changed the notification your phone gets when a followed chat's reply finishes to show the start of the reply instead of "Reply ready", so you can see what came back before opening it.
- Changed the iPhone and Android apps to show no notification for a finished reply while the app is open: the chat list shows it instead.
- Changed finished replies so your phone isn't notified while you're using your Mac, unless you sent that message from the phone.
- Changed the Mac's notification for a finished chat to show the start of the reply instead of "Task completed".
- Changed the iPhone and Android apps to connect to your Mac sooner when you open them after a while away.
- Changed the iPhone app to load your Mac's chat list faster when it connects through the internet: the Mac now compresses what it sends, and the app asks for the list as soon as it connects.
- Changed the Android app to load faster when it connects to your Mac through the internet: the Mac now compresses what it sends to it, as it does for the iPhone.
- Changed the iPhone and Android apps to show your Mac's chats sooner when you come back to the app: the phone asks for them while it reconnects instead of waiting for the Mac to answer first, and in the Android app a Mac that was online keeps showing Online meanwhile.
- Changed the chat on iPhone and Android to use narrower side margins, so messages use more of the screen width.
- Fixed Mac chats on the iPhone and in the Android app showing the old message list with a Release Mac: the Mac now gives the phone its chat page, so they look and work as they do on the Mac.
- Fixed a video or other file an agent names by its title, such as a downloaded YouTube video, opening on the iPhone and in the Android app as an unknown document that wouldn't play.
- Fixed a chat's notification in the Android app leaving its last reply in the notification shade after you answer or reply from it.
- Fixed pages from one paired Mac being able to read what another Mac's pages kept on the iPhone or Android phone; each Mac's pages now keep their own storage.
- Fixed file fields in an app opened beside a chat in the Android app doing nothing.
- Fixed buttons in a Space or app pressed right as the iPhone or Android app opens failing before the app reached your Mac; they now wait for the connection.
- Fixed the Android app's Mac pages, such as Home's agent and model, failing to load when you come back to the app: they now wait for your Mac to reconnect, as on the iPhone.
- Fixed a Mac chat you deleted coming back as New Chat when your phone later sent a message into it; the phone now says it couldn't send.
- Fixed the iPhone and Android apps staying disconnected after remote access was turned back on while the app was open: they now reconnect within about 30 seconds, and on Android a Mac chat takes no typing while remote access is off, as on the iPhone.
- Fixed the chat on iPhone and Android not showing what was said during a Live Talk call until the chat was opened again.
- Fixed Live Talk calls on iPhone and Android showing what was said in the chat in bursts every second or two instead of word by word as it is heard and spoken.
- Fixed the chat on iPhone and Android dropping back down when a reply finishes; the message you sent now stays at the top with the room below it, as on the Mac.
- Added hints to Mac Screen's Trackpad mode on the iPhone that teach the gestures as you use them; they move to the top of the picture while the pointer is near its bottom, and while they show, the first tap far from a pointer you haven't moved yet doesn't click.
- Added a keyboard button to Mac Screen's floating window over a Live Talk call on the iPhone: type on your Mac with the same key bar as full screen, with the window moving up so the picture stays visible above the keyboard.
- Changed Mac Screen's floating window over a Live Talk call on the iPhone to work like a trackpad by default: slide anywhere on the picture to move the pointer and tap to click where the pointer is, and a mode chip in the window's bar switches between Trackpad, Touch and Just Watch.
- Changed Mac Screen's Direct mode on the iPhone to be called Touch, and full screen's mode button now shows the mode's name; one tap switches between Trackpad and Touch.
- Changed Mac Screen's pointer in Trackpad mode on the iPhone to be larger with a soft halo, with a disc where your finger is and a ring and a light tap on each click; when you zoom in, the picture follows the pointer.
- Fixed pinch to zoom in Mac Screen on the iPhone often taking several tries to start: a deliberate pinch now zooms the first time, around the pointer in Trackpad mode, and pinching back to where your fingers started returns the picture to its size.
- Changed approval questions on the iPhone and Android apps to offer the agent's own choices, as the Mac does, such as Muse Code's Allow for this session and Always deny.
- Changed the speaker's small picture over a full-screen shared screen in the iPhone app's calls so you can drag it to any corner, out of the way of what's being shown.
- Changed the Android app downloaded from enconvo.com to check for updates every time you open it or come back to it, instead of at most every six hours.
- Added Skip This Version to the Android app's update alert, so a version you skip isn't offered again until a newer one comes out; Later now only hides the alert until you next open the app.
- Fixed the iPhone and Android apps showing the steps and to-do lists of Muse Code, Grok Build and Antigravity as plain text instead of tool steps.
- Fixed the iPhone app's chat showing an agent's messages to its Subagents and its look at them as plain steps; they now show as Subagent lines with whether the message steered the task, waits for it to finish, interrupted it, or started a new turn, each Subagent shows the model and effort it runs on and whether it is nested, and a message another agent sent says who it is from.
- Fixed the phone Companion failing to load chats, Agents and Projects with "Enconvo on your Mac isn't answering" when the Mac's connection dropped for a moment while it was busy.
- Fixed Mac chats on Android so a brief connection drop or a busy Mac no longer shows an error or clears the messages, a reply keeps streaming once the Mac is back, and a chat opened before shows again without loading.
- Fixed the iPhone app hiding an agent's approval request, such as calling a friend, behind a one-line bar when you came back to the chat right after sending; the request now opens with its Allow and Deny buttons.
- Fixed the iPhone chat covering the call controls when you answer a scheduled call from your agent; the call's buttons now stay on top, as in a Live Talk call you start yourself.
- Fixed the "Calling you" card in an iPhone chat still saying Ringing after you picked up; it now follows the call as it does on the Mac.
- Fixed a Mac chat on iPhone that could keep loading forever when part of its page didn't arrive from the Mac; it now loads again, or shows the chat without the page.
- Fixed a Mac chat in the Android app that could keep loading forever when part of its page didn't arrive from the Mac; it now loads again, or shows the chat without the page.
- Fixed a Mac chat's reply on iPhone and Android disappearing below the screen when the keyboard opened after sending or the background tasks bar appeared, so the reply now stays in view.
- Fixed switching between Mac chats on iPhone and Android, which now opens a chat that wasn't loaded yet faster.
- Fixed messages sent from iPhone after changing a Mac chat's agent still going to the previous agent.
- Fixed subagent and Hub task chats appearing as their own rows in the Mac chat list on iPhone and Android; as on the Mac, they open from the chat that started them.
- Fixed a Mac chat on iPhone and Android that could stop showing a new reply when another chat's run, such as a subagent's, updated at the same moment.
- Fixed words sent to a Mac chat on iPhone and Android being lost when the send didn't go through or the chat's page stopped; they now come back to the message box.
- Fixed a Mac chat on iPhone and Android losing its earlier messages, or not showing a reply the Mac started, after the connection dropped and came back.
- Fixed Edit on an older message in a Mac chat on iPhone and Android, Find in a long chat, and going back to where you were reading after the app reopened.
- Fixed Claude Code background tasks showing twice in a Mac chat on iPhone and Android, and subagent cards staying on Running after reopening the chat.
- Fixed documents, images and links in a Mac chat on iPhone and Android that opened nothing when tapped; what the phone can't open now says to open it on your Mac.
- Fixed a Mac chat on iPhone and Android showing the wrong light or dark appearance after the phone changed it, or against the appearance set in Settings on Android.
- Fixed a Mac chat on iPhone and Android that stopped following a reply when Enconvo on the Mac restarted or the connection dropped mid-answer, and a Mac chat whose page loads slowly now shows its messages right away.
- Fixed the chat on iPhone and Android not showing what was said during a Live Talk call until the chat was opened again.
- Changed the chat on iPhone and Android to use narrower side margins, so messages use more of the screen width.
- Fixed Live Talk calls on iPhone and Android showing what was said in the chat in bursts every second or two instead of word by word as it is heard and spoken.
- Fixed the chat on iPhone and Android dropping back down when a reply finishes; the message you sent now stays at the top with the room below it, as on the Mac.
- Fixed Android's Enconvo call ring screen staying up after a lost hang-up, Decline doing nothing after the app was closed, and an ended call ringing again from a late notification.
- Added the Connect card to phone chats: when the agent needs a key during a chat on your iPhone, the card shows there and the key is saved in Credentials on your Mac; in an IM chat the agent never asks for a key and sends a link that opens the chat in Enconvo instead.
- Fixed a message sent from the iPhone or Android app coming back into the message box after it was sent, when its reply was still running or the app reconnected to the chat.
- Fixed a reply showing again below a message steered into a running task, after the iPhone or Android app opened the chat again or came back to it.
- The iPhone and Android apps now steer a running task with what you send, as Return does in the Mac chat, instead of queuing it; the box reads “Steer the running task…” while a reply runs.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.12 Changelog (2026-11-07)

Thanks for all the feedback during the beta. This release adds Video Conference, Friends and Friend Chat, lets you pair your other computers and work on them from here, and adds Email, Dashboards, Slides and Motion films. Projects get team workflows, goal checks and Ideas that work toward their goal, each Agent gets a Sandbox, Dictation gains Draft, and custom and local model providers get a long list of fixes. Update the iPhone app to 1.2.13 and the Android app to 1.4.3 to try the phone changes.

[Watch the 2.5.12 update tour](https://youtu.be/FFlvUZfw0B0) to see Video Conference, Friends, Email, paired computers, Motion films and more in action.

<YouTubePlayer videoId="FFlvUZfw0B0" title="What's New in Enconvo 2.5.12" />

## Highlights

- **Video Conference**: start a call from the Chat Window, the Dynamic Island, the iPhone or Android, ring friends, your Team or your other devices, or send a link anyone can join from a browser; schedule calls with email invites, run them with organizer controls and a waiting room, and add live captions with translation, AI notes and recording, backgrounds, screen sharing you can draw on, and picture in picture on the phones.
- **Friends**: add people by email or QR code, find your friends and Team in the sidebar, chat with them with files up to 1 GB, voice messages, emoji, and videos and PDFs that open right in the chat, and call them in one click, from the Mac, the iPhone or Android.
- **Email**: add your own mailboxes over IMAP and read, search and answer them in the Chat Window, with summaries, suggested replies, Polish, Snooze, Send Later and Undo Send, and agents that always ask before a mail goes out.
- **Pair your other computers**: from Settings › Connections, use another computer's chats, terminal and screen from here, see its chats and Projects in your sidebar, pick which computer a new chat runs on, and let your agent hand work to it.
- **Motion films**: ask for a short animated video with music, narration and captions, confirm its rough cut, scrub its scenes, and export it as MP4 or GIF.
- **Dashboards**: the agent makes a page of numbers and charts from a file, a web address or a connected service, keeps it up to date, and Check a number shows the row behind any figure.
- **Slides**: the agent makes a deck you edit, present with Presenter view and download as a PowerPoint with real text boxes, shapes, tables and speaker notes.
- **Automations has a new design**: Today shows what comes next and what ran, each Automation reads as When, Steps and Then, and Duplicate and a Run limit join it.
- **A Sandbox for each Agent**: with it on, an Agent's commands change only its working folder and temp, can't read your keys or saved credentials, and can't control other apps.
- **Safer Full access**: harmful commands ask first however they are spelled or hidden, many harmless ones no longer ask, and Approve for me checks a command the rules can't judge against what you asked for.
- **Response Styles and Intelligent UI levels**: an Enconvo agent answers as Normal, Concise, Explanatory, Formal or a style made from your own writing, and you set how readily it answers with charts, tables and cards.
- **Dictation Draft**: hold the Dictation hotkey with ⇧, say what to write or change, and watch the draft appear before it goes in, from a new Dictation pill with the live transcript.
- **Dictation learns your words**: the Dictionary suggests spellings it keeps hearing, Learn from my fixes adds a word you correct by hand, and smart spacing and snippets by voice save edits.
- **Live Desktop**: a board behind your windows shows what your agents, coding sessions and automations are doing today, with a character that reacts as things run, need you, finish or fail.
- **New models and providers**: Enconvo Fast, Balanced and Ultra on Enconvo Cloud, PipeLLM with one API key, GPT Image 2.6 Flare and Sunburst, Gemini Omni 1.2 Flash for video, and voice cloning in Text-to-Speech.
- **Custom and local providers**: dozens of fixes for OpenAI-compatible gateways, relays, Azure, OpenRouter and Gemini 3, and Ollama, llama.cpp, LM Studio and KoboldCpp chats that fit the context the server really runs with.
- **New default shortcuts**: the Chat Window opens with ⌃⌘X and the Smart Bar with ⌥Space, so your Dock, Finder's Go to Desktop and Mail's Send work again.
- **Your Mac's screen in Live Talk on the phone**: during Live Talk on the iPhone or Android, open your Mac's screen in a small floating window over the call, where a tap clicks and pinch zooms, and Full Screen opens the usual Mac Screen.

## Video Conference

- Added Video Conference between Enconvo users: start one from Video Conference in the Chat Window sidebar, the Dynamic Island, the menu bar menu or the iPhone, ring friends, teammates or your other devices, and invite anyone with a link at conference.enconvo.com.
- Added Video Conference's Home in the Chat Window, where one box pastes a link, calls a friend by name or starts a conference now, with Coming up, Earlier for the past month's calls and their recordings, Call again, and Call your Team.
- Added joining a conference from a web browser: anyone with the invite link can join as a guest without installing Enconvo, talk and type with the people there, and share their screen from a computer.
- Added a page before you join from a link, on the Mac and in a browser, where you check your camera and microphone, pick your devices, captions and translation language, go in muted, and see who is already there.
- Added cameras to conferences, with a choice of microphone, speaker and camera during the call, and the back camera on the phones.
- Added a gallery that keeps its shape as people join, speaker view, pinning, and a click on someone to make them big; in a call with one other person, your own small picture can be dragged to any corner or tapped to swap.
- Added Spotlight, which lets the organizer make one person big for everyone, while anyone can step back to Gallery.
- Added a floating dock with labels and separate Mic, Camera and Captions buttons, tiles with a green ring and level bars on whoever is talking, and one side panel with tabs for Chat, People and Captions.
- Added a chat to conferences: messages go to everyone, a count shows what you haven't read, whoever joins later can read back, and pictures and files up to 1 GB can be attached, pasted or dropped.
