- Fixed File Search without Full Disk Access never showing some Desktop, Documents and Downloads files on Macs where Spotlight takes over three seconds to list them: the listing now runs to the end, and typing on narrows it instead of asking again.
- Changed File Search to leave one or two typed letters to the index alone, since Spotlight listing those takes seconds and shows nothing useful.

## Skills, Plugins and Sync

- Added Add from GitHub to the Skills page: paste a link to a repository with SKILL.md at its root, or to a skill's folder inside one, and the skill installs into your skills folder and opens, with Enconvo remembering where it came from.
- Added Check for updates to the Skills page for skills added from GitHub: each skill whose repository has moved on gets an Update available mark and an Update button in its details, and the result offers Update all.
- Fixed the launch-time update of a skill added from GitHub into a folder other than ~/.enconvo/skills, which installed the new copy into ~/.enconvo/skills instead of the folder the skill was in.
- Added Skills and Plugins to Desktop Sync, each with its own switch under Settings › Sync: the skills you install or make, with whether each is on, and the plugins you install from the Enconvo store, GitHub, npm or a link now sync between your computers, which install them from where they came from and uninstall what you uninstall, while plugins that come with Enconvo, plugins you are developing, Connectors and sign-ins stay on each computer.
- Removed the Bland Connector from the Plugins page; if you already installed it, it stays installed.
- Fixed a Connector, such as Twilio, missing from the Plugins page on a Mac where its plugin folder had been left with only empty folders in it.

## Models, Sign-in and Settings

- Added OpenCode and OpenCode Go as AI model providers: add your OpenCode Console API key once under Settings › AI Model to chat with OpenCode's pay-as-you-go models, such as Claude, GPT and Gemini, or with the models of your OpenCode Go subscription.
- Fixed the model picker listing a provider you had only looked at in Settings: a built-in provider such as OpenRouter or DeepSeek now appears in the pickers once its API key or sign-in is saved, so opening its page no longer adds it with no key, while Enconvo Cloud, local models and providers you created stay listed as before.
- Added a Back to Enconvo button to the page an OpenAI, xAI or Antigravity sign-in ends on in the browser, and a sign-in started in Settings or the guide now brings that window back by itself once it connects, as one started from a chat does.
- Changed signing in with a Claude subscription or Claude Code: copying the code the sign-in page shows is enough, and Enconvo signs in without it being pasted; the code stays out of Clipboard History.
- Fixed AI Commands that follow the default model, such as PopBar buttons, the Translator and Dictation's Polish and Translate, showing your default model in their model button and picker while running on the All Commands model, so an Out of Cloud points notice could name a model the button never showed; the button now shows the All Commands model, the picker's Use default reads All AI Commands for these commands, a reasoning effort picked for one of them now applies, and the notice after picking another model points to Settings › AI Commands.
- Added Upgrade to Pro and Upgrade to Teams in Settings › Account and the Plans page for Plus and Pro license owners: what you paid for your license counts toward the new one, so you pay only the difference, promo codes still apply, every Mac you use keeps a seat on Teams, and a refund within 30 days gives your old license back.
- Fixed Automation showing as Not granted on the Permissions page and in the Computer Use permission prompt after a relaunch, when the grant stood but System Events wasn't running.
- Fixed Set up Telnyx in Settings › Agent Phone doing nothing when Telnyx was already connected in Plugins: it now waits while the Telnyx plugin updates, and says so when Agent Phone can't use it yet.

## Voice

- Fixed a reply being read aloud talking over Dictation, as when you clicked the mic in the Dynamic Island's, Chat Window's or Smart Bar's input or pressed the Dictation hotkey while the answer was read: the reading now pauses before Dictation starts listening, including the sentences still to come, and reads on from where it paused when you finish or cancel, while a reading you had paused yourself stays paused, one that was stopped or replaced while you dictated doesn't come back, and Voice Command keeps its own behavior.
- Changed voice calls on the Mac to pause her the moment you start talking over her, before your words are recognized: your words then stop her for good, while an "mm", a cough or a laugh lets her go on from just before where she stopped, and talking while your microphone is muted doesn't pause her.
- Fixed a voice call that hears you with a local speech model being slow to stop when you talk over her: it now listens again about every quarter second while you go on, where it waited a second and a half after first hearing too little, and a word or two said over her, such as "wait", stops her as soon as you pause after it.
- Fixed a voice call on the Mac or iPhone staying on after you said goodbye in a longer sentence, such as "I just need to say hi and say goodbye, bye": Jev now reads whether your words and her reply end the call, in any language, and the call hangs up once her goodbye has played; a goodbye to someone else, or more words after hers, keeps the call on.

## iPad and Mac Screen

- Added iPad support to the iPhone app: the chat list stays beside the chat on a wide screen, the side panel opens beside the chat with Full Screen in its tab strip, chats keep to a readable column, the app works in a resizable window, and strings that named the iPhone name the iPad.
- Added the iPad menu bar's commands with the Mac's shortcuts: File › New Chat (⌘N), and in View Show Sidebar (⌃⌘S), Show Side Panel, Full Screen Panel, Doodle, a Mac chat's Files (⌘P), Changes (⌃⇧G), Terminal (⌃`) and Side chat (⌥⌘S) in the side panel, Open Location (⌘L), Reload Page (⌘R), Back (⌘[) and Forward (⌘]) for the side panel's page, Show Next and Previous Tab (⌃⇥ and ⌃⇧⇥), Show Tab (⌘1 to ⌘9) and Reopen Closed Tab (⇧⌘T); Edit › Find (⌘F, ⌘G and ⇧⌘G) finds in the side panel's page.
- Added a divider between the chat and the side panel on iPad that you drag to resize them, as on the Mac; the width you leave stays for every chat.
- Added side panel windows on iPad: drag a web page's tab out of the side panel, or choose Open in New Window from its menu, and it opens in a window of its own with its tabs and New Tab, a button that brings back the chat it came from, and closing its last tab closes it.
- Added Apple Pencil to the side panel's Doodle on iPad: once the Pencil marks, a finger scrolls the page, a Draw with Finger switch in Doodle's bar lets a finger mark too, and a hovering Pencil shows where it will land.
- Added the Live Talk avatar beside the chat on iPad, as on iPhone Duo's inner display, with Full Screen and Back to the chat in her header; a link or the side panel opened from the chat meanwhile shows over the chat.
- Changed notes at the top of the iPad app to show as a centred line instead of a bar across the whole window.
- Added Mac Screen to the iPhone and iPad app: see your Mac's screen live from Remote, take control to click, scroll and type on it with trackpad or direct touch gestures and the phone's keyboard, with the toolbar on iPhone Duo's bar edge and a trackpad half when half folded; agents using the screen wait while you have it, a dropped connection, such as the phone moving to another network, connects again by itself, and Settings › Connections › Screen on the Mac turns it off.
- Added a Screen button to your connected Mac in a new chat's Runs on menu in the iPhone and iPad app, with a one-time tip that points to it, so Mac Screen is a tap away when you start a chat; each Mac there now says only whether it's connected.

## iPhone and Android

- Added the / menu to Enconvo chats in the iPhone and Android apps: typing / at the start of a message lists /loop, /compact and your skills, with what each takes, and typing a command and a space shows its argument hint dimmed after it, in Claude Code and Codex chats too.
- Added interactive apps to Mac chats on the iPhone, where they run as they do on the Mac, Fix button included.
- Changed the Work plan card in the iPhone app to say what each task waits for, as on the Mac: before Start, the tasks it runs after or its turn in a folder the tasks share, and once the plan runs, the task, the lead's check or the free slot it waits for.
- Changed the message box of a new chat that runs on your Mac in the iPhone and iPad app to match the Mac chat's, with the text above a row of round buttons.
- Changed the iPhone app's message box so Return on a hardware keyboard sends and Shift-Return starts a new line, as on the Mac.
- Changed the side panel's Terminal tab to show only the Escape key above the keyboard while a hardware keyboard is attached, on iPad and iPhone.
- Added Android phones to the phone apps Mac agents can use: an agent can ask a paired Android phone where you are or set an alarm on it, as it can an iPhone.
- Fixed web pages at plain http:// addresses not opening in the iPhone app's side panel or from a link in a chat: they now load as they do in Safari.
- Fixed the iPhone app offering Move to Project for a chat that works in a worktree, where moving always failed.
- Fixed sliders, drawing areas and sideways-scrolling parts of chat widgets on the iPhone, where a drag on them scrolled the chat or opened the sidebar instead.
- Fixed questions the Agent asks in a chat started on the iPhone, which were cancelled when the Dynamic Island on the Mac closed while you were still answering them on the phone.
- Fixed a background command's output in a Claude Code chat on the iPhone and iPad app staying on its first lines on iOS 26 instead of following the newest ones.
- Fixed the Remote row at the top of the iPhone app's chat list cutting off whether your Mac is online when its name is long.
- Added xAI on your own account to the iPhone and iPad app's Text-to-Speech, Dictation and Live Talk: with an xAI API key or the Grok sign-in in Settings › AI Model › xAI, answers read aloud in xAI's voices, dictation streams to xAI's real-time speech recognition, and calls in phone chats hear and speak with xAI, all straight to xAI with no Cloud points.
- Added xAI on Enconvo Cloud to Dictation and Live Talk in the iPhone, iPad and Android apps, paid for with Cloud points and needing no xAI account, beside xAI on your own account.
- Changed the Text-to-Speech, Dictation and Live Talk settings in the iPhone, iPad and Android apps to list their services the way Settings › AI Model lists its providers, with Default on the one in use and a Cloud tag on each Enconvo Cloud service, as on the Mac; Text-to-Speech lists xAI on Enconvo Cloud and xAI on your own account as two services, and each service opens its own page with the account it uses, the model to download, the voice with a sample, and Set as Default.
- Fixed Live Talk calls in Mac chats in the iPhone, iPad and Android apps with Listen or Speak set to xAI on your own account: they now use the xAI account on the phone, the Grok sign-in included, so a call no longer ends with an xAI sign-in error when the Mac has no xAI account, and replies are spoken in the xAI voice you chose instead of a Gemini voice.
- Changed Kokoro voices in the iPhone app to work on iOS 27.2 and later too, whose Core ML no longer crashes them during long readings; they stay unavailable on iOS 26.4 to 27.1.
- Fixed voice samples in the iPhone and Android apps' Text-to-Speech and Live Talk settings cutting into a voice chat in progress; they now ask you to end the call first.
- Fixed Text-to-Speech settings in the iPhone and Android apps showing a system voice that's no longer on the phone by its identifier instead of its name.
- Fixed the iPhone and Android apps naming the regions of system voices in the phone's language instead of the app's.
- Fixed the voice filters in the iPhone and Android apps hiding the filter you pick at the screen's edge.
- Fixed Dictation's Languages and Details in the iPhone app treating Cantonese as Chinese for models other than Soniox.
- Fixed the Dictation row in the iPhone and Android apps' Settings saying the model isn't downloaded while it downloads.
- Fixed the Android app's Text-to-Speech settings showing voices you install in Android's settings only after the app restarts.
- Fixed the Android app cutting off a voice sample you start right after leaving a voices page.
- Fixed the Android app letting go of the dictation model after Mac Screen or another screen opened over it, so the next dictation waited for the model to load again.
- Fixed the Android app's Settings opening a page twice, or going back two pages, after a quick second tap.
- Fixed the Android app sometimes going back two pages when a change from your Mac arrived just as you left a voices page.
- Fixed the Android app sometimes closing when a web page or Terminal in a chat's side panel stopped, such as after the app was away for a while; the tab now loads again on its own.

## Answers on the iPhone and Android

- Added charts to answers in the iPhone and Android apps: the bar, line, area, pie, donut and funnel charts the Agent draws now show on the phone, where they were left out before.
- Added sliders, number fields, pickers and switches to answers in the iPhone and Android apps: a calculator or another view the Agent builds with them works its results out again as you change them, as on the Mac, where the inputs were left out and the results blank before; a button can send your values along with its prompt, and Reset Values in the card's menu puts them back.
- Added quizzes you can answer in the iPhone and Android apps: pick an option and check it to see whether it's right, the answer and why, then try again, as on the Mac, where the phone showed only the question and its options before.
- Added flashcards you can study in the iPhone and Android apps: tap a card or Flip card to see its back and note, and move through the deck with the arrows, as on the Mac, where the phone showed the fronts and backs as a list before.
- Added checklists you can tick off in the iPhone and Android apps: tap an item to tick it or clear it, with the count and a progress bar filling as you go, as on the Mac, where the phone showed the items as a list before.
- Added tabs in the iPhone and Android apps, as on the Mac: a plan in parts, such as the days of a workout, shows one part at a time under a row of tabs you tap, where the phone showed every part in a long list before.
- Added step-by-step walkthroughs in the iPhone and Android apps, as on the Mac: an explanation in stages, such as how a recipe or an algorithm goes, shows one step at a time with Previous, Next and a bar of the steps you can tap, where the phone showed every step in a list before.
- Added schedules to answers in the iPhone and Android apps: a day or week the Agent plans shows as an agenda by day, each block's time beside a bar of its kind's color, with a legend of the kinds and their hours, as on the Mac, where it was a plain list before.
- Changed option comparisons in the iPhone and Android apps to look like the Mac's: plans or products to choose from show as cards, each with its price, what it includes and lacks, and the best fit ringed with its badge, where the phone showed a plain list before.
- Changed timelines in the iPhone and Android apps to look like the Mac's: itineraries, histories and the steps of a process show each event as a dot on a line, its time beside its title and its details under them, where the phone showed a plain bulleted list before.
- Changed flowcharts in the iPhone and Android apps to look like the Mac's: steps go down a column with arrows between them, a start or end in a small capsule, a decision in a diamond, and its Yes and No branches side by side under their labels, where the phone showed a numbered list before.
- Changed roadmaps in the iPhone and Android apps to look like the Mac's: phases stand side by side, each a colored header over its progress bar with its title and percentage, and the overall completion shows under them, where the phone showed a bulleted list before.
- Changed trees in the iPhone and Android apps to look like the Mac's: org charts and breakdowns show as colored boxes joined by lines, or as an indented outline when they are too wide for the screen, where the phone showed a bulleted list before.
- Changed cycles in the iPhone and Android apps to look like the Mac's: stages sit around a loop as numbered colored circles joined by arrows, with the title in the middle and a key of what each stage does under it, where the phone showed a numbered list before.
- Changed maps in the iPhone app to look like the Mac's: places are marked by number on an Apple Maps picture, joined by a dashed line when they make a route, with the numbered list of them under it, where the phone showed a plain list before; the Android app shows the numbered list.
- Changed weather cards in answers in the iPhone and Android apps to show like the Mac's: the conditions now with their tiles, the next six hours and the days ahead, or the one-row card, loaded live for the named place or for where the phone is, where the phone left the weather out before.
- Changed flow charts and trees in answers in the iPhone and Android apps to show their icons as on the Mac: each box's icon sits above its name, and in an outline it takes the place of the dot, where the phone left the icons out before.
- Changed step lists in answers in the iPhone and Android apps to show as on the Mac: each step is a row with its icon in a tinted tile beside its name and description, where the phone showed a plain bulleted list before.
- Changed colored words in answers in the iPhone and Android apps to show as on the Mac: right answers in green, mistakes in red and other colored or bold words keep their color and weight and stay on the line with the words around them, where the phone showed them plain before.
- Changed progress bars in answers in the iPhone and Android apps to show as on the Mac: each one is a thin bar in its color under its label and share, with its note under it, and one past its limit, such as spending over a budget, marks where the limit falls and stripes the part beyond it, where the phone showed a row of number tiles before.
- Changed Mermaid diagrams in answers in the iPhone and Android apps, such as sequence and state diagrams, to show drawn as on the Mac while the Mac is connected, where the phone showed their code before; a tap opens the diagram to zoom in.
- Changed the main next step under an answer in the iPhone and Android apps to look as on the Mac: a button the Agent makes full width spans the card on a line of its own as a rounded pill with an arrow after its label.
- Fixed an answer in the iPhone and Android apps losing everything after a part that changes with a choice, when that part's text held an apostrophe.

## Languages

- Fixed Chinese switching between the formal "您" and the informal "你", as an empty chat asking "有什么可以帮您？" and the Mac's location and photo permission prompts; it says "你" throughout.
- Fixed Traditional Chinese mixing in mainland wording, as an agent called 智能體, 智慧體 or 智慧代理 on different screens, and 保存, 點擊 and 剪貼板 for save, click and clipboard; it says 代理程式, 儲存, 按一下 and 剪貼簿 throughout.
- Fixed Korean switching from the formal "합니다" to the casual "해요" on some screens, as a stopped task saying "작업을 멈췄어요", and Simplified Chinese showing "模型與服務費率" in traditional characters.
- Fixed German text switching between the informal du and the formal Sie, as in the chat list reading "auf Ihrem Mac" under "Was soll dein Mac tun?"; it says du throughout.
- Fixed French text switching between the formal vous and the informal tu, as in Settings › Dictation reading "ce que tu dis"; it says vous throughout.
- Fixed Portuguese on the Mac mixing in Brazilian words, as Sync, Scheduled Tasks, Teams and the design canvas saying "equipe", "arquivo", "salvar" and "excluir" next to "Definições"; they say "equipa", "ficheiro", "guardar" and "eliminar".
- Fixed Portuguese switching between the formal "o seu" and the informal "o teu", as on the xAI page offering "Usar o teu plano" next to "o seu plano"; it says o seu throughout.
- Fixed Portuguese addressing the reader as "você" and starting sentences with a bare "Seu" or "Sua", as the Agent Phone settings saying "Uma tarefa precisa de você"; it says "si" and "o seu" as European Portuguese does.
- Fixed Portuguese on iPhone and iPad mixing Brazilian and European Portuguese, as in Settings › AI Model saying both "Ajustes" and "Definições" or both "login" and "sessão"; it reads as European Portuguese throughout.
- Fixed the iPhone app's Portuguese permission prompts for the camera, location, contacts, calendars, reminders, Home and alarms using Brazilian wording such as "câmera" and "código de pareamento"; they now read as European Portuguese.
- Fixed Settings › AI Model's sign-in choices on iPhone and iPad in German and French, where In use left no room for the choice's name; In use now moves under a name it would cut.
- Fixed the New chat button at the bottom of the iPad chat list in French, where the pencil hung off the button and "Nouvelle discussion" was cut off.
- Fixed the iPhone app's Home showing "Ask anything" in English in the message box when the app uses another language.
- Fixed the iPhone app's chat list, Remote, open chats and sheets, and a chat's messages, such as file cards, changed-file summaries and the buttons under a reply, keeping some labels in the previous language after you change the app's language in Settings.
- Fixed VoiceOver reading many of the iPhone app's buttons, such as a chat's Side Panel, Chat options, Add photos and Dictate, and the Back, Forward, Reload, Share, Find and More buttons of a side panel page, in the previous language after you change the app's language in Settings.
- Fixed VoiceOver reading the iPhone app's sidebar button in English when the app uses another language.
- Fixed VoiceOver announcing in English, in every language, that a chat moved to or out of a project, that an invite code was copied, or that a file arrived during a call.
- Fixed the iPhone app's file changes card showing its file count in English, and VoiceOver reading its files and line counts in English, when the app uses another language.
- Fixed the iPhone app naming a web page, window, app or messages added to a message in English in every language.
- Fixed the iPhone app's settings showing their status, pairing errors, notices and dates in English in every other language, the pairing steps naming a button the iPhone no longer has, and copy confirmations for plugin routes, invite links and phone app links reading in English.
- Fixed some Worktree messages showing in English in every language, such as the warning that a terminal still has one of a Project's chats open when you archive or delete it, and the reasons a Worktree can't be archived or restored.
- Fixed the chat's tool summaries, such as "Read a file, ran commands", the "Working for", "Worked for" and "You stopped after" lines, the chat's message buttons, the Permissions menu and tool steps (search counts, approval buttons, output labels) staying in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed chat error and status messages (attaching files, sending, editing, forking, compacting and inserting prompts) showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the message box's slash menu and the model chip's reasoning level (Low, Medium, High, and the rest) showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the chat's Install card and the app context chips above the message box showing English text when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the menus and messages of visualizations and cards in chat (copying, downloading, resetting values and the download dialog) showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed agent question and approval cards showing their hints, placeholders, "asks" heading and answered labels in English in every other language.
- Fixed the conversation points pill's Context and Usage tabs showing English group names, counts and notes when Enconvo uses another language.
- Fixed Archived Chats (the settings page, the sidebar panel and its menu), the archive and restore messages, and the workflow canvas's input and end steps showing in English, and the archived chats' dates ignoring Enconvo's language.
- Fixed the Memory page (its filters, the box for adding a Memory, the details panel and who reads each Memory) showing in English, and its dates ignoring Enconvo's language.
- Fixed the saved post preview, an agent's Memory settings, the changes review, the Claude Code usage limits and the archived chat banner showing English in every other language.
- Fixed Ask AI in the knowledge base, and the chat menus of the workflow Assistant, Plugin Studio and scheduling chats, showing English lines and times when Enconvo uses another language.
- Fixed Ask AI in the knowledge base asking its suggested questions and Explain in English, which could bring the answer back in English, and showing citation places and errors in English when Enconvo uses another language.
- Fixed the lines under Ask AI answers that say what was read and searched, and its status while it works, showing in English when Enconvo uses another language.
- Fixed knowledge base messages (renaming, moving files to the Trash, pasting files, relinking a folder, saving transcripts), knowledge base items' type, date, size and status (card labels, the sort and Types menus, the list columns and the details panel), and a Project's Finish setting notes showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the MCP servers page's messages (connecting, installing, updating, importing and signing out of a server), its AI model access choices and its version notes showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the Skills page's messages (installing, uninstalling, importing and moving skills to the Trash), its switch names and the detail panel's source and date showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the Plugins page's subtitle, its Connector messages (connecting, disconnecting and changing the default account) and its buttons' names showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed a Connector's page and its row on the Plugins page showing English or mistranslated, spliced sentences in other languages, such as the line about turning off macOS access after Disconnect.
- Fixed the card an agent shows to connect a Connector, its Gmail Mail Alerts question, and the API key form staying in English when Enconvo uses another language.
- Fixed the Computer Use settings showing English permission cards and browser connection times when Enconvo uses another language.
- Fixed the OpenClaw, Hermes and Ollama setup guides in AI model settings (their steps, statuses, setup dialogs and messages) showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed model routes, tag management, reply details, the workflow overview, the knowledge source card and the skill install dialog showing English in every other language, the Command Return shortcut reading as "command result" in German, French, Spanish, Italian and Portuguese, and Chinese leaving the Dynamic Island untranslated on some screens.
- Fixed the X post saving settings, knowledge base filters and categories, the workflow step palette, the Smart Bar appearance setting and Live Talk's napping status showing English in every other language.
- Fixed IM Channels' connection messages, the knowledge base editor's word count, read-only and save messages, and New Knowledge Base and Screen Doodle settings messages showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed Settings › Account's plan details, the Appearance, refund and log out messages, Settings search messages, the log out and remove Project dialogs, and the Cancel button of several confirmations showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed the plans dialog and Settings › Permissions showing in English in other languages: plan cards, the benefits table, the buy, upgrade and add-seat buttons, and each macOS permission's name and purpose now follow the app's language, with point counts written the language's way.
- Fixed the Points page showing its history, daily usage chart and savings line partly in English or in a garbled word order in other languages, with its dates and numbers now written the way the app's language writes them, and Chinese calling points 点数 everywhere.
- Fixed Manage devices, Invite Friends, Redeem Code and About showing parts of their text in English or in English word order in other languages, writing dates and counts the language's way, and fixed Korean sentences that ended with a Chinese full stop.
- Fixed the icon picker, Raycast extension installs from GitHub, device limit notices, the redeem page and the MCP bundle installer showing English in every other language, and Portuguese addressing you informally on some screens, as "Tenta novamente" after an error.
- Fixed the Close button label of dialogs, the sidebar toggle, page buttons in Points usage and Heartbeat logs, workflow task states, and a few chat and speech model errors showing in English when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed settings dropdowns showing a garbled empty-search line and English search hints, and settings text and number fields showing an English "Enter …" hint, when Enconvo is set to another language.
- Fixed page lists showing "Next step" instead of "Next page" in Chinese, the local model's memory usage showing as the Memory feature, German calling the Memory feature "Speicher", and page and count lines such as "Showing 1–20 of 80" keeping English word order in other languages.
- Fixed lines in chat, skills, MCP install, heartbeat, credentials, subscription usage, avatars and knowledge bases showing English word order or stray words in other languages, such as Chinese file counts ending in "秒", and wrote their times and dates in the app's language.
- Fixed searches with no match, delete and authorization prompts and other settings lines in languages other than English, which split a quoted name or a count from its sentence, such as Chinese reading 未找到匹配“的文件 before the search term.

# Enconvo 2.5.10 Changelog (2026-10-27)

Thanks for all the feedback during the betas. This release turns Projects into
teams of your agents led by a Lead, opens Artifacts and Docs beside the chat,
has your iPhone ring when an agent needs you, brings scheduled tasks back to the
chat they came from, and lets Coding agents work in git worktrees of their own.

## Highlights

- **Projects that work as a team**: give a Project a Lead and members from your
  agents and Coding agents; the Lead plans who does what, members carry it out
  on the Board through Playbooks, another member reviews the work, and a
  Boundary keeps them to the folders, Connectors and knowledge bases you allow.
- **Artifacts and Docs beside the chat**: pages, apps and diagrams the agent
  makes open beside the chat with numbered versions, the docs it writes are
  yours to edit with comments, Suggesting and @Enconvo, and Excel, Numbers and
  OpenDocument sheets open there too.
- **Agent Phone**: your iPhone rings when an agent needs your answer while
  you're away from your Mac, and with Twilio or Telnyx agents can call your
  number, or call someone for you, such as a restaurant, and report back in the
  chat.
- **Scheduled tasks that report back, and /loop**: a scheduled task's result
  comes back to the chat you set it up in, where its agent tells you what it
  found, with a notification on your phone, and /loop runs a prompt again on an
  interval or whenever the agent decides.
- **Coding agents in worktrees**: each chat can work in a git worktree of its
  own, on a new branch, a branch or a pull request, and Finish with a commit, a
  merge or a pull request, and the Changes tab reviews each turn's changes as in
  Codex.
- **Codex and Claude Code as in their own apps**: Codex chats get Plan mode,
  Approve for me, $ mentions and Codex's own commands such as /status, /diff and
  /usage, Claude Code chats get Claude Artifacts and your claude.ai Connectors,
  and every Coding agent gets a permission mode picker.
- **Compare answers**: send one message to 2 to 6 agents and see where their
  answers agree and differ, with a recommendation.
- **Doodle in the side panel**: mark up and comment on a PDF, picture, file,
  artifact or web page beside the chat, and your numbered marks go with your
  next message.
- **A new Knowledge Base**: it opens in the Chat Window in under a second with
  thousands of items, with Ask, a table view and Library views.
- **Memory you can see and steer**: Memory files what it learns under the
  people, projects, places and topics it's about, with a Map of how they relate,
  the Memory page shows every Memory in full, and each Agent can choose which
  Memories it reads and whether it saves new ones.
- **Your AI plans on the iPhone**: Phone chats sign in with your ChatGPT, Grok,
  Le Chat, GitHub Copilot or Antigravity plan with no API key, and the iPhone's
  AI Model page gets 13 more providers and models added by ID.
- **An iPhone app that does more**: Phone chats can use your calendar,
  reminders, contacts, Home and alarms, a Mac chat opens its files, changes and
  terminal in the side panel, and Compare answers, Project plans and on-device
  dictation come to the phone.
- **Live Talk hears you as you speak** with Soniox, AssemblyAI, Azure,
  ElevenLabs, Volcengine and xAI, so answers start sooner, and Kokoro adds free,
  offline voices in English, Mandarin and Japanese on Macs with Apple silicon
  and the iPhone.
- **The Chat Window in glass**: it takes on the Smart Bar's translucent look, a
  chat moves between the Smart Bar, the Dynamic Island and the Chat Window with
  its running reply, switching chats shows the whole chat at once, and finished
  chats archive in a click or on their own.
- **New models**: GPT-6.1 Terra, OpenAI's new mid-range model, and Kimi K3.1 Pro
  with a 1M-token context, on Enconvo Cloud and with your own key, and the model
  picker shows your subscription's 5-hour and weekly limits.
- **Agents that handle your Mac and files better**: Computer Use can swipe,
  scrolls in about half the time and clicks more reliably, and agents read text
  in older encodings such as GBK and Big5 and search inside PDF, Office, EPUB
  and iWork files.
- **New Connectors and plugins**: Twilio, Telnyx and Bland Connectors send
  messages, place calls and manage phone numbers and AI voice agents, an Airmail
  plugin writes and files your email, and Pollinations makes images on a free
  tier with no key.
- **Your own Avatar characters**: make a plush character in Settings › Avatar,
  describe one for your AI model to draft, or add one from the OpenAvatar
  gallery.
- **See what Enconvo did**: an Activity log in Settings › Usage Statistics lists
  every AI request, tool call and scheduled task, kept only on your Mac, and
  Submit Diagnostics… sends recent logs and crash reports with personal details
  taken out.
- **Fewer freezes and crashes**: Enconvo no longer stops for seconds when a slow
  app launches or you select text in a busy app, no longer quits when you start
  Dictation with AirPods, and no longer holds about 250 MB of memory after
  launch.

## Artifacts and Docs

- Added Artifacts: pages, small apps, diagrams and programs the agent makes open
  beside the chat with numbered versions you can restore, edit or download, and
  the Chat Window sidebar lists them all.
- Added Docs: ask for a report, plan or letter and the agent writes a doc beside
  the chat that you edit with tables, images and charts, comments, Suggesting
  and earlier versions.
- Added asking Enconvo inside a doc: type @ on an empty line, select text and
  choose Edit with Enconvo, or mention @Enconvo in a comment.

## Projects and Worktrees

- Added members to Projects: give a Project a Lead and members, your agents and
  Coding agents each with a role, or start from a template, and new chats in the
  Project start with its Lead.
- Added Work plans: the Lead shows who does what, which you can change or
  reassign before Start, then runs each task in its own chat on the Board, and
  you can hand work to a Project from any chat or the @ menu.
- Added Playbooks: the built-in Build, Fix a bug, Review and Research Playbooks,
  or your own from the Workflow editor, take a task step by step, with the
  member you choose for each part.
- Added reviews, checks and recovery to Work plans: a task that changes files is
  reviewed by another member, and tasks cut short, stalled or going in circles
  run again or wait for you.
- Added a Needs you tray, Allow in this Project, a points budget, Pause and Stop
  plan to Project chats, with a Run log of each decision the Lead made.
- Added a Boundary to each Project that keeps its agents to the folders, Mac
  control, Connectors, MCP servers and knowledge bases you pick, even with Full
  access.
- Added Projects at work to the Dynamic Island, turning amber when a member
  needs you so you can answer in place.
- Added Worktrees to git Projects: a chat can work in its own worktree on a new
  branch, a branch or a pull request, with its own ports and Prepare command,
  set up in Project settings.
- Added Finish to worktree chats: commit with a drafted message, merge or open a
  pull request, and archive the worktree once it's merged, with Restore to bring
  it back.

## Side Panel and Doodle

- Added Doodle to the side panel: draw on a PDF, picture, file, artifact or web
  page, or point at a paragraph or table and comment on it, typed or spoken, and
  the numbered marks go with your next message.
- Added spreadsheets to the side panel: Excel, Numbers and OpenDocument sheets
  open with their sheets and merged cells, and you can copy cells out.
- Changed the Changes tab into a review, as in Codex, one turn at a time with
  unified or side-by-side diffs, opened from Review on a turn's files changed
  card.

## Chats and Chat Window

- Changed the Chat Window to the Smart Bar's translucent look, following the
  Liquid Glass setting on macOS 27, with an Appearance option in Settings › Chat
  Window.
- Added Open in Chat Window to the Smart Bar and the Dynamic Island, and
  Continue in Smart Bar (⇧⌘O) to the Chat Window, which move a chat with its
  running reply.
- Added Compare answers to the + menu: pick 2 to 6 agents, a saved set or Second
  Opinion, and the chat's agent lays out where their answers agree and differ.
- Added quick archiving: archive chats from the sidebar, several at once, all
  finished ones from the ⋯ menu, or automatically after 3, 7 or 30 days.
- Added /loop to agent chats, as in Claude Code, which runs a prompt again on an
  interval or whenever the agent decides.
- Changed Automations to Scheduled, where a task's result comes back to the chat
  you set it up in, with a notification on your phones, and a task can pause
  until a date.
- Changed switching chats to show the whole chat at once at its latest message,
  and the sidebar's status marks to quieter ones, as in Codex.
- Added the PopBar to text selected in the Chat Window, and ↑ and ↓ in an empty
  message box to step through the prompts sent in that chat.
- Changed messages sent while a reply runs to show at once and queued messages
  to show their pictures and files, and fixed Edit on a sent message doing
  nothing.
- Fixed attaching an .ico, BMP, TIFF or HEIC image breaking a chat, and keys
  typed in Enconvo's windows going to the Dynamic Island.

## Coding Agents

- Added Codex's own commands to Codex chats, such as /status, /diff, /usage,
  /mcp, /plugins, /hooks and /fork, with $ mentions of apps and skills.
- Added Plan mode and the Approve for me permission mode to Codex chats, and
  Hooks, Skills, Plugins, Apps and Personality to Codex's settings.
- Added a permission mode picker to new Claude Code, Codex, Grok Build and
  Antigravity chats, and model and effort pickers to Grok Build and Antigravity
  chats.
- Added Claude Artifacts and claude.ai Connectors to Claude Code chats on a
  Claude subscription, and Claude in Chrome and Output Style to Claude Code's
  settings.
- Added Grok Build's questions and plan approval to Grok Build chats, and your
  Antigravity plan's Claude models to Antigravity chats.
- Changed renaming a Claude Code or Codex chat to rename its session too, and
  Continue in Terminal to keep the options from the agent's settings.
- Fixed Coding agent chats that won't start because of a setting, which now
  point to the setting to change.

## Memory and Knowledge Base

- Changed the Knowledge Base to open in the Chat Window instead of its own
  window, with Ask, a table view and Library views, and in under a second with
  thousands of items.
- Changed web pages and long files you save to a knowledge base or Memory to be
  read by sections, so agents get more of what they say.
- Changed the Memory page, now in the Chat Window sidebar too, to show every
  Memory in full, grouped by Pinned and by day or project, with filters and an
  editable detail panel.
- Added Concepts to Memory, filing your Memories under the people, projects,
  places and topics they are about, and a Map view of your Memories and
  knowledge base items.
- Added a Memory section to each Agent's settings to choose which Memories it
  reads and whether it saves new ones.
- Removed the Memory card at the end of a chat: Memory now learns quietly, and
  Settings › Memory lists each change with Undo, going back 90 days for Memory
  Tidy.
- Fixed Memory saving relative dates instead of the date, passing illnesses and
  things you asked it to forget, writing in a language other than yours, and
  saving while Memory is off.

## Live Talk, Voices and Dictation

- Changed Live Talk to hear you as you speak with Soniox on your own key,
  AssemblyAI, Azure, ElevenLabs Scribe v2 Realtime, Volcengine and xAI, and with
  xAI to speak over one connection for the whole call, so answers start sooner.
- Added Kokoro to Text to Speech and Live Talk on Macs with Apple silicon and
  macOS 15 or later: free, offline voices in English, Mandarin and Japanese.
- Fixed Phonon seeming to switch between a man's and a woman's voice mid-reply,
  and changed it to download from Enconvo's own server, much faster in many
  places.
- Changed SenseVoice on your Mac to write punctuation and numbers as digits, and
  fixed Dictation with Soniox stopping with "Context is too long".

## Agent Phone

- Added Agent Phone: when an agent's question waits while you're away from your
  Mac, your iPhone rings after the time you choose, and you answer by voice.
- Added Twilio and Telnyx to Settings › Agent Phone, so agents can call your
  phone number when your iPhone can't ring, with Test call and quiet hours.
- Added calls agents make for you, such as booking a table, through Twilio or
  Telnyx, keeping to what the agent wrote down and reporting back in the chat.

## Agents in Your Apps and Files

- Added a swipe action to Computer Use, made its scrolls take about half as
  long, and fixed dozens of cases with clicks, keys, checkboxes and sliders.
- Changed agents to read and edit text in older encodings such as GBK, Big5 and
  Shift_JIS, and to search inside PDF, Office, EPUB and iWork files.
- Fixed many cases in agents' file, calendar, reminder, Apple Notes, Obsidian
  and Contacts tools, such as names with curly quotes and all-day events.
- Fixed web pages crashing in Chrome and Edge with "Aw, Snap!" while Browser Use
  worked in them, with the Enconvo browser extension 0.1.16.
- Removed the Tools tab from Agent settings, since every Agent already uses its
  built-in tools, your MCP tools and everything you install.

## Smart Bar, Plugins and Connectors

- Added Twilio, Telnyx and Bland Connectors for sending messages, placing calls
  and managing phone numbers and AI voice agents in your accounts.
- Added an Airmail plugin that writes an email from what you dictate or type,
  opens it in Airmail's composer, and searches, replies to and files your mail.
- Added Pollinations to Image Generation, with Sign in with Pollinations and a
  limited free tier that needs no key.
- Added a hotkey for each app in Settings › Applications that opens it, brings
  it to the front or hides it.
- Changed the Smart Bar's Pin window back to a toolbar button, and list hotkeys
  such as Clipboard History to open the list at once.
- Changed Screen Doodle text to grow and wrap as you type, and fixed Screen
  Doodle closing by itself when the Dock or the menu bar showed.
- Fixed weather, Emoji Picker, the calculator, File Search, Clipboard History,
  the compress commands and Mistral OCR in many cases.

## Pet and Avatar

- Added an Ask box to the Pet: click it, or drop files, text or images on it, to
  ask its Agent, and the answer appears above the Pet.
- Added your own Avatar characters: make a plush in Settings › Avatar, describe
  one for your AI model to draft, or add one from the OpenAvatar gallery.
- Removed the Puppy, Bear and Cat characters; the iPhone and Android apps now
  start with Duet, as the Mac does.

## Models and Sign-ins

- Added GPT-6.1 Terra, OpenAI's new mid-range model, with your own key, a
  ChatGPT sign-in and on Enconvo Cloud, as the OpenAI provider's default.
- Added Kimi K3.1 Pro and K3.1 Pro Highspeed, Moonshot's newest models with a
  1M-token context, on Enconvo Cloud and with your own key.
- Added a subscription's 5-hour and weekly limits to the model picker, and
  clearer sign-in progress in Settings › AI Model.
- Changed agents on Claude Haiku 5.5 to think at Low effort by default, so they
  answer in your language and get dates right more often.
- Fixed LM Studio chats on a model loaded with a short context, Gemini Web
  Search not searching, and ChatGPT plan usage failing to load.

## iPhone and Android

- Changed Settings › API Keys in the iPhone app to Settings › AI Model, as on
  the Mac, with 13 more providers, OpenAI and Anthropic Compatible ones, and
  models added by ID.
- Added sign-ins with your ChatGPT, Grok, Le Chat, GitHub Copilot or Antigravity
  plan, so Phone chats use your plan's models with no API key.
- Added iPhone Apps to Phone chats for events, reminders, contacts, places, Home
  accessories and alarms, and agents on your Mac can ask your iPhone too.
- Added the side panel to iPhone chats, with web pages and, for a Mac chat, its
  Files, Changes, Side chats, MCP Apps, Terminal and Doodle.
- Added Compare answers, chats with a Project's Lead and its Work plan, and a
  Live Activity for a Project at work to the iPhone app.
- Added Nemotron 3.5 Multilingual and SenseVoice Small for Dictation and Live
  Talk, and Kokoro voices on iOS 18 to iOS 26.3, all free and on the iPhone.
- Added iPhone Duo layouts that put chats, docs, sources and files side by side
  on the open inner display.
- Changed iPhone voice calls to a Siri-style look, with light around the
  screen's edge that pulses with the voices.
- Added dropping photos and files on a chat, and sharing files of any size to a
  Mac chat.
- Fixed phones losing the Mac after a short Enconvo relay outage until you
  relaunched Enconvo.

## Usage, Plans and Privacy

- Added an Activity log to Settings › Usage Statistics with every AI request,
  tool call and scheduled task, kept only on your Mac, and fixed cached tokens
  being counted twice.
- Added the renewal terms below every Enconvo Cloud subscribe button, and
  changed the Affiliate program so Cloud points packs no longer earn a
  commission.
- Changed AI-made pages and shared chats to load fonts from Enconvo's servers
  instead of Google Fonts, and shared chat links to leave out steps' details and
  your folder paths.

## Reliability

- Added Submit Diagnostics… to the menu bar menu, which sends recent logs and
  crash reports with personal details taken out, with each report in
  Settings › Diagnostics.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing for seconds when a slow app launched, when you selected
  text in a busy app, at launch, or while your microphone switched.
- Fixed Enconvo quitting when you started Dictation with AirPods, staying
  unresponsive after its engine got stuck, and crashing now and then when you
  sent a message.
- Fixed Enconvo keeping about 250 MB of memory after launch, loading its local
  embedding model after every launch, and Keep Awake staying on after you turned
  it off.
