# Enconvo 2.5.9 Changelog (2026-10-23)

Thanks for all the feedback during the betas. This release gives Enconvo a
Memory that learns from your chats, lets you watch and take over the agent in
your browser and apps, gives Live Talk and Voice Command their own settings with
Enconvo Fast and Quick Actions, lets agents hand work to each other, and
redesigns Recordings with free summaries.

## Highlights

- **Memory that learns from your chats**: Enconvo keeps your preferences, people
  and plans as Memories, tidies them, pins the ones every agent must see, and
  recalls across your chats, recordings and knowledge bases.
- **Watch and take over the agent** in a browser tab or an app window, with a
  glow, a cursor, Take over and Stop, and handoffs for sign-ins, CAPTCHAs and
  payments.
- **Live Talk and Voice Command settings** with Enconvo Fast, free up to $5 a
  day, Fish Audio voices, and Quick Actions you can see and make yourself.
- **Agents calling agents**: any agent, Claude Code and Codex included, can hand
  part of its work to another agent, and AI apps on your Mac can ask your agents
  for work.
- **Recordings redesigned** with free automatic summaries, action items you can
  tick off, a transcript with a player, and Live Translation.
- **Chats that read cleaner**: interactive cards, the agent's work folded behind
  one "Worked for …" line, and a suggested next message.
- **Smart Bar in Liquid Glass**, built-in plugins with their own settings, and
  every hotkey in one place.
- **Phonon, a free voice on your devices**: an English voice from Gradium that
  reads answers aloud and speaks in Live Talk and Voice Command almost at once,
  on Macs with Apple silicon and macOS 15 or later and on iPhones with iOS 18.

## Memory

- Added Memory Review, which keeps your preferences, the people you mention and
  your plans as Memories once a chat or a Live Talk call goes quiet, and updates
  or forgets the ones it changed.
- Added Memory Tidy, which merges duplicates, settles plans whose date has
  passed and tidies tags once a day or once a week.
- Added Pinned Memories, which every agent always sees, such as an allergy or
  how you want to be addressed.
- Added recall across your Memories, past chats, recordings and knowledge bases
  in one search, keeping to the day, week or month you name.
- Added a Memory page in Settings to turn Memory on or off, undo what it learned
  or tidied, choose where agents recall from and pick its model, and a card in
  chat that shows what it learned, with Undo.
- Changed agents on a model running on your Mac to keep your past chats on your
  Mac, without sending passages of them, or Memory Review, to the cloud.

## Live Talk, Voice Command and Quick Actions

- Added Live Talk and Voice Command pages in Settings, with a guide to each, to
  choose how it hears you, the model it talks with and its voice.
- Added Enconvo Fast, the default model for Live Talk and Voice Command: Claude
  Haiku 5.5 with thinking off, which answers right away, sees a photo, your
  camera or a shared screen, and is free up to $5 a day.
- Added Fish Audio as a Text to Speech provider and Live Talk voice, with Drama
  3, S2.1 Pro and S2 Pro, free for now on Enconvo Cloud.
- Added Phonon, a free English voice from Gradium that runs on Macs with Apple
  silicon and macOS 15 or later, for Read Aloud, Live Talk and Voice Command,
  speaking almost at once.
- Added Settings › Quick Actions, which shows what Voice Command and Live Talk
  can run at once, and your own Quick Actions, made from a sentence or from
  built-in actions, Shortcuts, apps, links and scripts.
- Changed ElevenLabs to use Eleven v4 Turbo by default, and added Eleven v4
  Turbo, v3 Conversational and Turbo v2 and v2.5 on Enconvo Cloud, with models
  and voices that refresh without an app update.
- Changed the ElevenLabs sound effects, music, dubbing and voice tools to need
  your own ElevenLabs API key; Enconvo Cloud still covers ElevenLabs speech and
  transcription.
- Changed Live Talk to answer sooner, with Smart Turn for every speech model,
  and added Parakeet and Fluid Speech Mini, which run free and offline on your
  Mac, to the recommended speech models.
- Changed tasks you hand off by voice to start sooner, with the app or web page
  in front already read.
- Fixed Voice Command missing your first words, and voice calls saying they did
  something without starting it, starting a task twice, or answering from an
  outdated state of your Mac.

## Agents in Your Browser and Apps

- Added a glow, a cursor and a control center over the browser tab or app window
  the agent works in, with Take over, Stop and Return to agent.
- Added handoffs for steps only you should take, such as a CAPTCHA, a sign-in, a
  password or a payment: the agent waits, then goes on from where you left it.
- Added browser scripts and App scripts, so the agent goes through long lists,
  feeds and many pages in one step, and stops before anything that sends,
  deletes or buys unless you asked for it.
- Added reading the data a web page loads from its site, so the agent gets exact
  numbers and tables, and remembers how it reached a site's or an app's data for
  next time.
- Changed the agent to treat instructions in web pages, files and emails as
  content, never as commands, and to tell you when one asks for something you
  didn't.
- Changed Browser Use to handle dropdowns, date pickers, menus, rich text
  editors, uploads and pages that load as you scroll far more reliably, in tabs
  behind the one you're looking at too.
- Changed Computer Use to wait until you stop typing or clicking before it acts
  in a window, and to use what an app offers besides its window, such as
  AppleScript commands and URL schemes.

## Workflows and Agents

- Added Judge and Classify steps to workflows, which answer yes or no, or pick
  one of your options, in about a second.
- Added workflows to Browser Use and Computer Use, with a repair agent that does
  a failed step another way so the workflow goes on.
- Added agent calls: any agent, Claude Code, Codex, Grok Build and Antigravity
  included, can ask another agent to do part of its work, in a Permission mode
  no wider than its own, and get the answer back.
- Added Connect AI Clients to the Agents page, so AI apps on your Mac, such as
  Claude Code, Codex CLI, Cursor and Claude Desktop, can ask your agents for
  work over MCP.
- Changed the agent to call its tools directly, so multi-step tasks finish
  noticeably faster.
- Fixed several ways an agent could reach past its Permission mode or skip
  asking before running a script or a command that deletes files.

## Chats

- Added sliders, fields, choices, switches, progress bars, timelines and live
  data to the cards the agent draws in chat.
- Changed a finished agent reply to show only its answer, with its notes,
  thinking and steps folded into one "Worked for …" line, and each step reading
  as one line.
- Added a suggested next message in the empty message box after a reply; press
  Tab to use it.
- Added file links in agent answers that open in the side panel, and one gallery
  for a reply with several images.
- Added a model and reasoning effort picker to new Claude Code and Codex chats.
- Changed the Chat Window to show the agent at the top left, with a menu to
  switch it in a new chat.
- Added the pictures and files an Agent makes to the Dynamic Island, the voice
  reply and the call card as soon as they're done.
- Changed chats to start replying sooner, and Enconvo Cloud replies up to a
  second and a half sooner after a break.

## Recordings and Transcription

- Changed a recording's page to show the transcript with a player that marks
  when each speaker talks, beside the summary, action items, notes and Ask AI.
- Added free automatic summaries of recordings with key points, decisions and
  action items you can tick off.
- Added Transcribe on this Mac for a recording whose transcription stopped, such
  as when your Enconvo Cloud points ran out.
- Added Live Translation to recordings with a model that can translate, such as
  Soniox Realtime.
- Changed the Recordings list to rows grouped by day with a Needs attention
  filter, and the start view to change the microphone, call audio and model in
  place.
- Fixed many issues with renaming recordings, naming speakers, deleting
  recordings and transcribing them again.

## Smart Bar, Plugins and Mini Apps

- Changed the Smart Bar, the Small Smart Bar and the App Sidebar to the look of
  macOS Tahoe's Liquid Glass in Light and Dark Mode, with an Appearance option
  in Settings › Smart Bar.
- Changed list Mini Apps such as Clipboard, File Search and Bookmark Search to
  open at once, search as you type, page through every item, and filter with a
  menu beside the search field.
- Added a Built-in tab to Plugins with settings pages for Clipboard History,
  File Search and Browser Bookmarks, and Shortcut and Alias settings for each
  Mini App.
- Added an on/off switch to installed plugins, and switches for where each
  plugin's views show up.
- Fixed File Search taking seconds for each letter without Full Disk Access;
  results now show as you type.
- Changed Smart Bar search to rank what you use higher.

## Hotkeys and AI Commands

- Added Other Hotkeys and Find by hotkey to Settings › Shortcut, which list
  every hotkey and what uses a key combination, with a clash warning and Use it
  here.
- Changed every hotkey field to one recorder that takes key combinations,
  modifier keys pressed together, and Hold, Tap, Tap, Hold or Double Tap.
- Changed Enconvo's default global shortcuts to only Smart Bar, Chat Window,
  Dictation, Voice Command and App Sidebar.
- Added All Commands to Settings › AI Commands: one model for every AI Command,
  writing tool, PopBar button and Dictation's Polish and Translate, set to
  Claude Haiku 5.5 on Enconvo Cloud.
- Changed AI Commands and writing tools to answer in about a second, keep your
  text's language and reply with the result alone.
- Fixed the PopBar not showing for text selected in Google Docs and other web
  editors that draw their pages on a canvas.

## Dictation

- Added a choice of where Dictation pastes when you switch apps while dictating.
- Fixed custom Dictionary words with Parakeet, and pressing another key while
  holding the Dictation key cancelling the dictation.
- Fixed the first Dictation after Enconvo opens starting late, and stray symbols
  while a terminal app was in front.

## Dynamic Island and Avatars

- Added a task list to the Dynamic Island: a Tasks chip opens a list grouped
  into Needs you, Failed, Running and Done.
- Changed the Dynamic Island settings to Home, Opening & Display and Character
  tabs, with Home shortcuts you can drag, rename and add.
- Changed the onboarding guide's host to Duet, Enconvo's own character, with an
  upbeat new voice.
- Added Live-Talk Voice to the Avatar's right-click menu, and the Avatar now
  acts on Voice Command replies and does the motions you ask for right away.

## Models and Local Models

- Added Mercury 3 on Enconvo Cloud, Inception's diffusion model that writes over
  1,000 tokens a second.
- Added Reasoning Effort for Mistral Large 4, Medium 3.5 and Small 4, and fixed
  Mistral's model list and Large 4 chats.
- Removed GPT-OSS 120B, GPT-OSS 20B and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite from Enconvo
  Cloud; Mistral Small and MiniMax M3.1 Flash are the free models.
- Added a Local Models page in Settings that shows what the models on your Mac
  are doing, with Pause, Run everything in the cloud, and a choice of what reads
  text in images.
- Added Settings › Memory › Search, where the search index can be built free on
  Enconvo Cloud instead of your Mac's GPU, and the knowledge base index upgrade
  no longer keeps the GPU busy for hours.
- Changed local model downloads to ask whether to switch to a model once it
  finishes.
- Changed the Enconvo Cloud model list to check for new models and prices every
  hour.

## Mac Apps

- Changed Calendar, Contacts, Mail, Messages, Music, Notes, Reminders and Maps
  into separate plugins on the Mac Apps tab, each turned on or off on its own.

## iPhone and Android

- Added a Side chat and Live Talk settings to the iPhone app, with Parakeet,
  Fluid Speech Mini and Phonon hearing and speaking on the iPhone itself.
- Added Start a Voice Chat for the Action Button, the Shortcuts app and Control
  Center on iOS 18, without opening the app.
- Added a viewfinder and camera control to Share Camera in iPhone voice chats.
- Changed Mac chats on the iPhone to fold the agent's work behind "Worked for …"
  and offer a suggested next message, as on the Mac.
- Changed agent approvals and questions on the iPhone to arrive as Time
  Sensitive notifications, and on iPhone and Android to show where they were
  answered once you answer them elsewhere.

## Reliability

- Added crash and freeze reports on the Mac and iPhone while sharing analytics
  is on, without any of your content.
- Fixed Enconvo freezing until you restarted it when its background engine
  stopped.
- Fixed Retry deleting a chat's earlier messages, Clear All deleting archived
  chats, and settings and API keys being erased when saved at the same moment.
- Changed the built-in Node.js runtime to Node.js 26.12.1, with the latest
  security fixes.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.8 Changelog (2026-10-19)

Thanks for all the feedback during the betas. This release brings a brand-new
side panel to every chat, adds Codex, Grok Build and Antigravity beside Claude
Code, and rebuilds Local LLM into a fast on-device model engine.

## Highlights

- **A brand-new side panel** in every chat, with tabs for files, web pages, a
  terminal, Side chats and the agent's changes.
- **Codex, Grok Build and Antigravity** join Claude Code as coding agents, and
  the new Orchestrator Board runs coding tasks for a Project.
- **Local LLM (MLX)**, a powerful, fast on-device model engine with Hugging
  Face model search, live memory and speed charts, and local models that answer
  questions about your documents in seconds.
- **Mini Apps in the Smart Bar**, including Clipboard History, File Search and
  Browser Bookmarks.
- **Sync with iCloud** for paid members, and API keys that reach a new Mac
  without a passphrase.
- **Plugin Studio** builds plugins with you in a chat, and plugins from the
  store now update themselves.
- **New providers**: Antigravity, GitHub Copilot, OpenRouter speech, Mistral
  account sign-in and MiniMax M3.1 Flash.

## Automations

- Changed Scheduled to Automations, with filters, a Run box for tasks you start
  by hand, and switches for Important mail alerts.
- Added triggers for folders, apps, Apple Mail and Gmail, Discord and Telegram
  messages, MCP server events and transcribed recordings.
- Added web page and feed, clipboard, calendar, meeting, login and webhook
  triggers, and Mac triggers for waking, unlocking, plugging in and time away.
- Added conditions you describe in your own words, which AI checks for each
  event, even in a screenshot or scanned PDF, with Try on recent events.
- Added running automations by hand from the SmartBar, the PopBar and buttons on
  the Dynamic Island, which also shows each run's progress.
- Added an Approval step to workflows that waits up to 24 hours for your answer,
  and a step-by-step view of each run with Retry from the failed step.
- Added retries for steps that only read or ask a model, grouping of events that
  come close together, an hourly limit of 20 runs, and a loop guard.
- Added a Needs you card for failed events, pausing after 5 failed runs in a
  row, and a workflow of your choice to run when an automation fails.
- Added Make it run the same way every time, which turns a reliable agent
  automation into a workflow, and Undo for changes made in chat.

## Coding Agents and Orchestrator

- Added Codex as a coding agent with your ChatGPT sign-in, its own models,
  reasoning effort and permission modes, and your plan's usage limits.
- Added subagents, file change cards with Undo, background commands, generated
  images, `!` commands, /compact, /review, /goal and forking to Codex chats.
- Added Grok Build and Google Antigravity as coding agents, and Enconvo's skills
  and actions in Codex, Grok Build and Antigravity chats.
- Added one-click Install and Update for Claude Code, Codex and Grok Build
  without Terminal, with your account, plan and usage on their settings pages.
- Added a project's Codex, Grok Build and Antigravity sessions to the new-chat
  page, including ones started in a terminal, to continue as chats.
- Added Continue in Terminal or iTerm to the ⋯ menu, and Copy command for any
  other terminal, in Claude Code, Codex and Grok Build chats.
- Added the Orchestrator Board, which plans a Project's tasks and runs each
  coding task in its own worktree, to merge or open as a pull request.

## Chat and Side Panel

- Added a brand-new side panel to the Chat Window, Smart Bar and Dynamic
  Island, with tabs for Files, web pages, a Terminal and Side chats.
- Added Side chats with /btw for a quick question while the chat keeps working,
  and a Changes tab with Undo for the files each turn changed.
- Added web pages in the side panel that the agent can open and use to check its
  work, asking you before paying, sending, deleting or publishing.
- Added diagrams to chat replies and Markdown previews, drawing Mermaid blocks
  in the chat's colors with a button to switch to the code.
- Added web page cards for HTML pages in replies, HTML previews that run their
  scripts, and editing a file's source in the side panel with ⌘S.
- Added a conversation map along the edge of long chats, a mic button in the
  Chat Window's message box, and Up to bring back what you sent before.
- Changed chat search to return results several times faster without missing
  older chats, and added output speed to each reply's Details.

## Smart Bar, PopBar and Mini Apps

- Added Mini Apps: plugin apps open in the Smart Bar with a back button and an
  Actions menu (⌘K), and plugins can show searchable lists there.
- Added File Search and Browser Bookmarks Mini Apps, and Clipboard History now
  opens as a Mini App with ⌘1–⌘9 to paste.
- Added Auto-switch Input Source to the Smart Bar, and removed its Use
  Application as Context First and Shake to Dynamic Island options.
- Changed the Smart Bar to list your recent commands and apps near the top, find
  apps by names in other languages, and offer Quit App and Force Quit.
- Fixed the PopBar hotkey to hide the PopBar when pressed again and show it
  under the selected text, and Pinyin input in the PopBar's Ask field.

## Computer Use

- Changed agent mode to act in a Mac app's window right away and see only what
  changed after each step, so desktop tasks start sooner and use fewer tokens.
- Added right-clicks to the Computer and Browser Operators, and a screenshot
  fallback for controls and lists an app shows only on screen.
- Fixed Computer Use in Spotify and other web-based apps, so menus, drop-downs,
  sliders, checkboxes and long lists work as asked.
- Fixed Computer Use typing into background apps such as the App Store and into
  spreadsheet cells, and reporting actions as done when nothing happened.
- Removed the Computer Use and Browser Use MCP servers for other AI apps such as
  Claude Code, Codex and Cursor, along with Connect AI Clients (MCP).

## Dynamic Island, Read Aloud and Avatars

- Added activity chips to the Dynamic Island's Home for downloads, Read Aloud,
  working Agents and waiting questions, and two activities on the notch.
- Changed Read Aloud to open its controls when you click the notch, hold its
  place during a Voice Command or call, and leave the Avatar uncovered.
- Added Natural lip sync on Apple silicon Macs and the iPhone, an on-device
  model that moves an Avatar's lips and tongue with the voice she speaks.

## Voice and Dictation

- Changed Voice Command on the Mac to open apps, run quick actions and start
  browser searches as soon as you finish speaking, two or three at a time.
- Added browser search to Mac voice calls, and calls now find apps by names in
  other languages or the names people call them.
- Fixed Voice Command talking over you when you speak again, and voice calls
  pausing when Enconvo Cloud's ElevenLabs voice runs out of credits.
- Changed On-Device Cleanup to Enconvo Tidy 1, which also cleans up Chinese,
  Japanese, Korean, German, French, Spanish, Portuguese and Italian.
- Changed On-Device Cleanup to work on macOS 14 too, and removed its Tone
  setting and the Spokenly Cleanup 1 download, freeing about 730 MB.
- Fixed Dictation in Pages moving the cursor, crashes when Dictation read
  Enconvo's own window, and permission alerts hiding behind other windows.

## Recordings and Downloads

- Changed meeting recording to happen on Settings › Recordings, with a live
  transcript, Ask AI during the meeting, notes, bookmarks and Pause.
- Added an Audio sources window for the microphone, call audio and transcription
  model, a no-sound warning, and WeChat calls in the Meeting reminder.
- Added video and audio download progress to the Dynamic Island, with Cancel, a
  percent ring on the notch and Show in Finder when done.
- Changed video downloads to start in half the time, fetch long videos up to
  three times faster, save to chat history and reuse a file you already have.
- Fixed YouTube links from a playlist or Mix downloading the whole list.

## Local Models and Knowledge Base

- Changed the MLX provider into Local LLM, a powerful, fast built-in engine for
  macOS 15 and later, with live memory and speed charts and a best model picked
  for your Mac.
- Added Hugging Face model search, Bonsai 2 models with vision, Thinking and
  Unload when idle, and Qwen3.5 4B as the default local model.
- Changed local model downloads to resume after a restart, retry on network
  drops and check free disk space before they start.
- Changed the local Anthropic and Responses endpoints to read PDFs, follow a
  JSON schema, and answer Claude Code on a local model much faster.
- Changed chat to search a long knowledge base item or recording you @-mention,
  so local models answer in seconds, and follow-ups keep searching it.
- Fixed agents on local models losing their tools, and knowledge base answers
  switching to English or guessing when a search timed out.

## Models and Enconvo Cloud

- Added Antigravity and GitHub Copilot providers with Google or GitHub sign-in.
- Added Mistral account sign-in for Mistral AI, Voxtral and Mistral OCR.
- Added OpenRouter as a text-to-speech and speech-to-text provider, so its
  speech models work with one OpenRouter API key.
- Added MiniMax M3.1 Flash on Enconvo Cloud and with your own key, at half the
  price of MiniMax M3 and about three times faster.
- Changed provider settings to show API Key, Subscription and Enconvo Cloud as
  sign-in cards, with a Cloud points button on Enconvo Cloud providers.
- Added DeepSeek to Web Search and Web Fetch; past the free monthly quota,
  Enconvo Cloud search uses it at 35 points per search with results.
- Added Enconvo Cloud to the agent's ElevenLabs tools, such as sound effects,
  music and dubbing, so they work without your own ElevenLabs key.
- Removed the Temperature setting from every AI model provider, and Gemini AI
  OAuth and Qwen account sign-in, which now take an API key.
- Removed the Japanese voices from MLX Kokoro, which always failed to speak.

## Plugins and Connectors

- Added Plugin Studio, where Create plugin opens a chat that builds plugins and
  PopBar extensions with you, ready to use or publish to the plugin store.
- Added automatic updates for plugins from the store, GitHub, npm, marketplaces
  and the PopBar catalog, holding back ones that change hooks you allowed.
- Added Comments and Report to plugin store pages, and review status, Publish
  again and Withdraw to the plugins you published.
- Added entry points for plugins with their own interface in the Chat Window
  sidebar, the side panel, SmartBar search, @ and the PopBar.
- Added a Location & Maps plugin for travel times, when to leave and places
  nearby or anywhere, using Apple Maps with no API key.
- Changed uninstalling a plugin to also delete its data, unless you add
  `--keep-data` to `enconvo plugin uninstall`.
- Removed installing .dxt files; MCP bundles now install only as .mcpb files.
- Added about 300 new Connectors, including Greenhouse, GitBook, DigitalOcean,
  Runway, Luma, Kling AI and Canny.

## Sync and Settings

- Added iCloud as a Sync storage option for paid members, with changes from your
  other Macs often arriving within seconds.
- Changed API key sync so a new computer is approved from one that already has
  your keys with a six-digit code, instead of a passphrase.
- Fixed Sync losing edits made while it was off, bringing back deleted items,
  and replacing newer settings or API keys with older copies.
- Changed Settings search to open results without a pause, keep typing smooth,
  and rank pages and whole-word matches first.
- Fixed web pages shown inside Enconvo being able to send commands to the app,
  and plugin names or synced settings changing files outside Enconvo's folders.

## iPhone and Android

- Added Codex chats to the iPhone app, and a project's Codex sessions to the
  iPhone and Android apps, including ones started in a terminal.
- Added message search to Search chats in the iPhone app, finding what was said
  in your iPhone's and Mac's chats, with previews.
- Added hiding the Avatar during voice chats on iPhone and Android, and web page
  cards that open HTML pages full screen in the iPhone app.
- Changed Mac chats in the iPhone app to keep typing and sending through a brief
  connection drop, and to show your first message the moment you tap Send.
- Fixed text in the iPhone app showing in English in other languages, across
  chat lists, search, settings, pairing, errors and VoiceOver labels.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.7 Changelog (2026-10-15)

Thanks for your patience and for all the feedback that shaped this release. It
makes voice calls and Dictation far more capable, rebuilds the PopBar, and
helps Enconvo reach you at the right moment, at your Mac and on your iPhone.

## Highlights

- **Agents choose how to run each message** with the new Auto run mode, while
  Auto Route picks the model for each kind of work.
- **Voice calls that get things done**, from playing music and adding reminders
  to looking things up and controlling your Mac.
- **A smarter Dictation** that edits text by voice, learns the words it
  mishears, and runs offline with Fluid Speech Mini.
- **Updates waits until you're free**, holding agent questions and finished
  tasks while you work.
- **A rebuilt PopBar with extensions**, AI Commands you create by describing
  them, and one-click saving to your knowledge base.
- **Ask Enconvo in your browser** about the page, post or video you're on, now
  in Chrome, Firefox and Safari.
- **Claude Code chats** in the Chat Window, on the Mac and iPhone.

## Agents and Chat

- Added an Auto run mode for Agents, now the default, which answers quick
  questions directly and runs tasks with tools, replacing the Run Mode switch.
- Changed Agents settings to a card gallery where agents can be paused and
  resumed, and removed the Tools picker and Tutor Mode.
- Changed Steer to answer your new message right away, and added Cmd+Enter to
  steer every queued message into the running task.
- Changed agents to find your files by name in about a second, open the pages
  you name straight away, and ask before changing settings you didn't mention.
- Added recommended answers to agent questions, chosen after three minutes.
- Added Rename, Pin, Fork and Archive to the Chat Window's ⋯ menu, a Details
  button under each reply, and Copy as Rich Text, Markdown or Plain Text.
- Added Response Language to Settings › General, so agents, voice calls and the
  Avatar always reply in the language you choose.
- Changed My Profile to About You, now under Agents in Settings.
- Changed shared chat links to look the same as the chat in Enconvo, with
  charts, citations, media and tool steps that open as in the app.
- Fixed agents switching to English after using tools, replies showing words
  twice, and attachments with unusual file names.
- Fixed the chat history looking empty after an update until Enconvo restarted.

## Voice Calls and Voice Command

- Changed voice calls to run on Enconvo Voice, which hears you with Soniox and
  answers in an expressive ElevenLabs voice with live captions.
- Added 42 ElevenLabs voices in 12 languages for calls, Read Aloud and Avatars,
  and each Agent now speaks in its Avatar's voice.
- Changed voice calls to search the web, read files, control your Mac and
  handle music, reminders and notes on their own, several at once.
- Changed Mac voice calls to work like the iPhone's, with words that appear as
  you speak and Screen Doodle and Live Screen sent to the call.
- Removed Auto Hang-Up, so a quiet call naps until you speak instead of ending.
- Changed voice calls to remember much longer conversations and recognize the
  words in your Dictation dictionary.
- Added spoken answers to push-to-talk Voice Command, which knows which app
  you're using when you speak.
- Changed Voice Command and voice call replies to be shorter and arrive sooner.
- Changed voice calls to try what you ask before saying it can't be done, doing
  it in the browser or handing it to the Agent.
- Fixed voice calls claiming a task was done without doing it, missing your
  corrections, giving tasks the wrong date, and ending when speech recognition
  dropped.

## Dictation and Recording

- Added Edit selected text by voice, so you can select text and say how to
  change it, such as "make this more formal".
- Added Paste Last Dictation, Heard as entries and suggestions in the
  Dictionary, and an Overview in Dictation History.
- Added App Rules for the post action, language and Return key in each app or
  website, plus a Dictation Language setting.
- Added Fluid Speech Mini as an on-device English transcription model on macOS
  15 and later, for dictation and live captions.
- Added On-Device Cleanup, a post action that removes filler words and fixes
  punctuation offline on Apple silicon Macs with macOS 15 and later.
- Changed Settings › Dictation to follow the steps of a dictation, with Clean
  up options whose prompts and models you can edit, or add your own.
- Changed Dictation to show your words sooner, stay ready for five minutes, and
  keep the text on the clipboard when no field is focused.
- Changed Silence other audio while recording to pause music and videos and
  resume them when you finish.
- Added recording from second-channel microphones, and meeting recordings now
  continue when the microphone changes.

## Updates and Notifications

- Added Updates, which holds agent questions, approvals and finished tasks
  while you're busy and sends them to your iPhone once you leave the Mac.
- Added Pause Updates for 1 Hour and Quiet Hours to the menu bar and
  Settings › Notifications.
- Added Approve and Deny buttons to approval notifications, and changed the
  Dynamic Island to wait for you instead of opening for a question.

## SmartBar, PopBar and AI Commands

- Changed the SmartBar to show results sooner, redraw far less and follow the
  app language throughout.
- Added PopBar extensions in PopClip's format from Enconvo's reviewed catalog,
  a link, a file or your PopClip.
- Changed the PopBar with a searchable More panel, an Ask panel for any AI
  Command, info buttons, button groups and a result card.
- Added Save to Knowledge Base as a default PopBar button, with its progress on
  the Dynamic Island and links saved as the page itself.
- Added New AI Command from a description, Customize for the built-in commands,
  and Try it on your own text.
- Changed Translator to join the Writing Toolkit and follow your Response
  Language, with Source and Target pickers.
- Removed the DeepL, Youdao, Caiyun and Translator (Interactive) commands, the
  AI Command template store, and AI Edit on the PopBar.
- Added spoken labels to Screen Doodle for the shapes you draw.
- Changed Screenshot OCR to open its result on the Dynamic Island.

## Dynamic Island, Read Aloud and Avatars

- Changed Read Aloud to play on the Dynamic Island one sentence at a time, with
  captions and speed and voice pickers.
- Added reading aloud by the Avatar, in her own voice with lip sync.
- Added eighteen built-in Avatar characters on the Mac and iPhone, including
  six Enconvo plush originals led by Duet, the new default.
- Added dances, motions, outfits and working moods to the Avatar characters,
  plus head pats and pointer tracking on the Mac.
- Added Island Character, which shows your Avatar in the compact Dynamic
  Island, and an All Screens option, now the default.

## Music and Mac Apps

- Added Spotify and Apple Music plugins, so agents and voice calls can find,
  play and organize your music without signing in.
- Added a Mac Apps tab to Plugins, and Spotify and Apple Music buttons on the
  Dynamic Island that listen for what to play.
- Changed Dance Along with Music to be on by default.
- Fixed Play/Pause and track controls doing nothing, and Spotify taking up to
  25 seconds to play a song by name.

## Browser Extension and Knowledge Base

- Added Ask Enconvo to the browser extension on X, Reddit, YouTube, Gmail,
  Google Docs, GitHub, Wikipedia and many more sites.
- Added Firefox and Safari support, and video and audio downloads from YouTube,
  X, Bilibili, TikTok, TED and Vimeo.
- Added an Enconvo button to X's composer for polishing, shortening and
  replying, and AI reply suggestions on Reddit and Facebook.
- Changed saving posts on X to use X's own bookmark button, with automatic
  categories for posts saved from any site.
- Added Tags to the knowledge base, with Suggest categories, Manage Tags, an
  Untagged filter and a filter bar.

## Plugins, Connectors and Computer Use

- Added about 300 new Connectors, including AWS, Microsoft 365, Google Maps,
  Tableau, Zoho CRM and Sonos.
- Added installing Codex and Claude Code plugins from GitHub, npm, a folder or
  a marketplace, with their commands, agents and hooks.
- Added `enconvo plugin` commands to validate, pack and publish plugins.
- Added a Cloudflare CLI plugin and the EmbeddingGemma 2 local model.
- Changed install links to show what they would install before you confirm.
- Changed Computer Use to ask before pressing Delete, Send or Buy, hide
  password fields, and report actions that didn't take effect.
- Fixed a crash on macOS 27 when Computer Use read Enconvo's own windows.
- Removed the Straico plugin and its providers.

## Claude Code

- Added Claude Code to the Chat Window, running the Claude Code installed on
  your Mac in any folder, with your Claude subscription or an API key.
- Added Plan mode, bash mode, background tasks, Fast mode, a context meter,
  slash commands and clickable file references to Claude Code chats.
- Added your Claude Code sessions to Enconvo, including ones started in
  Terminal, with Fork and Continue in Terminal or iTerm.
- Added Claude Code chats to the iPhone app, with the same steps and plan card.

## Models and Usage

- Added Auto Route, the new default model, which runs each message on the model
  you set for that kind of work.
- Added Claude Haiku 5.5, now the default for Enconvo Cloud and Anthropic.
- Added Gemini 4 Argon, half price on Enconvo Cloud through November 30, 2026.
- Changed the model picker to two columns, with a Fast mode chip for supported
  models that runs them faster at a higher price.
- Changed Manage Models to add models in a form, restore deleted models, and
  load OpenRouter models faster.
- Changed the quick decisions behind Auto Route, Auto run mode and Memory to
  use no Cloud points.
- Changed Live Talk voice calls and Soniox dictation to be free for Cloud Plan,
  Lifetime and trial members.
- Added HeyGen Voice on Enconvo Cloud for Chinese and English speech.

## iPhone and Android

- Added a Dictation page to iPhone Settings with Soniox and two offline models,
  Fluid Speech Mini and Parakeet TDT V3.
- Added Fast mode to the iPhone model sheet, and Read Aloud now uses the
  ElevenLabs voice picked on the Mac.
- Fixed notifications sent while the iPhone was disconnected being lost.
- Added Continue with Google back to the iPhone and Android apps, and account
  deletion to Android.

## Account and Teams

- Added a one-time 7-day Plus trial for Free accounts with no card needed, and
  trial codes for 30 days of Plus Cloud.
- Changed Teams so each Seat goes to a teammate's own account, managed by the
  admin from a new Teams page.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.6 Changelog (2026-10-05)

Thanks for your patience and all the feedback during the beta. I've spent this
release making Enconvo feel more connected: to your apps, your other Macs, and
the things you want your assistant to remember.

## Highlights

- **Talk to an Avatar or use Voice Command hands-free**, with spoken replies,
  live captions, screen awareness, and voice calls from Dynamic Island.
- **Pick up where you left off on another Mac** with Sync for chats, files,
  knowledge bases, Agents, workflows, skills, and preferences.
- **Connect your everyday apps** so your Agent can work with email, calendars,
  documents, and other services without switching between windows.
- **Ask AI about your documents, recordings, and saved posts**, with answers
  that cite the passage or moment they came from.
- **Let Agents work together** on independent tasks, with clearer progress,
  permissions, and control over changes to your files.
- **Build and schedule workflows by describing them**, then follow each step
  in a visual editor with an Assistant to help fix failed runs.
- **See where your AI usage goes**, including estimated cost, tokens, cache
  savings, subscription limits, and response speed.
- **Run local MLX models on your Mac**, or use your API keys, supported AI
  subscriptions, and Enconvo Cloud from one model picker.

## Voice and Avatars

- Added desktop Avatars with their own appearance, voice, expressions, and
  gestures, including Puppy, Bear, and Cat.
- Added voice calls from Chat Window, SmartBar, and Dynamic Island, with live
  transcripts, screen and camera sharing, and optional automatic hang-up.
- Added spoken Voice Command replies with captions that follow the speech.
- Changed Dynamic Island to keep your words, the Agent's progress, and its
  answer visible while a voice task runs.
- Added Avatar appearance and motion controls, saved looks, and a lighter
  rendering mode that reduces resource use.
- Fixed voice calls losing their chat context, ending while waiting for your
  answer, or failing to pass along photos and files.
- Fixed Dictation startup, post-actions, recording recovery, and local speech
  model loading.
- Added richer Screen Doodle controls with more reliable screenshot and
  annotation handoff.

## Sync and Memory

- Added Sync across Macs for chats and their attachments, Projects, knowledge
  bases, settings, AI commands, Agents, workflows, skills, and snippets.
- Added optional API key sync, encrypted with a passphrase and off by default.
- Changed Sync to recover more quickly after a Mac reconnects and to explain
  files or settings that stay on one Mac.
- Changed Sync to keep new chats flowing when storage fills by removing the
  oldest cloud copies while preserving copies already on your Macs.
- Changed Memory to recall relevant preferences and older facts more reliably,
  update information that has changed, and handle dates more accurately.
- Changed Memory settings to use the same card and list views as Knowledge Base.
- Fixed Quick Memory and voice-note saving, transcription, and cancellation.

## Knowledge Base and Saved Posts

- Added Ask AI alongside documents, books, recordings, and saved posts, with
  citations that open the relevant passage or playback position.
- Changed knowledge base search to find exact names and codes as well as
  related ideas, with clearer source references.
- Added saving X posts and threads, including images, into your knowledge base.
- Added readable saved-post views, video transcripts, and questions about
  individual posts or collections.
- Added a Knowledge Base MCP server for searching your saved material from
  Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and other compatible apps.
- Fixed importing, previewing, and searching documents and recordings.

## Agents and Everyday Automation

- Added parallel subagents for independent tasks, with support for project
  helpers and visible progress.
- Added per-Agent skill selection, shared and workspace skills, and safer
  folder imports and deletion.
- Added permission choices, task checklists, file-change undo, message editing,
  and conversation forks.
- Added faster Browser Operator and Computer Operator modes that pause before
  sensitive actions.
- Fixed a conversation with a custom Agent unexpectedly switching to the
  default Agent while a task was running.
- Fixed typing, scrolling, file picking, browser tab control, and interrupted
  tool requests across Computer Use and Browser Use.
- Changed tools on Mac to use Enconvo's bundled Node.js for a more dependable
  environment, even when another Node.js is installed.

## Connectors and MCP

- Added Connectors for Gmail, Google services, Outlook, Notion, Slack, and more
  everyday work tools, with guided account setup.
- Changed Agents to recognize your connected accounts so requests such as
  "email me" or "add it to my calendar" use the right account.
- Added local-file attachments to emails sent or drafted with Gmail and
  Outlook, with download links for files too large for an email.
- Added interactive MCP Apps inside chat and clearer server and tool controls.
- Fixed Gmail migration, account sign-in, credential refresh, and reconnection.

## Workflows and Scheduled Tasks

- Added an Assistant that creates and edits workflows from a description,
  with templates and workflow YAML import.
- Added a searchable step palette, recently used tools, clearer step cards,
  run summaries, and individual step results.
- Added input forms, value pickers, and warnings for missing or unavailable
  inputs, with an Assistant to help fix failed steps.
- Changed the Workflows page to a list and details view with recent runs,
  scheduling, and quick actions.
- Added setting up scheduled tasks by chatting, with plain-language schedules,
  run history, notifications, and delivery to connected chats.
- Added scheduling saved workflows with their own inputs and links back to
  each run.

## Chat and Sharing

- Added public or password-protected chat links and Continue in Enconvo to
  copy a shared conversation into the app.
- Added Share and Copy menus to Chat Window, SmartBar, and Dynamic Island,
  including Copy as Markdown and chat links.
- Changed new shared chat links to open at app.enconvo.com in the reader's
  language while keeping earlier links working.
- Fixed chat scrolling over code blocks, tables, and wide content, including
  on shared chat pages.
- Changed long conversations to stream more smoothly, with clearer code
  highlighting, message queues, and history navigation.
- Fixed pinning chats, restoring empty chats, and sidebar chat names.

## Models and Usage

- Added support during this release for GPT-6.1 Sol, Claude Opus 5.5 and
  Sonnet 5.5, GLM-5.3 models, and other supported provider updates.
- Added an MLX Models page for downloading, loading, and choosing local models,
  with OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible endpoints for other apps.
- Added prompt caching for local MLX models and more reliable cancellation.
- Added Response Timeout settings to LM Studio and Ollama, with no time limit
  by default for local models.
- Added Usage Statistics across AI providers with estimated costs, token and
  cache breakdowns, model details, and a request log.
- Added clearer credential labels and subscription usage in the model picker.
- Added Cloud points visibility per conversation and spending checkpoints
  during long Agent runs.
- Added more image, video, and speech providers, including ElevenLabs voices
  through Enconvo Cloud.

## Setup and Companion

- Added a shorter narrated first-launch guide, settings search, and expanded
  interface languages.
- Added pairing with the iPhone Companion to continue Mac chats, answer Agent
  questions, and receive task notifications.
- Changed the iPhone Companion experience with native chats, voice calls,
  Avatars, and settings shared with your Mac.
- Changed iPhone Companion notifications about activity on your Mac to play
  the alert sound.
- Changed the free plan to monthly allowances for Recording, Live Captioning,
  and Live Talk, with clearer limits for scheduled tasks and connected devices.
- Changed Sync to require an Enconvo Cloud plan or lifetime license.
- Changed the Mac app to use Enconvo's company signing identity; when updating
  from 2.5.5, macOS will ask you to grant permissions such as Accessibility,
  Screen Recording, and Microphone again.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.5 Changelog (2026-09-10)

Friends, thanks for waiting and for all your feedback along the way. I've spent
this release making Enconvo easier to reach, more useful in your daily work,
and smoother from the first voice command to the finished result.

## Highlights

- **Dynamic Island feels faster and more capable**, with a live voice HUD,
  immediate recording, richer attachments, and easier Default Agent switching.
- **Meetings and live captions are easier to follow**, with speaker recognition,
  word-by-word playback, live translation, and local transcription models.
- **CoreML dictation runs on your Mac's Neural Engine**, with smaller downloads,
  lower memory use, and fast on-device speech recognition.
- **Build workflows with your AI Agent**, combining Enconvo features and MCP
  tools in a visual editor with live progress and step-by-step results.
- **Projects keep related work together**, bringing chats, files, and shared
  instructions into a single workspace.
- **Knowledge Base has a new asset browser**, while Quick Memory lets you save
  app content or voice notes with the right Shift key.
- **DeepSeek V4.1 Flash is now supported** through your own DeepSeek API key or
  Enconvo Cloud, with image understanding, a 1M-token context window, and tool use.
- **Enconvo Cloud gives you more DeepSeek usage**: Pro uses half the standard
  points and Max uses one quarter for all DeepSeek models.

## Dynamic Island And Everyday Interaction

- Added a live Voice Command HUD with a waveform, transcript, and clear
  recording and processing states.
- Improved hotkey response so recording starts immediately and captures your
  first words while the panel opens.
- Added screenshots, file attachments, Screen Doodle, and automatic read-aloud
  controls to Dynamic Island.
- Added Default Agent switching from the agent icon in SmartBar, Chat Window,
  and the Dynamic Island panel.
- Fixed commands returning their results to the wrong window.
- Improved App Sidebar opening, window following, closing, and remembered width
  for each app.
- Added Inline Mode for applying commands directly to selected text, with a
  compact progress indicator and the result pasted back in place.
- Improved SmartBar input, Chinese text composition, command ordering, and
  PopBar behavior around window dragging and design apps.

## Voice, Recording, And Live Captioning

- Added CoreML Parakeet, SenseVoice, and Paraformer dictation models with
  download, load, unload, and delete controls.
- Added speaker diarization and recognition to meeting recording, with
  word-level transcript alignment during playback.
- Added Live Captioning with realtime transcripts, speaker labels, translation,
  local model support, and saved history.
- Fixed Voice Command on macOS 27, Dictation post-actions, and local speech
  model loading.
- Improved recording sounds, volume restoration, dictation translation, and
  the way spoken input merges with your typed edits.
- Improved Read Aloud reliability and refreshed xAI voice selection.

## Workflows, Agents, And Visual Results

- Added a visual workflow builder with step configuration, live execution
  status, individual step runs, and YAML editing.
- Added AI-assisted workflow creation and editing using Enconvo capabilities,
  MCP tools, and supported VS Code tools.
- Added the Visualize plugin for SVG, HTML previews, and interactive widgets
  inside chat, alongside native tables, charts, weather cards, and other
  generated results.
- Improved workflow cancellation and output so runs are easier to follow and
  stop.
- Added immediate Steer messages so you can correct an active Agent task while
  its tools continue running, including messages from Telegram and Discord.
- Improved long-conversation memory, model switching, prompt caching, and
  cancellation of stopped requests.
- Added settings to open Dynamic Island or send a system notification when a
  task finishes, with fewer duplicate prompts for conversations already visible.

## Projects, Knowledge, And App Context

- Added Projects with shared instructions, folder-backed chats, a file browser,
  and Git branch controls.
- Added file and folder mentions from your current project, alongside workflow,
  MCP server, Finder file, Notes, and Mail context.
- Redesigned Knowledge Base with a folder tree, previews, tags, an inspector,
  drag-and-drop imports, and a standalone window.
- Added Quick Memory: double-tap right Shift to save the current app context,
  or hold right Shift to record a voice note for your knowledge base.
- Improved imports and retrieval for PDFs, images, and larger knowledge bases.
- Added app presets and site Command Packs for more direct automation, plus
  improved Office, Finder, Browser Use, and Computer Use support.
- Added inline attachment previews and live progress for video and audio
  downloads.

## Models And Cloud Plans

- Expanded model choice with GPT-6 Astra, the GPT-5.6 family, Grok 4.6,
  Gemini 3.8 Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash, Kimi K3, MiniMax M3, and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash
  through their supported providers.
- Added GPT Image 2.5 Flare and Sunburst for image creation and editing, with
  support for API keys, Enconvo Cloud, and supported ChatGPT sign-in workflows.
- Added Google Gemini and Kie.ai video-generation options and expanded xAI
  voice, image, video, and search support.
- Improved custom provider model lists, fuzzy model search, and saved choices.
- Added clearer Cloud plan comparisons and upgrades, with DeepSeek usage at
  half the standard point rate on Pro and one quarter on Max.
- Redesigned Points Usage with clearer balances, usage breakdowns, and monthly
  allowance reset history.

## Settings And Reliability

- Added new Skills, Plugins, MCP Servers, and Permissions management pages.
- Improved MCP setup, OAuth sign-in, credential refresh, and reconnection.
- Added account device management and clearer recovery when you reach your
  device limit or move to a new Mac.
- Added the Pet desktop companion with live task cards and community packs.
- Added a help menu for companion setup, release notes, and keyboard shortcuts.
- Fixed high background CPU usage, slow app switching, Dock icon hiding, and
  workspace preview close controls.
- Improved chat syntax highlighting, scrolling, panel transitions, and startup
  performance throughout the app.

# Enconvo 2.5.4 Changelog (2026-06-26)

Hi friends, thanks for testing this round. Enconvo 2.5.4 is mostly about making
the app feel cleaner and steadier in daily use: Dynamic Island is easier to
control, Sidebar handoff is quicker, chat rendering is smoother, analytics are
more privacy-conscious, and the macOS app package is lighter.

## Highlights

- **Dynamic Island is easier to control** with a new menu bar toggle, more
  reliable hover activation, better keyboard handling, and an optional notch
  exclusion setting.
- **Ask Sidebar is now available from selected text or file context**, so you
  can send content into Enconvo from Finder or other right-click workflows.
- **Chat answers and long conversations feel cleaner** with better answer
  formatting and improved message list rendering for longer histories.
- **Analytics are more privacy-conscious** with consistent signed-in user
  identity and filtering for sensitive values such as API keys, passwords, and
  tokens.
- **The macOS app package is lighter** after removing unused Swift packages and
  dead UI helper code.
- **OpenRouter Claude reasoning options are clearer** for newer Opus and Sonnet
  model families.

## Dynamic Island

- Added an Enable or Disable Dynamic Island item to the menu bar menu.
- Improved Dynamic Island event handling to match SmartBar behavior more
  closely, including Escape, Cmd+W, Cmd+N, and Return key flows.
- Replaced raw event monitors with stack-based hotkey monitors for more reliable
  open and close cycles.
- Fixed repeated compact hover entries sometimes failing to open.
- Hover validation now respects the padded hover target, so activation is less
  fragile around the visible pill.
- Added an optional Exclude Notch Area setting. When enabled, hover inside the
  physical notch area will not trigger Dynamic Island, while hover below or
  beside the notch still works.
- Removed streaming text preview from compact and expanded Dynamic Island modes
  when agent auto-read is active.

## Chat And Sidebar

- Added the Ask Sidebar command for sending selected text or file context into
  the app sidebar.
- Registered Ask Sidebar as a Finder service and general right-click action.
- Chat With AI now avoids fenced code blocks unless you ask for code, commands,
  structured data, logs, or exact copyable text.
- Formula-style answers now stay in normal prose or Markdown math instead of
  code-style boxes.
- Improved long chat rendering by stabilizing Streamdown props and using
  content visibility for off-screen cached messages.

## Privacy And App Package

- Signed-in users are identified more consistently across the Mac app, Node.js
  runtime, and web settings app.
- Sensitive values such as API keys, passwords, and tokens are filtered before
  analytics events are sent.
- Event names are clearer for command runs, panels, providers, settings,
  login/logout, and feature flags.
- Removed unused Swift packages including OpenAISwift, ActivityIndicatorView,
  SwiftUIPager, OpenAI by MacPaw, Blackbird, GoogleSignIn, GoogleSignInSwift,
  MenuBarExtraAccess, MSDisplayLink, SpringInterpolation, and Splash.
- Deleted unused WindowAnimation helpers and cleaned dead Swift imports in views
  and services.

## Models And Providers

- OpenRouter Claude Opus and Sonnet model ids now match reasoning effort
  profiles by model family and version, even when naming order or separators
  vary.
- Opus 4.7 and 4.8 now show adaptive Low, Medium, High, X-High, and Max effort
  choices instead of legacy token-budget choices.
- Other Claude fallback models, including Sonnet 4.5 and latest aliases, now use
  the simpler Low, Medium, High, and Max effort list.

---

# Enconvo 2.5.3 Changelog (2026-06-25)

Friends, thanks for waiting. This release took longer than usual because I spent
time polishing the whole app experience: Dynamic Island, Chat Window, SmartBar,
Computer Use, and more model and media tools should all feel smoother in daily
use.

## Highlights

- **Dynamic Island is more capable** with voice command input, smoother session
  reuse, better hover behavior, cleaner model picking, app-context screenshots,
  and more reliable handoff back into chat.
- **Voice input is easier to use** in the places where quick capture matters,
  including Chat Window and Dynamic Island workflows.
- **Chat Window and SmartBar feel more refined** across long conversations,
  history navigation, model selection, mentions, paste behavior, and follow-up
  prompts.
- **Computer Use and Browser Use are more dependable** for clicks, drags,
  scrolling, file picking, app targeting, and browser tab control.
- **Knowledge, context, and search are easier to navigate** with better
  Knowledge Base access, clearer context actions, and improved app, website,
  plugin, and window mentions.
- **More models and media tools are available**, including Claude Opus 4.8,
  GLM-5.2, MiniMax M3, Soniox v5, xAI Imagine, xAI speech-to-text, and GPT
  Image 2 through OpenAI OAuth.

## Dynamic Island And Voice

- Added voice command input for Dynamic Island so you can capture an idea or
  ask for help without switching into the full chat window.
- Improved Dynamic Island session reuse, hover behavior, model picker behavior,
  handoff state, and app-context screenshots.
- Added voice input to Chat Window for a faster way to start or continue a
  conversation.
- Improved how conversations move between native surfaces and web chat so the
  same session stays consistent.

## Chat Window And SmartBar

- Improved Chat Window history syncing, titlebar navigation, row actions,
  prompt mentions, paste routing, footer controls, and model picker spacing.
- Improved SmartBar command mentions, slash model picking, status handling,
  paste behavior, mention chip removal, and follow-up flow.
- Made long conversations and repeated quick actions feel steadier across both
  main chat surfaces.

## Computer Use And Browser Use

- Improved native app control with faster app state loading, stronger app and
  window targeting, better action waiting, and safer background behavior.
- Improved clicking, coordinate clicking, dragging, scrolling, low-level mouse
  control, and native panel actions.
- Added better support for native file picker selection, app menu element
  indexes, clipboard access, and permission prompts.
- Improved browser automation with exact tab targeting, cross-origin iframe
  handling, cleaner page state output, drag-safe clicks, and clipboard proxy
  support.

## Knowledge Base, Context, And Search

- Improved Knowledge Base navigation, access, local API behavior, and settings
  history navigation.
- Added a Knowledge Base context manager and clearer search context actions.
- Added Docling document loader support.
- Improved application and website mentions, local SmartBar mention icons,
  installed plugin mentions, and window context capture.

## Models, Providers, And Media

- Added Claude Opus 4.8 Cloud Plan support.
- Added GLM-5.2 and MiniMax M3 Cloud Plan support.
- Added xAI Imagine image generation and xAI video local media upload support.
- Added xAI OAuth credentials, xAI speech-to-text, and improved Grok model
  visibility.
- Added OpenAI OAuth GPT Image 2 and OpenAI OAuth Transcribe support.
- Added Soniox v5 speech-to-text models.
- Improved Anthropic OAuth account info, account switching, cancel behavior,
  high reasoning defaults, and image fallback behavior.

## Settings, Skills, And Polish

- Improved hooks configuration, manual documentation, sound notifications, and
  agent monitor callbacks.
- Improved Skills Manager multi-source install reliability.
- Simplified Settings sidebar labels for General and Shortcuts.
- Improved Settings layout, Knowledge Base settings navigation, provider rows,
  and reset behavior.
- Improved PopBar row density, keyboard dismissal, copy shortcut conflicts,
  App Sidebar empty states, and window exclusions.
- Fixed TTS provider voice preview selection and IM channel launch lock
  recovery.
- Improved tool-result compaction, multimodal tool results, agent prompt
  cleanup, thinking message behavior, and chat stream rendering.

# Enconvo 2.5.2 Changelog (2026-06-20)

Enconvo 2.5.2 focuses on a smoother everyday chat experience, clearer update
prompts, stronger voice input, more reliable Computer Use, and broader model
and provider support.

## Highlights

- Chat Window and SmartBar are more polished, more responsive, and easier to
  use across long conversations.
- New update indicators can appear directly in Chat Window and SmartBar when a
  new Production or Beta version is available.
- Voice input is now available in more places, including Chat Window and
  Dynamic Island voice commands.
- Computer Use and Browser Use are more reliable for clicking, dragging,
  scrolling, file picking, tab targeting, and native app control.
- Knowledge Base context, settings, and search flows are easier to navigate.
- More providers and models are available, including Claude Opus 4.8,
  GLM-5.2, MiniMax M3, Soniox v5, xAI Imagine, xAI speech-to-text, and GPT
  Image 2 through OpenAI OAuth.

## Chat Window And SmartBar

- Added voice input to the Chat Window prompt, so you can dictate directly
  where you already type.
- Added an **Update** button in both Chat Window and SmartBar when Enconvo
  detects a new version.
- Improved Chat Window history syncing, titlebar navigation, row actions,
  prompt mentions, paste routing, footer controls, and model picker spacing.
- Refined message readability, text selection, realtime assistant history, and
  table rendering in chat messages.
- Improved SmartBar status handling, command mention routing, slash model
  picking, paste behavior, mention chip removal, and follow-up flow.

## Update Experience

- Simplified update channels to **Production** and **Beta**.
- Beta releases now publish to the matching Beta distribution path and appcast,
  so users on the Beta channel can detect the correct update.
- Update checks now run when Chat Window or SmartBar is shown, with a
  10-minute throttle to avoid repeated background checks.
- Automatic update downloads are off by default.

## Voice, Dictation, And Dynamic Island

- Added Dynamic Island voice command input.
- Improved voice command session reuse, so follow-up voice commands feel more
  continuous.
- Added xAI speech-to-text provider support.
- Added OpenAI OAuth Transcribe support.
- Added Soniox v5 speech-to-text models.

## Computer Use And Browser Use

- Improved native app control with faster app state loading, stronger app and
  window targeting, better action waiting, and safer background behavior.
- Improved clicking, coordinate clicking, dragging, scrolling, low-level mouse
  control, and native panel actions.
- Added better support for native file picker selection, app menu element
  indexes, clipboard access, and permission authorization prompts.
- Improved browser automation with exact tab targeting, cross-origin iframe
  handling, cleaner page state output, better readable snapshots, drag-safe
  clicks, and clipboard proxy support.

## Knowledge Base, Context, And Search

- Improved Knowledge Base navigation, access, local API behavior, and settings
  history navigation.
- Added a Knowledge Base context manager and clearer search context actions.
- Added Docling document loader support.
- Improved hierarchical local API schema lookup and API schema explorer runtime
  paths.

## Models, Providers, And Media Generation

- Added Claude Opus 4.8 Cloud Plan support.
- Added GLM-5.2 and MiniMax M3 Cloud Plan support.
- Added xAI Imagine image generation and provider feature APIs.
- Added xAI video local media upload support.
- Added xAI OAuth credentials, Grok 4.3 model filtering, and improved xAI
  model visibility.
- Added OpenAI OAuth GPT Image 2 support and improved OpenAI OAuth credential
  sync and LLM stability.
- Improved Anthropic OAuth account info, account switching, cancel behavior,
  high reasoning defaults, and image base64 fallback behavior.

## Workflows, Hooks, And Developer Tools

- Added a Workflow YAML management editor.
- Added workflow reference checkout support.
- Improved local API descriptions, local API common tool availability, module
  API schema hygiene, and inline API schemas in generated module skills.
- Added unified tagged logging across Swift, web, and Node.js flows.
- Added a logging clear button.

## Capture, Translation, And Polish

- Improved screenshot translation with SSE streaming.
- Added AppShots capture sound controls and exact paired-option hotkeys.
- Improved AppShots context hotkey behavior and live screen attachment sending.
- Improved PopBar row density, keyboard dismissal, and copy shortcut conflict
  handling.
- Fixed TTS provider voice preview selection, IM channel launch lock recovery,
  post-tool thinking loading, and collapsed thinking message behavior.

# Enconvo 2.5.1 Changelog (2026-05-08) 🚀

## OpenClaw

- Enconvo now supports **OpenClaw** as an LLM provider — bring any of your OpenClaw agents into Enconvo and run them seamlessly across **SmartBar**, **PopBar**, **App Sidebar**, and every other Enconvo surface

## Hermes

- Enconvo now supports **Hermes** as an LLM provider — every Hermes agent is instantly available across **SmartBar**, **PopBar**, **App Sidebar**, and the rest of Enconvo, so you can use them anywhere you work

## File Preview

- Added **File preview** support — you can now preview files (images, video, audio, Markdown, PDF, and more) right inside SmartBar, beside the chat
- Action buttons on the right side stay visible while previewing
- Fixed an issue with PDF page numbers

## MLX

- **Local MLX models** (beta) — run models on‑device for writing and translation use cases, keeping everything private and fully offline

## AI Model Provider

- New provider: **Xiaomi MiMo** (BYOK & Cloud Plan)

## TTS

- New provider: **Xiaomi MiMo TTS** (BYOK & Cloud Plan)
- New local TTS providers, all powered by **MLX** and running fully on‑device:
  - **Kokoro 82M** — lightweight and multilingual
  - **Qwen TTS** — multilingual with natural prosody

## Dictation

- New on‑device ASR providers, powered by **MLX**:
  - **Parakeet ASR**
  - **Whisper ASR**
- New realtime / sync ASR providers (BYOK & Cloud Plan):
  - **Azure** (realtime)
  - **ElevenLabs Scribe v2** (realtime / sync)
  - **AssemblyAI** (realtime / sync)
  - **Soniox** (realtime / sync)
  - **Volcengine** (realtime / sync)
- Your personal **Memory** is now used as the dictation dictionary, improving recognition of names, jargon, and custom terms you frequently use
- Added **Dictation History**, with support for using dictation records as Knowledge Base context
- Smoother dictation experience in SmartBar with full **realtime / sync** mode support

## SmartBar

- SmartBar now shows an **Update** button at the bottom when a new version is available
- **Explain follow‑up** — keep the conversation going naturally after an explanation
- **"Use as context"** is now the first action when you use `@`
- Improved **paste image** flow and refined screenshot logic for a smoother experience
- You can now **delete queued messages** while an agent is still running
- Context is now **automatically compacted** to keep long conversations responsive
- Fixed an issue with `@` mention switching

## Chat Window

- **Message history search** lets you quickly find past conversations in the chat window
- Fixed table rendering issues in chat messages
- Fixed: model selection not being applied correctly
- Fixed: model selector dismissing unexpectedly
- Fixed a history switching issue
- Added a **Show in Finder** icon for delivered files

## Settings

- Optimized the shortcut settings page for a smoother configuration experience

## Knowledge Base

- **Local embeddings** — embeddings now run on‑device, so your knowledge base data never leaves your machine
- Knowledge Base entries can now be referenced directly with **`@` context mentions**
- Knowledge Base search now accepts `query: string[]`, enabling multi‑query searches in a single call

## IM Channels

- Fixed: IM channels no longer disconnect when an error occurs
- Added a **verbose mode** — streams the agent's step‑by‑step execution details directly into your IM channel, so you can follow exactly what the agent is doing in real time

## Web Search

- New web search provider: **Brave Search**

## Analytics

- Integrated **PostHog** as the analytics platform — you can opt out at any time from **Settings → About**

## New Logo

- Enconvo has a fresh new look — meet the refreshed app logo

## Bug Fixes

- Fixed context handling when returning from another view
- Improved explain context handling
- Fixed links being blocked unexpectedly
- Fixed paste issues in WeChat
- Fixed an issue with the mail notification sound
- Fixed lost context messages
- Fixed an incorrect session directory being used
- Fixed a livescreen image issue



---

# Enconvo 2.5.0 Changelog (2026-04-28) 🚀

## SmartBar

- Resizable SmartBar window — position and size are persisted independently for Command List mode and Chat mode
- New SmartBar suggestion panel
- Dynamic Context: agents now read a live overview of your foreground context and pull in specific items only when needed (for example, ask about the current page directly in your open browser without manually attaching context or opening the App SideBar)
- Smoother task switching with optimized switch logic
- Ask AI: select text inside a SmartBar response and use it as context for follow-up questions
- SmartBar list: menu opens on both click and right-click; added a search box for quickly filtering options
- Pending messages — append new messages while the agent is still running; they are queued and consumed automatically

## Dictation

- Refreshed Dictation UI/UX
- Added Qwen ASR support (local, MLX-powered)
- New setting: toggle the start/stop sound effect when dictation is activated
- New setting: toggle whether system audio is muted while dictation is active
- Optimized post-dictation actions — defaults now include Polish and Translate
- New shortcut mode: "Hold or Toggle", default shortcut is `Right Command` (hold for push-to-talk, tap to toggle)
- AI-powered voice input dictionary for improved recognition of names, jargon, and custom terms

## Agent

- New Voice Trigger feature — use `Right Option` to trigger voice input directly to the agent
- Improved session management
- Optimized context-compression logic

## Cron Scheduling

- Cron-scheduled execution for agents — let agents run tasks on a schedule
- Cron logs UI with fixed-height dialog and syntax-highlighted JSON detail view
- Manage cron schedules from both the UI and the API

## IM Channels

- Unified IM Channels module supporting Discord and Telegram
- In-channel bot commands: `/new`, `/stop`, `/voice`, `/status`, `/audio`
- Access control: pairing-based system with approve/deny notifications

## Skills Support

- Skills Store with curated skills (PPT, DOCX, Excel, Obsidian, photo dedup, and more)
- Use skills with the `/` slash command
- Custom skills directories — pick your own folders or symlinked directories

## Knowledge Base

- Reindex ability — rebuild the knowledge base on demand from the UI
- Added xlsx support for knowledge base ingestion
- Audio/video transcription supported in the file loader
- Indexing errors are surfaced in the UI

## Memory

- New Memory management UI
- Optimized memory use logic with smarter retrieval and compression strategies

## Recording

- Standalone, draggable recording bar with per-screen position persistence

## API System

- Expose Enconvo abilities through HTTP API — call Enconvo capabilities from external apps
- Base URL: `http://localhost:54535/`
- Example:

```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:54535/tts/tts" \
     -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
     -d '{ "input_text": "hello, world" }'
```

## Enconvo CLI

- Expose Enconvo abilities through a command-line interface
- `enconvo -h` shows the help message

## Computer Use

- New `computer-use` extension for native macOS control

## TTS Improvements

- New providers: xAI TTS, Edge TTS (free), Gemini 3.1 Flash TTS
- New Read Aloud UI/UX: horizontal bar with a live waveform and loading state

## AI Model Provider

- New providers: MiniMax, [Z.AI](http://z.ai) (Anthropic-compatible)
- Claude Opus 4.7 (Cloud Plan, Subscription, BYOK)
- Kimi 2.6 / Moonshot updates (Cloud Plan, BYOK)
- DeepSeek V4 Flash and V4 Pro (BYOK and Enconvo Cloud routing)

## Image Create

- gpt-image-2 model support (Cloud Plan & BYOK)

## Web Fetch

- When fetching x.com, requests are redirected to the Enconvo fetch provider (jina.ai, free tier)

## Extensions Improvements

- `apple-apps-and-services` consolidates all macOS app integrations: Mail, Notes, Calendar, Reminders, Contacts, Messages, and Maps. Replaces the legacy `apple_mail` and `apple_reminders` extensions
- `media-utils` (renamed from `video_utils`) absorbs `audio_utils` and adds online video download (YouTube, TikTok, Instagram, etc.) plus audio compress and transcript APIs
- `bash` util uses RTK to reduce LLM token consumption by 60–90% on common dev commands
- `read_file` tool now reads images, audio, and video
- `image-utils` replaces bundled binaries with auto-download from GitHub Releases
- New standalone `config` module (split out of `enconvo`) with `get` / `set` / `preferences` endpoints, auto-resolved `storeType`, and password auto-encryption
- New `ai-command` extension with migration path from the legacy `custom_bot` (preserves message history)

## UI/UX Improvements

- Refreshed tool-call display
- Tools like `write_file` now stream their text content live
- Workspace sidebar — quick access to the working directory and deliverables
- Customizable command icons
- Smoother agent flow
- Command settings now jump to a unified settings page

## PopBar

- Ask AI integration in PopBar

## Other Improvements

- `read_file` can read image content
- Prompts can reference settings parameters
- Screenshot explanation: clears prior conversation history and no longer auto-copies by default
- Anthropic SDK linked locally with a fork; added `jsonrepair` for robust streaming parses
- App shutdown: SIGTERM is sent to Python subprocess groups on app termination

## Bug Fixes

- Fixed up/down/enter behavior in settings input fields
- Fixed Ollama cloud model issues
- Fixed shortcut handling for menus
- Fixed `pasteIn` so keyboard events target the last frontmost app
- Fixed reasoning component flicker when content is empty or whitespace

## 2.2.17 (2025-11-04)

### Context Optimization
- Support obtaining the content of application windows as context
- Improved interaction settings
- Support selecting available contexts with the `#` character when inputting text
- You can use `@` to specify which tool to use when you input text

### Global Sidebar Panel
- A new always-on-top sidebar panel opens on the right side
- The SideBar supports invoking features with `@`, `#`, and `>`
- Default shortcut: `Shift + Command + U`

### Application Sidebar Panel
- Launches an always-on-top sidebar panel on the right within the current application
- Automatically switches to the Bot dedicated to the current Application (such as Finder)
- SideBar supports invoking features with `@`, `#`, and `>`
- Default shortcut: `Shift + Command + T`

### Multitasking
- Multiple tasks can now run simultaneously in the background
- Create a new session with `Command + N`
- Switch between different tasks by inputting `>`

### Voice Command
- Run a voice command to trigger voice input
- You can specify Window Mode to choose whether the command runs in the Smart Bar or Side Bar
- Set a Default Command to decide which Bot should handle your input after voice transcription
- Default shortcut: `Shift + Command + A`

### My Profile Settings
- You can set your personal description as context to provide to the AI Model
- You can set a global reply language
- Location: Settings > Account > My Profile

### Variables (Dynamic Parameters)
- When editing prompts, you can specify dynamic Variables
- Supported Variables are viewable under Settings > Extensions > Variables
- While editing a prompt, activate the Variables list by typing `{{ ` (double curly brace + space)

### Custom Bot Creation Optimization
- Improved workflow for creating Custom Bots
- Support for `Duplicate Command`: Create a new command based on an existing one
- Set reply language independently
- Option to use `My Profile` or not

### Application Support
- Search all installed applications from the SmartBar and press Enter to open
- Every application is an Agent. Use `@` to begin a conversation with an application

### Screenshot Explain
- Support for taking a screenshot first, then running Explain on it

## 2.2.1 (2025-06-07)

- Brand new **visual workflow editor**
